Archive format: each section is the complete source file preceded by its exact absolute path and SHA-256. Original source code is preserved verbatim as text.

ORIGINAL ARCHITECTURE SUMMARY
- Chat has no Conversation table or create-conversation endpoint. A task is the conversation key; ChatMessage rows reference taskId and senderId.
- Freelancer flow: GET /api/tasks -> POST /api/tasks/{taskId}/bid (requires open task, no duplicate, active user and enough connects; connects are charged and ledgered) -> admin POST /api/admin/bids/{bidId}/award (creates ACTIVE TaskAssignment and sets task ASSIGNED) -> GET/POST /api/tasks/{taskId}/chat.
- Freelancer chat authorization requires a TaskAssignment for that user and task in ACTIVE, SUBMITTED, APPROVED, or REJECTED. Admin role bypasses assignment check. POST rejects closed chat; GET can load history if authorized.
- Chat rows persist in ChatMessage; history is returned oldest-first. The freelancer inbox is constructed from eligible assignments, while the admin inbox lists tasks with at least one message.
- Unread state is notification-level rather than per-message. The app lists chat threads using CHAT_MESSAGE notifications with /messages?task={id}; task chat read uses PATCH /api/notifications with taskId. Backend notification read supports user-scoped updates.
- Chat sends notify the other side through notifyUser; mobile delivery uses active FCM device registrations if configured/preferences permit it. Frontend API calls use the cookie-bearing shared ApiClient; Next.js API routes require a NextAuth session.
- Existing Flutter chat UI/service/controller and backend components/routes are included below with proposal, assignment, auth, notifications, models, and tests needed to reconstruct the behavior.

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';

import '../../notifications/models/notification_model.dart';
import '../models/chat_stamps.dart';

/// One row of the Messages list.
///
/// A conversation is addressed by its project, because that is the only thing
/// the backend's chat routes take — there is no conversation id.
typedef ConversationRow = ({
  String taskId,
  String name,
  String preview,
  String stamp,
  bool unread,
});

/// Turns the notifications the app already has into the conversations it can
/// honestly open.
///
/// The backend serves no conversation list, so a project appears here only when
/// it has sent a `CHAT_MESSAGE` notification naming it. That is the truth of
/// what is reachable, not a substitute inbox: a project whose messages were
/// read on another device, or whose notification was dismissed, cannot appear
/// here without a new endpoint.
class MessagesController extends ChangeNotifier {
  static const List<String> filters = ['All', 'Unread'];

  String _query = '';
  int _filter = 0;

  int get selectedFilter => _filter;

  void setQuery(String value) {
    _query = value;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void setFilter(int index) {
    _filter = index;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  /// Newest first, one row per project. [notifications] comes straight from
  /// [NotificationController], which already read the server's list.
  List<ConversationRow> conversations(List<NotificationModel> notifications) {
    final query = _query.trim().toLowerCase();
    final seen = <String>{};
    final rows = <ConversationRow>[];
    for (final notification in notifications) {
      final taskId = notification.chatTaskId;
      if (taskId == null || !seen.add(taskId)) continue;

      final name = _nameOf(notification);
      final preview = notification.body.trim();
      final matchesQuery =
          query.isEmpty ||
          name.toLowerCase().contains(query) ||
          preview.toLowerCase().contains(query);
      final matchesFilter = switch (_filter) {
        1 => !notification.isRead,
        _ => true,
      };
      if (!matchesQuery || !matchesFilter) continue;

      rows.add((
        taskId: taskId,
        name: name,
        preview: preview.isEmpty ? 'No message text' : preview,
        stamp: chatStamp(notification.createdAt),
        unread: !notification.isRead,
      ));
    }
    return rows;
  }

  /// The client's name, which is the only participant a chat notification
  /// carries — the backend writes the row as `Message from <clientName>`. A
  /// reply notification names the project instead, so that is what shows.
  static String _nameOf(NotificationModel notification) {
    const prefix = 'Message from ';
    final title = notification.title.trim();
    if (title.startsWith(prefix)) {
      final client = title.substring(prefix.length).trim();
      if (client.isNotEmpty) return client;
    }
    return title.isEmpty ? 'WorkingTrust' : title;
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';

import '../../../core/network/network_exceptions.dart';
import '../../notifications/controller/notification_controller.dart';
import '../../notifications/services/notification_service.dart';
import '../../profile/controller/profile_controller.dart';
import '../models/project_chat.dart';
import '../services/project_chat_service.dart';

enum ChatStatus { idle, loading, ready, empty, error }

/// One project's conversation, read from the backend and never invented here.
///
/// A thread exists only for a [taskId] someone else handed over — a
/// `CHAT_MESSAGE` notification's href is the app's one honest source — because
/// the backend offers no conversation list.
class ProjectChatController extends ChangeNotifier {
  ProjectChatController(this.taskId);

  final String taskId;

  final ProjectChatService _service = ProjectChatService.instance;
  final List<ChatMessage> _messages = [];

  Chat? _chat;
  ChatStatus _status = ChatStatus.idle;
  String _errorMessage = '';
  bool _loading = false;
  bool _sending = false;

  ChatStatus get status => _status;
  String get errorMessage => _errorMessage;
  bool get isLoading => _status == ChatStatus.loading;
  bool get isSending => _sending;
  int get bodyLimit => ProjectChatService.bodyLimit;
  List<ChatMessage> get messages => List.unmodifiable(_messages);

  /// The server's own `closed` flag. Until a thread has been read, nothing is
  /// known about it, so nothing is claimed.
  bool get isClosed => _chat?.closed ?? false;
  bool get isFlagged => _chat?.flagged ?? false;

  /// Whether the server has answered for this thread at all. Until it has, the
  /// pane has no title, no participants and nothing to write into.
  bool get isLoaded => _chat != null;
  bool get canReply => isLoaded && !isClosed && !_sending;

  String get projectTitle => _chat?.projectTitle ?? 'Project chat';

  /// Whoever the backend labels the messages that are not this account's. A
  /// thread this account started has no such label yet, so the participant the
  /// route names for the other side of this role is used instead.
  String get otherName {
    final thread = _chat;
    for (final message in _messages.reversed) {
      if (!isMine(message)) return message.displayName;
    }
    if (thread == null) return 'WorkingTrust';
    return _isAdmin
        ? (thread.freelancerName ?? thread.clientName)
        : thread.clientName;
  }

  String? get otherAvatarUrl {
    final thread = _chat;
    if (thread == null) return null;
    return _isAdmin ? thread.freelancerAvatarUrl : thread.clientAvatarUrl;
  }

  /// Staff accounts see the freelancer as the other party; everyone else is
  /// talking to the client, who has no login of their own.
  bool get _isAdmin => ProfileController.instance.role == 'ADMIN';

  /// The server's own label for this account's rows. It writes `You`, or
  /// `You (as <client>)` when staff posts on a client's behalf, so the words
  /// carry ownership even before this device has read its session.
  static bool _labelSaysMine(String displayName) =>
      displayName == 'You' || displayName.startsWith('You (');

  bool isMine(ChatMessage message) {
    final id = ProfileController.instance.userId;
    if (id.isNotEmpty) return message.senderId == id;
    return _labelSaysMine(message.displayName);
  }

  /// Reading the thread first, then clearing the unread rows that brought the
  /// user here. A notification that refuses to clear is not worth failing the
  /// chat over.
  Future<void> open() async {
    // A route can only be reached with an id from a notification, but an empty
    // one must not become a request to `/api/tasks//chat`.
    if (taskId.isEmpty) {
      _status = ChatStatus.error;
      _errorMessage = 'This message did not name a project.';
      notifyListeners();
      return;
    }
    await load();
    await clearUnread();
  }

  Future<void> load({bool silent = false}) async {
    if (_loading) return;
    _loading = true;
    if (!silent && _chat == null) {
      _status = ChatStatus.loading;
      notifyListeners();
    }
    try {
      final thread = await _service.load(taskId);
      _chat = thread;
      _messages
        ..clear()
        ..addAll(thread.messages);
      _status = thread.messages.isEmpty ? ChatStatus.empty : ChatStatus.ready;
      _errorMessage = '';
    } on NetworkException catch (e) {
      _fail(e);
    } catch (e) {
      _fail(NetworkException('Could not load this conversation.'));
    } finally {
      _loading = false;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  /// Answers true only once the server has stored the message. The row the
  /// `POST` returns is what lands in the list, so nothing here is ever shown
  /// ahead of the backend agreeing.
  Future<bool> send(String text) async {
    final body = text.trim();
    if (body.isEmpty || _sending) return false;
    if (isClosed) {
      _errorMessage = 'This conversation is closed.';
      notifyListeners();
      return false;
    }
    if (body.length > bodyLimit) {
      _errorMessage = 'A message can be up to $bodyLimit characters.';
      notifyListeners();
      return false;
    }

    _sending = true;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      final entry = await _service.send(taskId, body: body);
      _messages.add(entry);
      _status = ChatStatus.ready;
      _errorMessage = '';
      return true;
    } on NetworkException catch (e) {
      _fail(e);
      return false;
    } catch (e) {
      _fail(NetworkException('Your message was not sent.'));
      return false;
    } finally {
      _sending = false;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  Future<void> clearUnread() async {
    try {
      await NotificationService.instance.markTaskChatRead(taskId);
      await NotificationController.instance.refresh(silent: true);
    } catch (_) {
      // The unread count is the shell's business; a lost PATCH only means the
      // bell still shows a number.
    }
  }

  void _fail(NetworkException e) {
    _errorMessage = e.statusCode == 401
        ? NotificationController.sessionExpired
        : e.message;
    // Rows already on screen stay there; only a thread that never loaded can
    // take over the whole pane.
    if (_chat == null) _status = ChatStatus.error;
  }
}

/// How a message time is written down, in the three places it appears.
library;

/// The list row's stamp: a time for today, a date for anything older.
String chatStamp(DateTime at, {DateTime? now}) {
  final today = now ?? DateTime.now();
  if (at.year == today.year && at.month == today.month && at.day == today.day) {
    return chatTimeLabel(at);
  }
  return chatDayLabel(at, now: today);
}

/// `3:45 pm`, the line under a bubble.
String chatTimeLabel(DateTime at) {
  final hour = at.hour % 12 == 0 ? 12 : at.hour % 12;
  final minute = at.minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0');
  return '$hour:$minute ${at.hour < 12 ? 'am' : 'pm'}';
}

/// The divider above a day's messages: `Today`, `Yesterday`, or the date.
String chatDayLabel(DateTime at, {DateTime? now}) {
  final today = now ?? DateTime.now();
  final day = DateTime(at.year, at.month, at.day);
  final startOfToday = DateTime(today.year, today.month, today.day);
  final difference = startOfToday.difference(day).inDays;
  if (difference == 0) return 'Today';
  if (difference == 1) return 'Yesterday';
  const months = [
    'Jan',
    'Feb',
    'Mar',
    'Apr',
    'May',
    'Jun',
    'Jul',
    'Aug',
    'Sep',
    'Oct',
    'Nov',
    'Dec',
  ];
  final date = '${months[day.month - 1]} ${day.day}';
  return day.year == startOfToday.year ? date : '$date, ${day.year}';
}

/// The shape of one project chat, as `GET /api/tasks/<id>/chat` answers it.
///
/// There is no conversation list, no single-message fetch and no edit or delete
/// route: a thread is replaced whole by whatever the server says, and a send
/// answers with exactly the one row it stored.
library;

/// One stored message, already labelled by the backend.
///
/// [displayName] is the server's own answer for who wrote it *from this
/// account's point of view* — `You`, the client's name, or a staff member's
/// name — because a task's client is a name kept on the task, not a login.
/// [fromClient] marks the rows written by the client side, which can only ever
/// be an admin account.
class ChatMessage {
  const ChatMessage({
    required this.id,
    required this.body,
    this.attachmentUrl,
    required this.createdAt,
    required this.senderId,
    required this.displayName,
    required this.fromClient,
  });

  final String id;
  final String body;
  final String? attachmentUrl;
  final DateTime createdAt;
  final String senderId;
  final String displayName;
  final bool fromClient;

  /// Every field is read defensively: the route is shared with the web, and a
  /// row that stops carrying one key must not stop the thread from opening.
  factory ChatMessage.fromJson(Map<dynamic, dynamic> json) => ChatMessage(
    id: _text(json['id']) ?? '',
    body: _text(json['body']) ?? '',
    attachmentUrl: _text(json['attachmentUrl']),
    // A row with no readable time still belongs in the list; it groups with the
    // moment it was read rather than being dropped.
    createdAt:
        DateTime.tryParse(json['createdAt']?.toString() ?? '') ??
        DateTime.now(),
    senderId: _text(json['senderId']) ?? '',
    displayName: _text(json['displayName']) ?? 'WorkingTrust',
    fromClient: json['isClientSide'] == true,
  );
}

/// A thread: the two participants, the flags that decide whether it can still
/// receive messages, and its messages oldest first.
class Chat {
  const Chat({
    required this.projectTitle,
    required this.clientName,
    this.clientAvatarUrl,
    this.freelancerName,
    this.freelancerAvatarUrl,
    this.flagged = false,
    this.closed = false,
    required this.messages,
  });

  final String projectTitle;
  final String clientName;
  final String? clientAvatarUrl;
  final String? freelancerName;
  final String? freelancerAvatarUrl;
  final bool flagged;
  final bool closed;

  /// The order the backend sent, kept as it arrived.
  final List<ChatMessage> messages;

  factory Chat.fromJson(Map<dynamic, dynamic> json) {
    final rows = json['messages'];
    return Chat(
      projectTitle: _text(json['projectTitle']) ?? 'Project chat',
      clientName: _text(json['clientName']) ?? 'Client',
      clientAvatarUrl: _text(json['clientAvatarUrl']),
      freelancerName: _text(json['freelancerName']),
      freelancerAvatarUrl: _text(json['freelancerAvatarUrl']),
      flagged: json['flagged'] == true,
      closed: json['closed'] == true,
      messages: [
        if (rows is List)
          for (final row in rows)
            if (row is Map) ChatMessage.fromJson(row),
      ],
    );
  }
}

String? _text(Object? value) {
  if (value == null) return null;
  final text = value.toString().trim();
  return text.isEmpty ? null : text;
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../../app/theme/app_colors.dart';
import '../../../app/theme/app_dimensions.dart';
import '../../../app/theme/app_text_styles.dart';
import '../../../core/widgets/app_avatar.dart';
import '../../../core/widgets/app_tag.dart';
import '../../notifications/controller/notification_controller.dart';
import '../controller/messages_controller.dart';
import '../controller/project_chat_controller.dart';
import '../widgets/project_chat_view.dart';

/// The signed-in freelancer's project conversations.
///
/// The rows come from the notifications the shell already read, because the
/// backend has no conversation-list endpoint — see [MessagesController]. The
/// thread itself is always the server's own, fetched the moment a row is
/// opened.
class MessagesScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const MessagesScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<MessagesScreen> createState() => _MessagesScreenState();
}

class _MessagesScreenState extends State<MessagesScreen> {
  final MessagesController _controller = MessagesController();
  final TextEditingController _search = TextEditingController();

  /// The thread on screen, with the row it belongs to. One controller per
  /// opened project, disposed as soon as another is chosen.
  ProjectChatController? _chat;
  ConversationRow? _opened;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _search.dispose();
    _controller.dispose();
    _chat?.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _open(ConversationRow conversation) {
    _chat?.dispose();
    final chat = ProjectChatController(conversation.taskId);
    setState(() {
      _opened = conversation;
      _chat = chat;
    });
    chat.open();
  }

  void _close() {
    _chat?.dispose();
    setState(() {
      _opened = null;
      _chat = null;
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ListenableBuilder(
      listenable: Listenable.merge([
        _controller,
        NotificationController.instance,
      ]),
      builder: (context, _) {
        final conversations = _controller.conversations(
          NotificationController.instance.notifications,
        );
        final chat = _chat;
        return SafeArea(
          child: LayoutBuilder(
            builder: (context, constraints) {
              // A phone has no room for both panes, so the thread replaces the
              // list until it is closed again; a back control only makes sense
              // in that single-pane mode.
              final narrow = constraints.maxWidth < 640;
              final list = _ConversationList(
                controller: _controller,
                search: _search,
                conversations: conversations,
                selected: _opened,
                onOpen: _open,
                onRefresh: () =>
                    NotificationController.instance.refresh(silent: true),
              );
              final padded = EdgeInsets.all(AppDimensions.md);
              if (narrow) {
                if (chat != null) {
                  return Padding(
                    padding: padded,
                    child: _Panel(
                      child: ProjectChatView(
                        key: ValueKey('chat-${chat.taskId}'),
                        controller: chat,
                        showBack: true,
                        onBack: _close,
                      ),
                    ),
                  );
                }
                return Padding(padding: padded, child: list);
              }
              return Padding(
                padding: padded,
                child: Row(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                  children: [
                    SizedBox(width: 280, child: list),
                    const SizedBox(width: AppDimensions.md),
                    Expanded(
                      child: chat == null
                          ? const _PickAProject()
                          : _Panel(
                              child: ProjectChatView(
                                key: ValueKey('chat-${chat.taskId}'),
                                controller: chat,
                              ),
                            ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              );
            },
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

class _ConversationList extends StatelessWidget {
  final MessagesController controller;
  final TextEditingController search;
  final List<ConversationRow> conversations;
  final ConversationRow? selected;
  final ValueChanged<ConversationRow> onOpen;
  final Future<void> Function() onRefresh;

  const _ConversationList({
    required this.controller,
    required this.search,
    required this.conversations,
    required this.selected,
    required this.onOpen,
    required this.onRefresh,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Text('Messages', style: AppTextStyles.heading2),
        const SizedBox(height: AppDimensions.md),
        TextField(
          controller: search,
          onChanged: controller.setQuery,
          style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 14),
          decoration: InputDecoration(
            hintText: 'Search here...',
            hintStyle: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textHint),
            prefixIcon: const Icon(
              Icons.search_rounded,
              size: 20,
              color: AppColors.textHint,
            ),
            filled: true,
            fillColor: AppColors.surfaceVariant,
            contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
              horizontal: AppDimensions.md,
              vertical: AppDimensions.sm,
            ),
            border: OutlineInputBorder(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(30),
              borderSide: BorderSide.none,
            ),
            enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(30),
              borderSide: BorderSide.none,
            ),
            focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(30),
              borderSide: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.primary),
            ),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: AppDimensions.md),
        Wrap(
          spacing: AppDimensions.sm,
          runSpacing: AppDimensions.sm,
          children: [
            for (
              int index = 0;
              index < MessagesController.filters.length;
              index++
            )
              AppTag(
                MessagesController.filters[index],
                selected: controller.selectedFilter == index,
                onTap: () => controller.setFilter(index),
              ),
          ],
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: AppDimensions.md),
        Expanded(
          child: conversations.isEmpty
              ? const _NoConversations()
              : RefreshIndicator(
                  onRefresh: onRefresh,
                  child: ListView.builder(
                    physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
                    itemCount: conversations.length,
                    itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                      final conversation = conversations[index];
                      return _ConversationTile(
                        conversation: conversation,
                        selected: conversation == selected,
                        onTap: () => onOpen(conversation),
                      );
                    },
                  ),
                ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

/// The honest state for a list the backend cannot serve.
class _NoConversations extends StatelessWidget {
  const _NoConversations();

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Center(
      child: SingleChildScrollView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimensions.md),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: const [
            Icon(
              Icons.forum_outlined,
              size: AppDimensions.iconLg,
              color: AppColors.textHint,
            ),
            SizedBox(height: AppDimensions.sm),
            Text(
              'No project conversations yet',
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              style: AppTextStyles.labelLarge,
            ),
            SizedBox(height: AppDimensions.xs),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _ConversationTile extends StatelessWidget {
  final ConversationRow conversation;
  final bool selected;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  const _ConversationTile({
    required this.conversation,
    required this.selected,
    required this.onTap,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: AppDimensions.sm),
      child: Material(
        color: selected
            ? AppColors.accent.withValues(alpha: 0.35)
            : AppColors.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimensions.radius),
        child: InkWell(
          onTap: onTap,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimensions.radius),
          child: Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimensions.sm + 2),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimensions.radius),
              border: Border.all(
                color: selected ? AppColors.primary : AppColors.border,
              ),
            ),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                AppAvatar(name: conversation.name, radius: 20),
                const SizedBox(width: AppDimensions.sm + 2),
                Expanded(
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        conversation.name,
                        maxLines: 1,
                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                        style: AppTextStyles.labelLarge,
                      ),
                      Text(
                        conversation.preview,
                        maxLines: 1,
                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                        style: AppTextStyles.caption,
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: AppDimensions.sm),
                Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
                  children: [
                    Text(conversation.stamp, style: AppTextStyles.caption),
                    if (conversation.unread)
                      Container(
                        margin: const EdgeInsets.only(top: AppDimensions.xs),
                        width: 8,
                        height: 8,
                        decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                          color: AppColors.primary,
                          shape: BoxShape.circle,

SOURCE FILE: D:\WorkingTrust\lib\features\messages\services\project_chat_service.dart
SHA256: E303A3791A9AFB9ED57A24B6677CEDEF632FE517AEB6F65AD1F44BA958A02FE9
====================================================================================================
import '../../../core/network/api_client.dart';
import '../../../core/network/api_endpoints.dart';
import '../../../core/network/network_exceptions.dart';
import '../models/project_chat.dart';

/// The two chat routes the backend actually serves, with no invented extras.
class ProjectChatService {
  ProjectChatService._();

  static final ProjectChatService instance = ProjectChatService._();

  final ApiClient _apiClient = ApiClient();

  /// `chatSchema`'s own ceilings, applied before the request so a message that
  /// cannot be stored never leaves the device.
  static const int bodyLimit = 2000;
  static const int attachmentLimit = 500;

  /// The whole thread, oldest message first.
  Future<Chat> load(String taskId) async {
    final response = await _apiClient.get(ApiEndpoints.taskChat(taskId));
    if (response is! Map) {
      throw NetworkException('The server did not return this conversation.');
    }
    return Chat.fromJson(response);
  }

  /// Posts one message and answers with the row the server stored, which is the
  /// only copy the app ever shows as sent.
  Future<ChatMessage> send(
    String taskId, {
    required String body,
    String? attachmentUrl,
  }) async {
    final text = body.trim();
    final file = attachmentUrl?.trim() ?? '';
    if (text.isEmpty && file.isEmpty) {
      throw NetworkException('Write a message or attach a file.');
    }
    if (text.length > bodyLimit) {
      throw NetworkException('A message can be up to $bodyLimit characters.');
    }
    if (file.length > attachmentLimit) {
      throw NetworkException('That attachment link is too long.');
    }

    final response = await _apiClient.post(
      ApiEndpoints.taskChat(taskId),
      body: <String, dynamic>{
        'body': text,
        // The route reads a string or nothing; an empty one is not an
        // attachment.
        'attachmentUrl': file.isEmpty ? null : file,
      },
    );
    final row = response is Map ? response['message'] : null;
    if (row is! Map) {
      throw NetworkException('The server did not confirm your message.');
    }
    return ChatMessage.fromJson(row);
  }
}

/// One project conversation: the server's rows, a composer, and nothing local.
///
/// The backend answers oldest-first, and that order is what renders — the list
/// is built upside down so the newest row sits at the bottom edge and the view
/// never has to scroll to it after a send.
class ProjectChatView extends StatefulWidget {
  final ProjectChatController controller;

  /// The narrow Messages pane swaps this view in over the list, so it carries
  /// its own back button instead of an app bar.
  final bool showBack;
  final VoidCallback? onBack;

  const ProjectChatView({
    super.key,
    required this.controller,
    this.showBack = false,
    this.onBack,
  });

  @override
  State<ProjectChatView> createState() => _ProjectChatViewState();

class _ProjectChatViewState extends State<ProjectChatView> {
  final TextEditingController _draft = TextEditingController();

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _draft.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _send() async {
    final text = _draft.text;
    // Only the server's 201 empties the field. A refusal leaves the words where
    // the user can still fix them.
    if (await widget.controller.send(text)) _draft.clear();
  }

  Future<void> _openAttachment(String url) async {
    final failure = await LinkOpener.open(url);
    if (failure == null || !mounted) return;
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      SnackBar(content: Text(failure), behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ListenableBuilder(
      listenable: widget.controller,
      builder: (context, _) {
        final controller = widget.controller;
        return Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            _Header(
              controller: controller,
              showBack: widget.showBack,
              onBack: widget.onBack,
            ),
            const Divider(height: 1, color: AppColors.divider),
            if (controller.isFlagged)
              const _Notice(
                icon: Icons.flag_outlined,
                text: 'This conversation is flagged for review.',
              ),
            if (controller.isClosed)
              const _Notice(
                icon: Icons.lock_outline,
                text: 'This conversation is closed.',
              ),
            Expanded(
              child: _Body(controller: controller, onOpen: _openAttachment),
            ),
            _Composer(controller: controller, draft: _draft, onSend: _send),
          ],
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

class _Body extends StatelessWidget {
  final ProjectChatController controller;
  final Future<void> Function(String url) onOpen;

  const _Body({required this.controller, required this.onOpen});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    switch (controller.status) {
      case ChatStatus.loading:
        return const AppLoader();
      case ChatStatus.error:
        return AppErrorWidget(
          message: controller.errorMessage.isEmpty
              ? 'Could not load this conversation.'
              : controller.errorMessage,
          onRetry: () => controller.load(),
        );
      case ChatStatus.idle:
      case ChatStatus.ready:
      case ChatStatus.empty:
        if (controller.messages.isEmpty) {
          // A thread the server opened that nobody has written in is a real
          // state, so it says so plainly rather than showing an empty column.
          return const Center(
            child: Padding(
              padding: EdgeInsets.all(AppDimensions.lg),
              child: Text(
                'No messages yet on this project.',
                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                style: AppTextStyles.bodySmall,
              ),
            ),
          );
        }
        return RefreshIndicator(
          onRefresh: () => controller.load(silent: true),
          child: ListView(
            reverse: true,
            physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimensions.md),
            children: _rows(controller, onOpen),
          ),
        );
    }
  }

  /// Newest first in the list's own order, which rendered upside down puts the
  /// newest bubble at the bottom and each day divider above that day's rows.
  static List<Widget> _rows(
    ProjectChatController controller,
    Future<void> Function(String url) onOpen,
  ) {
    final children = <Widget>[];
    String? day;
    for (final message in controller.messages.reversed) {
      final messageDay = chatDayLabel(message.createdAt);
      if (day != null && messageDay != day) {
        children.add(_DayDivider(day: day));
      }
      children.add(
        _Bubble(
          message: message,
          mine: controller.isMine(message),
          onOpen: onOpen,
        ),
      );
      day = messageDay;
    }
    if (day != null) children.add(_DayDivider(day: day));
    return children;
  }
}

class _DayDivider extends StatelessWidget {
  final String day;

  const _DayDivider({required this.day});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: AppDimensions.md),
      child: Center(child: AppTag(day, highlight: true)),
    );
  }
}

class _Composer extends StatelessWidget {
  final ProjectChatController controller;
  final TextEditingController draft;
  final Future<void> Function() onSend;

  const _Composer({
    required this.controller,
    required this.draft,
    required this.onSend,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    // Until the server has answered for this thread there is nothing to write
    // into, and a thread that failed to load gets its retry from the body.
    if (!controller.isLoaded) return const SizedBox.shrink();

    final error = controller.errorMessage;
    return Column(
      children: [
        if (error.isNotEmpty && !controller.isSending)
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
              AppDimensions.md,
              AppDimensions.xs,
              AppDimensions.md,
              0,
            ),
            child: Text(
              error,
              style: AppTextStyles.caption.copyWith(color: AppColors.error),
            ),
          ),
        Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimensions.md),
          child: Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: TextField(
                  controller: draft,
                  enabled: controller.canReply,
                  minLines: 1,
                  maxLines: 4,
                  textInputAction: TextInputAction.send,
                  onSubmitted: (_) => onSend(),
                  inputFormatters: [
                    LengthLimitingTextInputFormatter(controller.bodyLimit),
                  ],
                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 14),
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    // Only a closed thread loses the hint: a send in flight
                    // disables the field without changing what it asks for.
                    hintText: controller.isClosed
                        ? 'This conversation is closed'
                        : 'Write a message',
                    hintStyle: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textHint),
                    filled: true,
                    fillColor: AppColors.surfaceVariant,
                    contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                      horizontal: AppDimensions.md,
                      vertical: AppDimensions.md,
                    ),
                    border: OutlineInputBorder(
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(
                        AppDimensions.radiusXl,
                      ),
                      borderSide: BorderSide.none,
                    ),
                    enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(
                        AppDimensions.radiusXl,
                      ),
                      borderSide: BorderSide.none,
                    ),
                    focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(
                        AppDimensions.radiusXl,
                      ),
                      borderSide: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.primary),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: AppDimensions.sm),
              Material(
                color: AppColors.primary,
                shape: const CircleBorder(),
                child: IconButton(
                  tooltip: 'Send',
                  // Disabled while a send is outstanding, so a keen thumb
                  // cannot post the same line twice.
                  onPressed: controller.canReply ? onSend : null,
                  icon: Icon(
                    Icons.send_rounded,
                    color: AppColors.textOnPrimary,
                    size: 20,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

SOURCE FILE: D:\WorkingTrust\lib\features\notifications\controller\notification_controller.dart
SHA256: FB977648D9AD39CC354B77F5224D267D0F6634BF5C96D0C357CE188185BB22BA

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../../core/network/network_exceptions.dart';
import '../../../core/network/session_store.dart';
import '../models/notification_model.dart';
import '../services/notification_service.dart';

enum NotificationStatus { idle, loading, ready, empty, error }

/// The signed-in user's notifications, shared between the shell's bell and the
/// Notifications screen.
///
/// FCM is only a delivery signal: the database remains the source of truth.
/// The feed reads when the shell opens, when the app resumes, when a push
/// arrives, when the feed screen opens, and on pull-to-refresh — never on a
/// timer, so a session left open on screen produces no polling traffic.
class NotificationController extends ChangeNotifier {
  NotificationController._();

  static final NotificationController instance = NotificationController._();

  static const String sessionExpired =
      'Your session has expired. Please log in again.';

  final NotificationService _service = NotificationService.instance;

  final List<NotificationModel> _notifications = [];
  bool _sessionActive = false;
  bool _paused = false;
  bool _fetching = false;
  bool _mutating = false;
  NotificationStatus _status = NotificationStatus.idle;
  String _errorMessage = '';
  int _unread = 0;

  NotificationStatus get status => _status;
  String get errorMessage => _errorMessage;
  int get unread => _unread;
  bool get hasUnread => _unread > 0;
  bool get isLoading => _status == NotificationStatus.loading;
  bool get isRefreshing => _fetching;
  List<NotificationModel> get notifications =>
      List.unmodifiable(_notifications);

  /// Called when a session becomes available: loads at once, and from then on
  /// only reloads when the app is resumed.
  void startSession() {
    if (!_signedIn) return;
    _sessionActive = true;
    _paused = false;
    refresh();
  }

  /// Called when the session goes away. Drops every notification body, which is
  /// never written to disk.
  void endSession() {
    _sessionActive = false;
    _paused = false;
    _notifications.clear();
    _unread = 0;
    _status = NotificationStatus.idle;
    _errorMessage = '';
    notifyListeners();
  }

  /// Reloads when the app comes back to the foreground, so the user sees what
  /// arrived while they were away. Nothing is read while it is backgrounded.
  void handleLifecycle(AppLifecycleState state) {
    if (state == AppLifecycleState.resumed) {
      if (!_sessionActive || !_paused) return;
      _paused = false;
      refresh();
    } else if (state == AppLifecycleState.inactive ||
        state == AppLifecycleState.paused ||
        state == AppLifecycleState.detached) {
      _paused = true;
    }
  }

  /// The backend recognises this device only through its NextAuth cookie, so
  /// asking without one is a guaranteed 401 and pointless traffic.
  bool get _signedIn => SessionStore.instance.hasSessionCookie;

  /// [silent] keeps the current list on screen while it re-reads, which is what
  /// a resume and a pull-to-refresh want; only a cold first load shows a
  /// spinner.
  Future<void> refresh({bool silent = false}) async {
    if (_fetching) return;
    if (!_signedIn) {
      endSession();
      return;
    }
    _fetching = true;
    final cold = _notifications.isEmpty && _status != NotificationStatus.error;
    if (!silent && cold) {
      _status = NotificationStatus.loading;
      notifyListeners();
    }
    try {
      final feed = await _service.fetch();
      _notifications
        ..clear()
        ..addAll(feed.notifications);
      _unread = feed.unread;
      _status = _notifications.isEmpty
          ? NotificationStatus.empty
          : NotificationStatus.ready;
      _errorMessage = '';
    } on NetworkException catch (e) {
      _failFetch(e.message, expired: e.statusCode == 401);
    } catch (e) {
      _failFetch(e.toString(), expired: false);
    } finally {
      _fetching = false;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  /// Marks one read, then updates the row the backend agreed about.
  Future<bool> markRead(String id) {
    return _mutate(() => _service.markRead(id), () {
      final index = _notifications.indexWhere((n) => n.id == id);
      if (index == -1) return false;
      if (!_notifications[index].isRead) {
        _notifications[index] = _notifications[index].markRead();
        _unread = _unread > 0 ? _unread - 1 : 0;
      }
      return true;
    });
  }

  /// Dismisses one row (`PATCH` with `dismiss: true` — there is no DELETE).
  Future<bool> dismiss(String id) {
    return _mutate(() => _service.dismiss(id), () {
      final index = _notifications.indexWhere((n) => n.id == id);
      if (index == -1) return false;
      final wasUnread = !_notifications[index].isRead;
      _notifications.removeAt(index);
      if (wasUnread && _unread > 0) _unread--;
      if (_notifications.isEmpty) _status = NotificationStatus.empty;
      return true;
    });
  }

  Future<bool> markAllRead() {
    return _mutate(_service.markAllRead, () {
      for (var i = 0; i < _notifications.length; i++) {
        _notifications[i] = _notifications[i].markRead();
      }
      _unread = 0;
      return true;
    });
  }

  /// A tap opens nothing on its own: most backend `href`s are web-only paths
  /// this app has no screen for. Reading it is still the honest response, and
  /// the next re-read of the feed re-syncs from the server either way.
  Future<bool> openNotification(NotificationModel notification) {
    if (notification.isRead) return Future.value(true);
    return markRead(notification.id);
  }

  Future<bool> _mutate(
    Future<void> Function() action,
    bool Function() applyLocally,
  ) async {
    if (_mutating) return false;
    _mutating = true;
    var changed = false;
    try {
      await action();
      changed = applyLocally();
      _errorMessage = '';
    } on NetworkException catch (e) {
      if (e.statusCode == 401) {
        // Signed out mid-tap: forget the feed first, then say why the row did
        // not change. endSession() clears the message, so it goes after.
        endSession();
        _errorMessage = sessionExpired;
      } else {
        _errorMessage = e.message;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      _errorMessage = e.toString();
    } finally {
      _mutating = false;
      if (changed || _errorMessage.isNotEmpty) notifyListeners();
    }
    return changed;
  }

  void _failFetch(String message, {required bool expired}) {
    // An expired session is not retried: the device is signed out, so stop
    // asking and forget the list rather than showing someone else's rows.
    if (expired) {
      endSession();
      _errorMessage = sessionExpired;
      return;
    }
    _errorMessage = message;
    // Whatever was on screen stays there; a failed refresh must not blank it.
    if (_notifications.isEmpty) _status = NotificationStatus.error;
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _sessionActive = false;
    super.dispose();
  }
}

/// One row of the backend's `Notification` table, as `GET /api/notifications`
/// returns it. Field names match the Prisma record exactly; the backend has no
/// `isRead` flag — a null `readAt` is what unread means.
class NotificationModel {
  const NotificationModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.title,
    required this.body,
    required this.createdAt,
    this.type,
    this.href,
    this.readAt,
  });

  final String id;
  final String title;
  final String body;

  /// A machine label such as `BID_AWARDED` or `CHAT_MESSAGE`. The backend
  /// stores this as a plain string, so unknown values must display gracefully.
  final String? type;

  /// Where the web app sends the user, e.g. `/messages?task=abc`. Paths only,
  /// and only the web's own route table — most of them have no Flutter screen.
  final String? href;

  final DateTime createdAt;
  final DateTime? readAt;

  bool get isRead => readAt != null;

  bool get hasDestination => href != null && href!.isNotEmpty;

  /// The project whose chat this row points at, read out of a web href such as
  /// `/messages?task=abc123`. This is the only honest way the app can learn a
  /// task id: the backend has no conversation-list endpoint, and staff hrefs
  /// (`/admin/...`) belong to a role this app does not serve, so they answer
  /// null rather than opening a thread the user cannot reach.
  String? get chatTaskId {
    final link = href;
    if (link == null || link.isEmpty) return null;
    final uri = Uri.tryParse(link);
    if (uri == null || uri.path != '/messages') return null;
    final taskId = uri.queryParameters['task'];
    return (taskId == null || taskId.isEmpty) ? null : taskId;
  }

  factory NotificationModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return NotificationModel(
      id: json['id']?.toString() ?? '',
      title: json['title']?.toString() ?? '',
      body: json['body']?.toString() ?? '',
      type: json['type']?.toString(),
      href: json['href']?.toString(),
      readAt: _date(json['readAt']),
      createdAt: _date(json['createdAt']) ?? DateTime.now(),
    );
  }

  /// The same notification after the backend accepted `PATCH {id: …}`. Only
  /// `readAt` changes; dismissal removes the row from the list entirely.
  NotificationModel markRead() {
    if (isRead) return this;
    return NotificationModel(
      id: id,
      title: title,
      body: body,
      type: type,
      href: href,
      createdAt: createdAt,
      readAt: DateTime.now(),
    );
  }

  static DateTime? _date(Object? value) {
    if (value == null) return null;
    return DateTime.tryParse(value.toString());
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../../app/routes/route_names.dart';
import '../../../app/theme/app_colors.dart';
import '../../../app/theme/app_dimensions.dart';
import '../../../app/theme/app_text_styles.dart';
import '../../../core/widgets/app_card.dart';
import '../../../core/widgets/app_empty_state.dart';
import '../../../core/widgets/app_error_widget.dart';
import '../../../core/widgets/app_loader.dart';
import '../../../core/widgets/app_tag.dart';
import '../../../core/widgets/module_app_bar.dart';
import '../controller/notification_controller.dart';
import '../models/notification_model.dart';
import '../services/fcm_push_service.dart';

/// The real `GET /api/notifications` feed for the signed-in session.
class NotificationsScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const NotificationsScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<NotificationsScreen> createState() => _NotificationsScreenState();
}

class _NotificationsScreenState extends State<NotificationsScreen> {
  final NotificationController _controller = NotificationController.instance;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    // Opening the screen always re-reads, so a row created seconds ago shows.
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      if (mounted) _controller.refresh();
    });
  }

  void _showFailure() {
    if (!mounted) return;
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      SnackBar(
        content: Text(
          _controller.errorMessage.isEmpty
              ? 'Could not update this notification.'
              : _controller.errorMessage,
        ),
        behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _onTapNotification(NotificationModel item) async {
    final changed = await _controller.openNotification(item);
    if (!changed) _showFailure();
    if (!mounted) return;

    final destination = FcmPushService.destinationFor({
      'type': item.type ?? '',
      'href': item.href ?? '',
    });
    if (destination.route == RouteNames.notifications) return;
    await Navigator.of(context)
        .pushNamed(destination.route, arguments: destination.arguments);
  }

  Future<void> _onDismiss(NotificationModel item) async {
    final changed = await _controller.dismiss(item.id);
    if (!changed) _showFailure();
  }

  Future<void> _onMarkAllRead() async {
    final changed = await _controller.markAllRead();
    if (!changed) _showFailure();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: ModuleAppBar(
        title: 'Notifications',
        subtitle: 'Alerts & reminders',
        actions: [
          ListenableBuilder(
            listenable: _controller,
            builder: (context, _) {
              if (_controller.status != NotificationStatus.ready ||
                  !_controller.hasUnread) {
                return const SizedBox.shrink();
              }
              return IconButton(
                tooltip: 'Mark all read',
                onPressed: _onMarkAllRead,
                icon: const Icon(Icons.done_all),
              );
            },
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: ListenableBuilder(
        listenable: _controller,
        builder: (context, _) => _buildBody(),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildBody() {
    switch (_controller.status) {
      case NotificationStatus.loading:
        return const Center(child: AppLoader());
      case NotificationStatus.error:
        return AppErrorWidget(
          message: _controller.errorMessage.isEmpty
              ? 'Could not load your notifications.'
              : _controller.errorMessage,
          onRetry: () => _controller.refresh(),
        );
      case NotificationStatus.empty:
        return const AppEmptyState(
          message: 'No notifications yet.',
          icon: Icons.notifications_none,
        );
      case NotificationStatus.idle:
      case NotificationStatus.ready:
        final items = _controller.notifications;
        if (items.isEmpty) {
          // A lost session is worth stating plainly; retrying from here cannot
          // help, so this error carries no button.
          if (_controller.errorMessage.isNotEmpty) {
            return AppErrorWidget(message: _controller.errorMessage);
          }
          return const AppEmptyState(
            message: 'No notifications yet.',
            icon: Icons.notifications_none,
          );
        }
        return RefreshIndicator(
          onRefresh: () => _controller.refresh(silent: true),
          child: ListView.separated(
            physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimensions.md),
            itemCount: items.length,
            separatorBuilder: (context, _) =>
                const SizedBox(height: AppDimensions.sm),
            itemBuilder: (context, index) {
              final item = items[index];
              return Center(
                child: ConstrainedBox(
                  constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 560),
                  child: _NotificationRow(
                    key: ValueKey('notification-${item.id}'),
                    notification: item,
                    onTap: () => _onTapNotification(item),
                    onDismiss: () => _onDismiss(item),
                  ),
                ),
              );
            },
          ),
        );
    }
  }
}

class _NotificationRow extends StatelessWidget {
  final NotificationModel notification;
  final VoidCallback onTap;
  final VoidCallback onDismiss;

  const _NotificationRow({
    super.key,
    required this.notification,
    required this.onTap,
    required this.onDismiss,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    // An unread row is marked by a dot in the accent colour plus a bolder
    // title; read rows settle back to the same surface as everything else.
    final unread = !notification.isRead;
    return AppCard(
      onTap: onTap,
      background: unread ? AppColors.surface : AppColors.surfaceVariant,
      child: Row(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          SizedBox(
            width: 14,
            height: 22,
            child: unread
                ? Container(
                    key: ValueKey('unread-dot-${notification.id}'),
                    width: 9,
                    height: 9,
                    decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                      color: AppColors.primary,
                      shape: BoxShape.circle,
                    ),
                  )
                : null,
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: AppDimensions.sm),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  notification.title,
                  style: AppTextStyles.bodyMedium.copyWith(
                    fontWeight: unread ? FontWeight.w700 : FontWeight.w500,
                    color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: AppDimensions.xs),
                Text(
                  notification.body,
                  maxLines: 3,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                  style: AppTextStyles.bodySmall.copyWith(
                    color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: AppDimensions.sm),
                Row(
                  children: [
                    // The tag takes every leftover pixel so a long backend
                    // type ellipsizes instead of pushing the timestamp off a
                    // narrow phone's card.
                    Flexible(
                      child: Row(
                        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                        children: [
                          if (notification.type != null)
                            Flexible(
                              child: AppTag(_prettyType(notification.type!)),
                            ),
                          if (notification.hasDestination) ...[
                            const SizedBox(width: AppDimensions.sm),
                            Icon(
                              Icons.open_in_new,
                              size: AppDimensions.iconSm,
                              color: AppColors.textHint,
                            ),
                          ],
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: AppDimensions.sm),
                    Text(
                      _timeAgo(notification.createdAt),
                      maxLines: 1,
                      overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                      style: AppTextStyles.caption.copyWith(
                        color: AppColors.textHint,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          IconButton(
            tooltip: 'Dismiss',
            onPressed: onDismiss,
            iconSize: AppDimensions.iconSm,
            padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
            constraints: const BoxConstraints.tightFor(width: 32, height: 32),
            icon: const Icon(Icons.close, color: AppColors.textHint),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  /// `BID_AWARDED` reads better as `Bid awarded`; the value itself is the
  /// backend's, only the separators change.
  static String _prettyType(String type) {
    final words = type.replaceAll('_', ' ').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (words.isEmpty) return type;
    return words[0].toUpperCase() + words.substring(1);
  }

  static String _timeAgo(DateTime moment, {DateTime? now}) {
    final reference = now ?? DateTime.now();
    final delta = reference.difference(moment);
    if (delta.isNegative) return 'just now';
    if (delta.inMinutes < 1) return 'just now';
    if (delta.inMinutes < 60) return '${delta.inMinutes} min ago';
    if (delta.inHours < 24) return '${delta.inHours} h ago';
    if (delta.inDays < 7) return '${delta.inDays} d ago';
    final local = moment.toLocal();
    const months = [
      'Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', //
      'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec',
    ];
    return '${local.day} ${months[local.month - 1]} ${local.year}';
  }
}

import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:firebase_core/firebase_core.dart';
import 'package:firebase_messaging/firebase_messaging.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter_local_notifications/flutter_local_notifications.dart';
import 'package:flutter_secure_storage/flutter_secure_storage.dart';

import '../../../app/routes/route_names.dart';
import '../../../core/navigation/app_navigation.dart';
import '../../../core/network/api_client.dart';
import '../../../core/network/api_endpoints.dart';
import '../../../firebase_options.dart';
import '../controller/notification_controller.dart';
import 'notification_service.dart';

class PushDestination {
  const PushDestination(this.route, {this.arguments});

  final String route;
  final Object? arguments;
}

class FcmPushService {
  FcmPushService._();

  static final FcmPushService instance = FcmPushService._();
  static const String _tokenStorageKey = 'fcm.registration-token.v1';
  static const String _notificationChannelId = 'workingtrust_notifications_v2';
  static const String _notificationChannelName = 'WorkingTrust notifications';
  static const FlutterSecureStorage _storage = FlutterSecureStorage();
  static const Duration _logoutRequestTimeout = Duration(seconds: 4);
  static final FlutterLocalNotificationsPlugin _localNotifications =
      FlutterLocalNotificationsPlugin();

  final ApiClient _apiClient = ApiClient();
  FirebaseMessaging? _messaging;
  StreamSubscription<RemoteMessage>? _foregroundSubscription;
  StreamSubscription<RemoteMessage>? _openedSubscription;
  StreamSubscription<String>? _tokenSubscription;
  Map<String, String>? _pendingOpen;
  bool _initialized = false;
  bool _authenticated = false;
  bool _authResolved = false;
  bool _navigationReady = false;
  bool _registeringToken = false;

  Future<void> initialize() async {
    if (_initialized) return;
    _initialized = true;
    if (kIsWeb ||
        (defaultTargetPlatform != TargetPlatform.android &&
            defaultTargetPlatform != TargetPlatform.iOS)) {
      return;
    }

    try {
      if (Firebase.apps.isEmpty) {
        await Firebase.initializeApp(
          options: DefaultFirebaseOptions.currentPlatform,
        );
      }
      await _initializeLocalNotifications();
      final messaging = FirebaseMessaging.instance;
      _messaging = messaging;
      _foregroundSubscription = FirebaseMessaging.onMessage.listen(
        _showForegroundMessage,
        onError: (Object error) =>
            _log('Foreground message listener failed', error),
      );
      _openedSubscription = FirebaseMessaging.onMessageOpenedApp.listen(
        _handleOpen,
        onError: (Object error) =>
            _log('Notification tap listener failed', error),
      );
      _tokenSubscription = messaging.onRegistered.listen(
        (token) => unawaited(_registerRefreshedToken(token)),
        onError: (Object error) => _log('FCM registration failed', error),
      );

      final initialMessage = await messaging.getInitialMessage();
      if (initialMessage != null) _handleOpen(initialMessage);
      final localLaunch = await _localNotifications
          .getNotificationAppLaunchDetails();
      final localPayload = localLaunch?.notificationResponse?.payload;
      if (localLaunch?.didNotificationLaunchApp == true) {
        _handleLocalOpen(localPayload);
      }
    } on FirebaseException catch (error) {
      _log('Firebase initialization failed (${error.code})', error);
    } on UnsupportedError catch (error) {
      _log('Firebase is not configured for this platform', error);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _initializeLocalNotifications() async {
    await _localNotifications.initialize(
      const InitializationSettings(
        android: AndroidInitializationSettings('@mipmap/ic_launcher'),
        iOS: DarwinInitializationSettings(
          requestAlertPermission: false,
          requestBadgePermission: false,
          requestSoundPermission: false,
        ),
      ),
      onDidReceiveNotificationResponse: (response) {
        _handleLocalOpen(response.payload);
      },
    );

    await _localNotifications
        .resolvePlatformSpecificImplementation<
          AndroidFlutterLocalNotificationsPlugin
        >()
        ?.createNotificationChannel(
          const AndroidNotificationChannel(
            _notificationChannelId,
            _notificationChannelName,
            description: 'Messages and updates from WorkingTrust',
            importance: Importance.high,
            playSound: true,
            enableVibration: true,
          ),
        );
  }

  /// Called after login or session restoration; push setup must never block auth.
  Future<void> setAuthenticated(bool authenticated) async {
    _authResolved = true;
    _authenticated = authenticated;
    if (!authenticated) {
      _pendingOpen = null;
      return;
    }

    _flushPendingOpen();
    if (_navigationReady) await _registerCurrentTokenSafely();
  }

  void markNavigationReady() {
    _navigationReady = true;
    _flushPendingOpen();
    if (_authenticated) unawaited(_registerCurrentTokenSafely());
  }

  Future<void> signOut() async {
    _authResolved = true;
    _authenticated = false;
    _pendingOpen = null;
    _navigationReady = false;

    final tokens = <String>{};
    try {
      final storedToken = await _storage.read(key: _tokenStorageKey);
      if (storedToken != null && storedToken.isNotEmpty) {
        tokens.add(storedToken);
      }

      for (final token in tokens) {
        await _apiClient
            .delete(ApiEndpoints.fcmToken, body: {'token': token})
            .timeout(_logoutRequestTimeout);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      _log('Unable to deactivate this device token during sign-out', error);
    } finally {
      try {
        await _storage.delete(key: _tokenStorageKey);
      } catch (error) {
        _log('Unable to clear the saved FCM token', error);
      }
    }
  }

  static PushDestination destinationFor(Map<String, String> data) {
    final type = data['type'] ?? '';
    final href = Uri.tryParse(data['href'] ?? '');

    if (href?.path == '/messages') {
      final taskId = href?.queryParameters['task'];
      if (taskId != null && taskId.isNotEmpty) {
        return PushDestination(RouteNames.projectChat, arguments: taskId);
      }
    }

    if ((href?.path == '/settings' &&
            href?.queryParameters['tab'] == 'withdrawals') ||
        type == 'WITHDRAWAL_REQUESTED' ||
        type == 'WITHDRAWAL_PAID' ||
        type == 'WITHDRAWAL_REJECTED') {
      return const PushDestination(RouteNames.settings, arguments: 4);
    }

    return switch (type) {
      'BID_AWARDED' ||
      'BID_REJECTED' ||
      'SUBMISSION_REJECTED' => const PushDestination(RouteNames.proposals),
      'MEMBERSHIP_RENEWED' => const PushDestination(RouteNames.membershipPlan),
      'SECURITY' => const PushDestination(RouteNames.settings, arguments: 1),
      'DEPOSIT_APPROVED' ||
      'DEPOSIT_REJECTED' ||
      'SUBMISSION_APPROVED' => const PushDestination(RouteNames.wallet),
      'REPORT_UPDATE' ||
      'ACCOUNT_SUSPENDED' => const PushDestination(RouteNames.yourReports),
      _ => switch (href?.path) {
        '/wallet' || '/deposit' => const PushDestination(RouteNames.wallet),
        '/my-work' => const PushDestination(RouteNames.proposals),
        '/report' => const PushDestination(RouteNames.yourReports),
        '/membership' => const PushDestination(RouteNames.membershipPlan),
        '/dashboard' || '/find-work' => const PushDestination(RouteNames.home),
        _ => const PushDestination(RouteNames.notifications),
      },
    };
  }

  Future<void> _registerCurrentToken() async {
    if (_registeringToken) return;
    final messaging = _messaging;
    if (messaging == null) return;

    _registeringToken = true;
    try {
      final settings = await messaging.requestPermission(
        alert: true,
        badge: true,
        sound: true,
      );
      if (settings.authorizationStatus == AuthorizationStatus.denied) {
        await _deactivateSavedToken();
        return;
      }

      await messaging.register();
    } finally {
      _registeringToken = false;
    }
  }

  Future<void> _registerCurrentTokenSafely() async {
    try {
      await _registerCurrentToken();
    } catch (error) {
      _log('Unable to register this device for push notifications', error);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _registerToken(String token) async {
    if (!_authenticated) return;
    final platform = _platformName;
    if (platform == null) return;

    final previousToken = await _storage.read(key: _tokenStorageKey);
    await _apiClient.post(
      ApiEndpoints.fcmToken,
      body: {
        'token': token,
        'platform': platform,
        if (previousToken != null && previousToken != token)
          'previousToken': previousToken,
      },
    );
    await _storage.write(key: _tokenStorageKey, value: token);
  }

  Future<void> _registerRefreshedToken(String token) async {
    try {
      await _registerToken(token);
    } catch (error) {
      _log('FCM token registration failed', error);
    }
  }

  String? get _platformName {
    return switch (defaultTargetPlatform) {
      TargetPlatform.android => 'android',
      TargetPlatform.iOS => 'ios',
      _ => null,
    };
  }

  Future<void> _deactivateSavedToken() async {
    try {
      final token = await _storage.read(key: _tokenStorageKey);
      if (token == null || token.isEmpty) return;
      await _apiClient.delete(ApiEndpoints.fcmToken, body: {'token': token});
      await _storage.delete(key: _tokenStorageKey);
    } catch (error) {
      _log('Unable to deactivate a token after permission was denied', error);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _showForegroundMessage(RemoteMessage message) async {
    unawaited(_syncNotification(null));

    final title =
        message.notification?.title ?? message.data['title']?.toString() ?? '';
    final body =
        message.notification?.body ?? message.data['body']?.toString() ?? '';
    if (title.isEmpty && body.isEmpty) return;
    final data = _messageData(message);
    try {
      await _localNotifications.show(
        (message.messageId?.hashCode ?? DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch) &
            0x7fffffff,
        title,
        body,
        const NotificationDetails(
          android: AndroidNotificationDetails(
            _notificationChannelId,
            _notificationChannelName,
            channelDescription: 'Messages and updates from WorkingTrust',
            importance: Importance.high,
            priority: Priority.high,
            playSound: true,
            enableVibration: true,
          ),
          iOS: DarwinNotificationDetails(
            presentAlert: true,
            presentBadge: true,
            presentSound: true,
            presentBanner: true,
            presentList: true,
          ),
        ),
        payload: jsonEncode(data),
      );
    } catch (error) {
      _log('Unable to show foreground notification', error);
    }
  }

  void _handleOpen(RemoteMessage message) {
    _handleOpenData(_messageData(message));
  }

  Map<String, String> _messageData(RemoteMessage message) => <String, String>{
    for (final entry in message.data.entries)
      if (entry.value != null) entry.key: entry.value.toString(),
  };

  void _handleLocalOpen(String? payload) {
    if (payload == null || payload.isEmpty) return;
    try {
      final decoded = jsonDecode(payload);
      if (decoded is Map<String, dynamic>) {
        _handleOpenData({
          for (final entry in decoded.entries)
            if (entry.value != null) entry.key: entry.value.toString(),
        });
      }
    } on FormatException catch (error) {
      _log('Invalid local notification payload', error);
    }
  }

  void _handleOpenData(Map<String, String> data) {
    if (_authResolved && !_authenticated) return;
    _pendingOpen = data;
    _flushPendingOpen();
  }

  void _flushPendingOpen() {
    final data = _pendingOpen;
    if (data == null ||
        !_authResolved ||
        !_authenticated ||
        !_navigationReady) {
      return;
    }

    final navigator = AppNavigation.navigatorKey.currentState;
    if (navigator == null) return;

    _pendingOpen = null;
    final destination = destinationFor(data);
    navigator.pushNamed(destination.route, arguments: destination.arguments);
    unawaited(_syncNotification(data['id']));
  }

  Future<void> _syncNotification(String? id) async {
    if (id != null && id.isNotEmpty) {
      try {
        await NotificationService.instance.markRead(id);
      } catch (error) {
        _log('Unable to mark the opened notification as read', error);
      }
    }
    try {
      await NotificationController.instance.refresh(silent: true);
    } catch (error) {
      _log('Unable to sync the notification feed', error);
    }
  }

  void _log(String message, Object error) {
    final details = switch (error) {
      FirebaseException() =>
        ' [${error.plugin}/${error.code}] ${error.message ?? ''}',
      _ => ' $error',
    };
    debugPrint('[FCM] $message (${error.runtimeType})$details');
  }

  Future<void> dispose() async {
    await _foregroundSubscription?.cancel();
    await _openedSubscription?.cancel();
    await _tokenSubscription?.cancel();
  }
}

import '../../../core/network/api_client.dart';
import '../../../core/network/api_endpoints.dart';
import '../../../core/network/network_exceptions.dart';
import '../models/notification_model.dart';

/// One `GET /api/notifications` answer, kept together because the backend
/// counts unread itself: `{"notifications": [...], "unread": 0}`.
class NotificationFeed {
  const NotificationFeed({required this.notifications, required this.unread});

  final List<NotificationModel> notifications;
  final int unread;
}

/// Reads and mutates the notifications the backend has already created.
///
/// There is deliberately no create call here: rows come from real events on
/// the server (bids, submissions, withdrawals, admin actions), never from a
/// client.
class NotificationService {
  NotificationService._();

  static final NotificationService instance = NotificationService._();

  final ApiClient _apiClient = ApiClient();

  /// Authenticated by the NextAuth session cookie the shared [ApiClient]
  /// attaches, not by a bearer token.
  Future<NotificationFeed> fetch() async {
    final response = await _apiClient.get(ApiEndpoints.notifications);
    if (response is! Map) {
      throw NetworkException('The server did not return your notifications.');
    }
    final data = Map<String, dynamic>.from(response);
    final rows = data['notifications'];
    return NotificationFeed(
      notifications: rows is List
          ? [
              for (final row in rows)
                if (row is Map)
                  NotificationModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic>.from(row)),
            ]
          : const [],
      // The count is advisory: a missing or odd value just means no badge.
      unread: switch (data['unread']) {
        final int value => value < 0 ? 0 : value,
        final String value => int.tryParse(value) ?? 0,
        _ => 0,
      },
    );
  }

  Future<void> markRead(String id) => _patch({'id': id});

  Future<void> dismiss(String id) => _patch({'id': id, 'dismiss': true});

  Future<void> markAllRead() => _patch({'all': true});

  /// Reads a project's conversation on the web's terms: the backend marks the
  /// `CHAT_MESSAGE` rows whose href names that task, and that is the only
  /// read state a chat has. There is no per-message receipt.
  Future<void> markTaskChatRead(String taskId) => _patch({'taskId': taskId});

  Future<void> _patch(Map<String, dynamic> body) async {
    await _apiClient.patch(ApiEndpoints.notifications, body: body);
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';

import '../../../app/routes/route_names.dart';
import '../../../app/theme/app_colors.dart';
import '../../../app/theme/app_dimensions.dart';
