/// Four-tab shell. The tabs sit in a horizontal [PageView], so a swipe moves
/// between them and the navigation bar follows, and vice versa. Only the pages
/// the viewport reaches are mounted, which keeps a sign-in down to one screen
/// and one set of reads; the page a swipe came from is kept alive, so the
/// board's search text and filters survive a round trip.
class HomeScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const HomeScreen({super.key, this.initialTab = 0});

  /// Defaults to the dashboard, because that is what a sign-in lands on.
  final int initialTab;

  /// Membership Plan is not a destination here; it is one of the Profile
  /// modules, so the bar carries the four places a session is actually spent.
  static const List<_ShellTab> _tabs = [
    _ShellTab('Home', Icons.home_outlined, Icons.home, showsBell: true),
    _ShellTab(
      'Messages',
      Icons.chat_bubble_outline,
      Icons.chat_bubble,
      showsBell: true,
    ),
    _ShellTab('Find Work', Icons.work_outline, Icons.work, showsBell: true),
    _ShellTab('Profile', Icons.person_outline, Icons.person),
  ];

  @override
  State<HomeScreen> createState() => _HomeScreenState();

  /// The board, by its number in [_tabs].
  static const int boardTab = 2;

  /// The tab a pushed screen wants the shell to show, or null when the request
  /// has been taken. A screen the account reaches from a module — the wallet's
  /// "Find work", say — asks the shell it already runs under to move, rather
  /// than mounting a second shell over the first.
  static final ValueNotifier<int?> _requestedTab = ValueNotifier<int?>(null);

  /// Returns to the shell with [tab] showing.
  static void openTab(BuildContext context, int tab) {
    // Cleared first so a second request for the same tab still notifies.
    _requestedTab.value = null;
    _requestedTab.value = tab;
    Navigator.popUntil(context, (route) => route.isFirst);
  }
}

class _HomeScreenState extends State<HomeScreen> with WidgetsBindingObserver {
  late int _index = widget.initialTab;

  /// Drives the tab pages. `keepPage` is on by default, so the page a swipe
  /// moves off keeps its own state instead of being thrown away.
  late final PageController _pages = PageController(
    initialPage: widget.initialTab,
  );

  /// The shell owns the notification feed: it reads once for a signed-in
  /// session and re-reads when the app returns to the foreground.
  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    HomeScreen._requestedTab.addListener(_openRequestedTab);
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addObserver(this);
    NotificationController.instance.startSession();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      FcmPushService.instance.markNavigationReady();
      // The board ranks its "Best Matches" tab from the profile title and
      // shows the bid count, so the session read belongs to the shell rather
      // than to a tab body the account has not opened yet. Deferred to after
      // the frame because the controller notifies listeners inside this one.
      ProfileController.instance.load();
    });
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    HomeScreen._requestedTab.removeListener(_openRequestedTab);
    _pages.dispose();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.removeObserver(this);
    NotificationController.instance.endSession();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _openRequestedTab() {
    final tab = HomeScreen._requestedTab.value;
    if (tab == null) return;
    _goToTab(tab);
  }

  @override
  void didChangeAppLifecycleState(AppLifecycleState state) {
    NotificationController.instance.handleLifecycle(state);
  }

  /// The body of one tab, in the order [HomeScreen._tabs] lists them.
  Widget _body(int tab) => switch (tab) {
    0 => const DashboardScreen(),
    1 => const MessagesScreen(),
    2 => const FindWorkScreen(),
    _ => const ProfileMenuScreen(),
  };

  void _goToTab(int value) {
    if (value == _index) return;
    // The bar answers first, then the pages slide to it. A swipe lands through
    // [PageView.onPageChanged] and moves the bar the same way.
    setState(() => _index = value);
    _pages.animateToPage(
      value,
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
      curve: Curves.easeInOutCubic,
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final tab = HomeScreen._tabs[_index];
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        // One tinted strip for every tab. The per-tab word is gone: each body
        // already names itself, and the icons under the bar carry the names as
        // tooltips and accessibility labels.
        backgroundColor: AppColors.primary,
        foregroundColor: AppColors.textOnPrimary,
        // The bar is dark, so the clock and battery have to be light.
        systemOverlayStyle: SystemUiOverlayStyle.light,
        // Without this the bar takes on the theme's scroll tint as soon as a
        // tab's list moves under it.
        scrolledUnderElevation: 0,
        centerTitle: false,
        title: const _BrandMark(),
        actions: [
          if (tab.showsBell)
            ListenableBuilder(
              listenable: NotificationController.instance,
              builder: (context, _) {
                final unread = NotificationController.instance.unread;
                return IconButton(
                  tooltip: 'Notifications',
                  onPressed: () =>
                      Navigator.pushNamed(context, RouteNames.notifications),
                  // Same rule as before: the bell only appears with something
                  // to show, but the count is now the server's own.
                  icon: unread > 0
                      ? Badge(
                          backgroundColor: AppColors.error,
                          label: Text('$unread'),
                          child: const Icon(
                            Icons.notifications_none,
                            color: AppColors.textOnPrimary,
                          ),
                        )
                      : const Icon(
                          Icons.notifications_none,
                          color: AppColors.textOnPrimary,
                        ),
                );
              },
            ),
        ],
      ),
      body: PageView(
        controller: _pages,
        onPageChanged: (value) => setState(() => _index = value),
        children: [
          for (int tab = 0; tab < HomeScreen._tabs.length; tab++) _body(tab),
        ],
      ),
      bottomNavigationBar: NavigationBar(
        selectedIndex: _index,
        height: AppDimensions.bottomNavHeight,
        indicatorColor: AppColors.accent,
        // Icons only: the four destinations read faster as shapes than as
        // words, and the row of labels cost height a tab body needs.
        labelBehavior: NavigationDestinationLabelBehavior.alwaysHide,
        onDestinationSelected: _goToTab,
        destinations: [
          for (final item in HomeScreen._tabs)
            // A hidden label is not lost: [NavigationBar] puts each one back on
            // as the destination's tooltip and accessibility name.
            NavigationDestination(
              icon: Icon(item.icon, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
              selectedIcon: Icon(item.activeIcon, color: AppColors.primary),
              label: item.title,
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _ShellTab {
  final String title;
  final IconData icon;
  final IconData activeIcon;
  final bool showsBell;

  const _ShellTab(
    this.title,
    this.icon,
    this.activeIcon, {
    this.showsBell = false,
  });
}

  }
}

import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io' as io;

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart' show debugPrint;
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import 'package:http/io_client.dart' as http_io;

import '../../app/config/app_config.dart';
import '../constants/app_constants.dart';
import 'network_exceptions.dart';
import 'session_store.dart';

/// A response kept whole, for endpoints where the status code or the redirect
/// target carries the answer (NextAuth replies to a credentials POST with a
/// 302 and puts the reason in the Location it would have used).
class HttpResponse {
  const HttpResponse({
    required this.statusCode,
    required this.body,
    required this.setCookies,
  });

  final int statusCode;
  final String body;

  /// One entry per cookie, taken from the split Set-Cookie headers.
  final List<String> setCookies;

  bool get isSuccess => statusCode >= 200 && statusCode < 300;

  Map<String, dynamic>? get json {
    if (body.isEmpty) return null;
    try {
      final decoded = jsonDecode(body);
      return decoded is Map<String, dynamic> ? decoded : null;
    } on FormatException {
      return null;
    }
  }

  /// Whatever the backend said, whichever key it used. Falling back to a
  /// generic status line only when it said nothing.
  String get message {
    final data = json;
    final reported = data?['error'] ?? data?['message'];
    if (reported is String && reported.trim().isNotEmpty) {
      return reported.trim();
    }
    return switch (statusCode) {
      400 => 'The request was rejected.',
      401 => 'Not authorised.',
      403 => 'Access denied.',
      404 => 'Not found.',
      429 => 'Too many attempts. Please try again later.',
      >= 500 => 'The server had a problem. Please try again.',
      _ => 'Unexpected response ($statusCode).',
    };
  }
}

/// Cookie-aware transport for the WorkingTrust backend.
class ApiClient {
  ApiClient._internal();

  factory ApiClient() => _instance;

  static const String _jsonType = 'application/json';
  static const String _formType = 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded';
  static const Duration _timeout = Duration(
    seconds: AppConstants.apiTimeoutSeconds,
  );

  static final ApiClient _instance = ApiClient._internal();

  http.Client? _client;

  String get baseUrl => AppConfig.backendUrl;

  /// Tests inject an [http.MockClient] so no socket is ever opened.
  void useClient(http.Client client) {
    _client?.close();
    _client = client;
  }

  Future<dynamic> get(
    String endpoint, {
    Map<String, String>? queryParameters,
  }) => _json('GET', endpoint, queryParameters: queryParameters);

  Future<dynamic> post(
    String endpoint, {
    Object? body,
    Map<String, String>? queryParameters,
  }) => _json('POST', endpoint, body: body, queryParameters: queryParameters);

  Future<dynamic> put(String endpoint, {Object? body}) =>
      _json('PUT', endpoint, body: body);

  Future<dynamic> patch(String endpoint, {Object? body}) =>
      _json('PATCH', endpoint, body: body);

  /// Two account settings routes (`security/oauth`, `withdrawals`) read a JSON
  /// body on DELETE, so the body is optional rather than never sent.
  Future<dynamic> delete(String endpoint, {Object? body}) =>
      _json('DELETE', endpoint, body: body);

  /// JSON request/response helper. Throws [NetworkException] carrying the
  /// backend's own wording for any non-2xx answer.
  Future<dynamic> _json(
    String method,
    String endpoint, {
    Object? body,
    Map<String, String>? queryParameters,
  }) async {
    final response = await send(
      method,
      endpoint,
      body: body,
      queryParameters: queryParameters,
    );
    if (!response.isSuccess) {
      throw NetworkException(response.message, statusCode: response.statusCode);
    }
    return response.json ?? (response.body.isEmpty ? null : response.body);
  }

  /// Performs one request of any method. Redirects are never followed: a
  /// NextAuth 302 is the answer itself, and following it would drop the session
  /// cookie attached to that hop.
  Future<HttpResponse> send(
    String method,
    String endpoint, {
    Object? body,
    bool formEncoded = false,
    Map<String, String>? queryParameters,
    Map<String, String>? headers,
  }) async {
    final uri = Uri.parse('$baseUrl$endpoint')
        .replace(queryParameters: queryParameters);
    final request = http.Request(method, uri)..followRedirects = false;
    request.headers['Accept'] = _jsonType;

    if (body != null) {
      if (formEncoded) {
        request.headers['Content-Type'] = _formType;
        request.bodyFields = (body as Map).cast<String, String>();
      } else {
        request.headers['Content-Type'] = _jsonType;
        request.body = jsonEncode(body);
      }
    }

    if (headers != null) request.headers.addAll(headers);

    return _perform(request);
  }

  /// Uploads [bytes] as one `multipart/form-data` part named [field].
  ///
  /// The avatar route reads `formData().get("file")` and rejects anything whose
  /// part Content-Type is not JPG, PNG or WEBP, so the MIME type is set by the
  /// caller rather than guessed from the file name.
  Future<HttpResponse> postFile(
    String endpoint, {
    required String field,
    required List<int> bytes,
    required String filename,
    required String mimeType,
  }) {
    final boundary = 'workingtrust${DateTime.now().microsecondsSinceEpoch}';
    final request = http.Request('POST', Uri.parse('$baseUrl$endpoint'))
      ..followRedirects = false;
    request.headers['Accept'] = _jsonType;
    request.headers['Content-Type'] = 'multipart/form-data; boundary=$boundary';
    request.bodyBytes = <int>[
      ...utf8.encode(
        '--$boundary\r\n'
        'Content-Disposition: form-data; name="$field"; filename="$filename"\r\n'
        'Content-Type: $mimeType\r\n\r\n',
      ),
      ...bytes,
      ...utf8.encode('\r\n--$boundary--\r\n'),
    ];
    return _perform(request);
  }

  /// Attaches the stored cookies, runs one request and absorbs the cookies the
  /// response sets. Every request answers through here, so the console shows
  /// exactly one line per round trip.
  Future<HttpResponse> _perform(http.Request request) async {
    final cookie = SessionStore.instance.cookieHeader;
    if (cookie != null) request.headers['Cookie'] = cookie;

    final client = _client ??= _defaultClient();
    final stopwatch = Stopwatch()..start();
    _log('--> ${request.method} ${request.url}');
    try {
      final response = await client
          .send(request)
          .then(http.Response.fromStream)
          .timeout(_timeout);
      final setCookies =
          response.headersSplitValues['set-cookie'] ?? const <String>[];
      SessionStore.instance.absorb(setCookies);
      final result = HttpResponse(
        statusCode: response.statusCode,
        body: response.body,
        setCookies: setCookies,
      );
      _log(
        '<-- ${result.statusCode} ${request.method} ${request.url} '
        'in ${stopwatch.elapsedMilliseconds}ms'
        '${result.isSuccess ? '' : ' | ${result.message}'}',
      );
      return result;
    } on TimeoutException {
      _log(
        '<-- TIMEOUT ${request.method} ${request.url} after ${stopwatch.elapsedMilliseconds}ms',
      );
      throw NetworkException(
        'The server took too long to respond. Check your connection and try again.',
      );
    } on io.SocketException {
      _log(
        '<-- UNREACHABLE ${request.method} ${request.url} '
        '(no backend answering on $baseUrl)',
      );
      throw NetworkException(
        'Could not reach the server. Check that the backend is running and reachable from this device.',
      );
    } on http.ClientException catch (e) {
      _log('<-- CLIENT ERROR ${request.method} ${request.url}: ${e.message}');
      throw NetworkException(e.message);
    } on io.HttpException catch (e) {
      _log('<-- HTTP ERROR ${request.method} ${request.url}: $e');
      throw NetworkException('The connection to the server failed.');
    } catch (e) {
      if (e is NetworkException) rethrow;
      _log(
        '<-- FAILED ${request.method} ${request.url} in ${stopwatch.elapsedMilliseconds}ms: $e',
      );
      throw NetworkException('Something went wrong: $e');
    }
  }

  /// One line per request in the debug console, so a device run shows exactly
  /// which endpoint was hit. Request bodies are never logged — they carry
  /// passwords.
  void _log(String line) {
    if (AppConfig.enableLogging) debugPrint('[API] $line');
  }

  http.Client _defaultClient() {
    final raw = io.HttpClient()
      ..connectionTimeout = const Duration(seconds: 15);
    return http_io.IOClient(raw);
  }
}

/// Backend paths, mirroring the Next.js 15 App Router routes in
/// `src/app/api`. Everything under `/api/auth/` except `register`,
/// `verify-email`, `resend-verification`, `forgot-password`, `verify-otp`,
/// `reset-password` and `mobile-google` is served by the NextAuth catch-all
/// route.
class ApiEndpoints {
  ApiEndpoints._();

  static const String csrf = '/api/auth/csrf';
  static const String credentialsCallback = '/api/auth/callback/credentials';
  static const String session = '/api/auth/session';
  static const String register = '/api/auth/register';
  static const String verifyEmail = '/api/auth/verify-email';
  static const String resendVerification = '/api/auth/resend-verification';
  static const String forgotPassword = '/api/auth/forgot-password';
  static const String verifyOtp = '/api/auth/verify-otp';
  static const String resetPassword = '/api/auth/reset-password';

  /// Google sign-in for this app. `POST {idToken}` — the Google ID token the
  /// device obtained natively — and the server answers by setting the same
  /// NextAuth session cookie the website gets. It refuses with 401 for a token
  /// Google did not sign, 403 for a suspended account and 429 for too many
  /// attempts. Only the token goes out; it never comes back.
  static const String mobileGoogle = '/api/auth/mobile-google';

  /// Open projects for the Find Work board. `GET` answers `{tasks: [...]}`,
  /// newest first, and takes two filters: `category` (a slug from
  /// [categories]) and `q`, which the server matches case-insensitively
  /// against the title, description, requirements, deliverables, client name
  /// and category name. There is no pagination, no sort parameter and no
  /// single-task route — the whole board arrives in one response.
  static const String tasks = '/api/tasks';

  /// One category of the board. `GET` answers `{categories: [...]}` with
  /// `{id,name,slug,description}` only — no open-task counts.
  static const String categories = '/api/categories';

  /// Applying on a project. `POST` answers `201 {bid}` after it has spent the
  /// connects, and refuses with 400 for a short proposal, a closed project or a
  /// repeat application, 403 for a suspended account and 400 for too few
  /// connects. There is no route that reads back the bids this account sent.
  static String taskBid(String taskId) => '/api/tasks/$taskId/bid';

  /// The signed-in user's own notifications. `GET` lists them with an unread
  /// count; `PATCH` marks one read, dismisses one, or marks all read. There is
  /// no DELETE — dismissal is a PATCH with `dismiss: true`.
  static const String notifications = '/api/notifications';

  /// Authenticated registration and deactivation of this device's FCM token.
  static const String fcmToken = '/api/devices/fcm-token';

  /// The signed-in user's own profile. `GET` answers the whole thing in one
  /// response — `{user: {name, title, bio, skills, hourlyRate, avatarUrl,
  /// videoIntroUrl}, languages, education, certifications, employment,
  /// portfolio}` — scoped to the session cookie, with no parameters to pass.
  /// `PATCH` replaces it and always requires a `name`.
  static const String profile = '/api/profile';

  /// `POST` uploads the avatar as a multipart `file` part and answers with the
  /// stored URL; `DELETE` removes it. Never send `avatarUrl` through
  /// [profile]: the backend deletes the previous Cloudinary asset when that
  /// field changes.
  static const String profileAvatar = '/api/profile/avatar';

  /// The five profile sections a device writes one row at a time. Each `POST`
  /// answers `201 {item}` and each row is then addressed by its own id through
  /// [profileEntry]. They have no `GET` of their own — [profile] lists all five
  /// in one response.
  static const String profileLanguages = '/api/profile/languages';
  static const String profileEducation = '/api/profile/education';
  static const String profileCertifications = '/api/profile/certifications';
  static const String profileEmployment = '/api/profile/employment';
  static const String profilePortfolio = '/api/profile/portfolio';

  /// One row of a section, for `PATCH` and `DELETE`.
  static String profileEntry(String collection, String id) => '$collection/$id';

  /// `POST` takes a multipart `file` of up to 60 MB and answers with the
  /// stored `url` and the `mediaType` the project row should keep.
  static const String profilePortfolioMedia = '/api/profile/portfolio/media';

  /// The intro video, held as one external link on the account: `POST {url}`
  /// answers `{url}`, `DELETE` clears it. There is no upload for it.
  static const String profileVideoIntro = '/api/profile/video-intro';

  /// Two-step verification. `GET` answers the three switches plus `phone`,
  /// `email`, `authenticatorConfigured` and which sign-in providers are linked;
  /// `PATCH` takes exactly the three booleans and answers `{ok:true, …}`.
  static const String accountSecurity = '/api/account/security';

  /// Notification *preferences* — not the list in [notifications]. `GET`
  /// answers `{prefs: {…}}` and `PATCH` takes those six keys verbatim,
  /// answering `{ok:true, prefs: {…}}`.
  static const String accountNotifications = '/api/account/notifications';

  /// `PATCH` only, and it needs a `section`: `"account"` with `name` and
  /// `email`, or `"location"` with `timezone`, `country`, `stateProvince` and
  /// `city`. Both answer `{ok:true, user: {…}, message}`.
  static const String accountContact = '/api/account/contact';

  /// `POST` with `currentPassword`, `newPassword` and `confirmPassword`,
  /// answering `{ok:true}`. There is no read endpoint for a password.
  static const String accountPassword = '/api/account/password';

  /// The authenticator half of two-step verification. `POST` takes no body and
  /// answers `{secret, otpauthUrl}` — the server holds that secret as pending
  /// until `PUT {code}` accepts a code from the app, which is the only thing
  /// that sets `twoStepAuthenticator`. `DELETE` clears the secret and the
  /// switch together. A device shows the secret only for as long as the setup
  /// is open; it is never stored.
  static const String accountSecurityTotp = '/api/account/security/totp';

  /// The SMS half, same shape: `POST` starts it and answers `{ok, channel,
  /// message}` with `channel` of `"sms"` or `"email"` when the provider is
  /// unusable, `PUT {code}` confirms the code that was sent, and `DELETE`
  /// turns the switch off. Nothing readable comes back, and the account needs a
  /// phone number before the route will start at all.
  static const String accountSecuritySms = '/api/account/security/sms';

  /// `DELETE` only, with `{provider: "google" | "apple"}`, answering `{ok:true}`.
  /// There is no route that links a provider — that stays a browser sign-in —
  /// so a device can disconnect one it already reported as linked.
  static const String accountSecurityOauth = '/api/account/security/oauth';

  /// The one saved payment method. `GET` answers `{method, methods}` with at
  /// most a single entry, whose card row carries `cardLast4`, `expMonth` and
  /// `expYear` and never a full number. `POST` replaces it and answers `201
  /// {ok, method, methods}`; `DELETE` clears it back to `{method:null,
  /// methods:[]}`. A card `securityCode` is checked and thrown away, and no
  /// payment is ever taken through here.
  static const String accountBilling = '/api/account/billing';

  /// The saved withdrawal accounts, up to five. `GET` answers `{methods:[…]}`
  /// of `{id, method, label, accountDetails, createdAt}`; `POST {method,
  /// accountDetails, label}` answers `201 {ok, methods}`; `DELETE {id}` removes
  /// one row. These are for listing only — withdrawal processing reads the
  /// separate `billingMethods` column, so saving an account here does not make
  /// it payable.
  static const String accountWithdrawals = '/api/account/withdrawals';

  /// A payout request. `POST {assignmentIds}` — between one and twenty of this
  /// account's own approved jobs — answers `201 {withdrawals}` with one row per
  /// job, and the server sets each amount from the job, decrements the stored
  /// balance and writes the ledger entry. A device never sends an amount. It is
  /// refused with 400 `NO_PAYMENT_METHOD` when no billing method is on file, 409
  /// when that work already has a request, 400 when the balance cannot cover it
  /// and 403 when the account is suspended; 401 without the cookie. Six
  /// requests an hour is all the route allows one account, answering 429 with
  /// the seconds left to wait.
  static const String withdrawals = '/api/withdrawals';

  /// One project's chat thread. `GET` answers the whole thread with the
  /// participant names and the `flagged`/`closed` flags; `POST {body,
  /// attachmentUrl}` answers `201 {message, …}`. It is refused with 403 until
  /// this account has been hired on that project, and a closed thread refuses
  /// new messages. There is no edit, delete, list or read-receipt call, and no
  /// way to discover which task ids exist — a thread is only ever addressed by
  /// an id that came from elsewhere, such as a `CHAT_MESSAGE` notification's
  /// `/messages?task=<id>` href.
  static String taskChat(String taskId) => '/api/tasks/$taskId/chat';

  /// The signed-in account's wallet, read from the same query the website's
  /// Wallet page runs. `GET` answers `200 {balance, connects, planActive,
  /// pendingDeposit, earningsByJob, transactions, connectsActivity}` — the
  /// stored balance and connects, the newest 30 money rows and 20 connects rows,
  /// the approved jobs that paid out, and the one deposit still `PENDING`. There
  /// are no parameters and nothing is written: reading a wallet cannot change a
  /// balance, a ledger row or a deposit. Refused with 401 without the cookie.
  static const String wallet = '/api/wallet';

  /// The Overview page's four figures and its job list. `GET` answers `200
  /// {workInProgressAmount, inReviewAmount, pendingWithdrawalsAmount,
  /// availableBalance, jobs}` with every assignment still ACTIVE, SUBMITTED or
  /// REJECTED, each carrying its own `status`, the page's `statusLabel`, the
  /// client's name and the due date. No parameters, and no balance is moved by
  /// reading it. Refused with 401 without the cookie.
  static const String reportsOverview = '/api/reports/overview';

  /// The Transactions page's merged money feed. `GET` answers `200 {availableBalance,
  /// pendingEarnings, hasPaymentMethod, rows, clients, total, page, limit,
  /// totalPages}` with `rows` newest first and already paged. Four parameters are
  /// honoured: `page` and `limit` (the server clamps `limit` to its own ceiling),
  /// `type` — one of `all, Client, Withdrawal, Bids, Subscription, Other, Earning`
  /// — and `client`, a name from `clients` or `all`. Anything else for `type` is
  /// a 400 with an `error` and no query. Refused with 401 without the cookie.
  static const String reportsTransactions = '/api/reports/transactions';

  /// My Stats, as the website computes them. `GET` answers `200 {twelveMonthEarnings,
  /// jobSuccessScore, connects, nextMonthConnects, proposalFunnel, profileViews,
  /// impressions, clicks, profileSeries}`. `proposalDays` and `profileDays` take
  /// `7`, `15` or `30`; anything else falls back to the route's own default, so a
  /// window nobody offered is never pretended. The figures are the server's — the
  /// app never recomputes a funnel or a score. Refused with 401 without the
  /// cookie, and [reportsMyStats] is the only source for these numbers.
  static const String reportsMyStats = '/api/reports/my-stats';

  /// The weekly report. `GET` answers the freelancer summary `{since, earnings,
  /// projects, proposals, approvedCount, submittedCount, messages}`, or `{since,
  /// clients}` with `?scope=client` — and the client scope needs an active Pro
  /// plan, answering 403 `{error}` without one. Refused with 401 without the
  /// cookie. Nothing is recalculated on the device.
  static const String reportsWeekly = '/api/reports/weekly';

  /// The same report as the text file the website downloads: `GET` answers
  /// `text/plain` with `Content-Disposition: attachment`, and `?scope=client`
  /// gives the client breakdown, which 403s for an account with no Pro plan.
  /// `scope=client` on this route and on [reportsWeekly] mean the same thing.
  static const String reportsWeeklyDownload = '/api/reports/weekly/download';

  /// The proposals this account sent. `GET` answers `200 {proposals, activeWork,
  /// counts, total, page, limit, totalPages}` — `counts` holds the four bid
  /// statuses whatever page is open, `activeWork` is the assignments still ACTIVE
  /// or SUBMITTED, and `proposals` is the paged, newest-first bid list. `status`
  /// narrows it to `PENDING`, `SHORTLISTED`, `AWARDED` or `REJECTED`; anything
  /// else is a 400 with an `error` and no query. `page` and `limit` are clamped
  /// by the server. Refused with 401 without the cookie, and no bid is awarded,
  /// rejected or refunded by reading it.
  static const String bidsMy = '/api/bids/my';

  /// The packages the app sells, read straight from the `Package` table.
  /// `GET ?kind=BIDS` or `?kind=MEMBERSHIP` answers `200 {packages:[…]}` with
  /// the active rows of that kind in the Admin Panel's order (`sortOrder`, then
  /// price), each carrying its real `id`, `name`, `priceUsd`, `connects`,
  /// `description`, `validityDays`, `features`, `audience`, `featured`, `active`
  /// and `serviceFeePct`. Any other `kind` is a 400 with an `error`; nothing
  /// else is ever written. No Stripe configuration crosses the wire.
  static const String packages = '/api/packages';

  /// Starting a Stripe payment for one package. `POST {packageId, purpose,
  /// returnTo}` — `packageId` is the `Package` row's own id, `purpose` is
  /// `"bids"` or `"membership"`, and `returnTo` is the website page Stripe
  /// hands the browser back to. The amount is never sent: the server reads the
  /// price and the connects off its own row and opens a one-off `payment` for a
  /// bid pack or a `subscription` for a plan. Answers
  /// `200 {success, checkoutUrl, sessionId}`. Refusals: 401 with no session,
  /// 400 `"Package is required."` / `"Invalid package."` for a missing, unknown
  /// or deactivated id, 400 `"Invalid membership plan."` or
  /// `"Invalid Buy Bids package."` when the row is of the other kind, and 500
  /// when no Stripe Price ID is configured for it. Only an id [packages] has
  /// listed can be bought, because that is the only route that reveals one.
  static const String stripeCheckout = '/api/deposits/stripe/checkout';

  /// Asking whether one Checkout session has been paid — the only proof of
  /// payment a device can get. `POST {sessionId}` with the id [stripeCheckout]
  /// returned: `200 {ok:true, purpose, planName, connects,
  /// stripeSubscriptionId, alreadyFulfilled}` once Stripe reports the session
  /// paid, which is also when the server applies the connects or the membership
  /// itself; `409 {error, paymentStatus}` while it has not. Anything the device
  /// does after the browser closes is a question, never a credit.
  static const String stripeConfirm = '/api/deposits/stripe/confirm';
}

import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io' as io;

import 'package:flutter_secure_storage/flutter_secure_storage.dart';

/// Where the cookie jar keeps its copy between app launches.
abstract class SessionCookieStorage {
  Future<String?> read(String key);

  Future<void> write(String key, String value);

  Future<void> delete(String key);
}

/// The device keystore (Keychain / Keystore) behind the Dart API.
class _SecureCookieStorage implements SessionCookieStorage {
  const _SecureCookieStorage();

  static const FlutterSecureStorage _storage = FlutterSecureStorage();

  @override
  Future<String?> read(String key) => _storage.read(key: key);

  @override
  Future<void> write(String key, String value) =>
      _storage.write(key: key, value: value);

  @override
  Future<void> delete(String key) => _storage.delete(key: key);
}

/// Widget tests cannot await a platform channel: `testWidgets` runs on a fake
/// clock, so a real plugin response never arrives.
class _MemoryCookieStorage implements SessionCookieStorage {
  String? _value;

  @override
  Future<String?> read(String key) async => _value;

  @override
  Future<void> write(String key, String value) async => _value = value;

  @override
  Future<void> delete(String key) async => _value = null;
}

/// The cookies the backend set for us, kept between app launches.
///
/// `package:http` is not a browser: it neither stores nor replays cookies, and
/// the Next.js backend recognises a signed-in device only through the NextAuth
/// session cookie. This jar is therefore the mobile equivalent of browser
/// cookie storage, scoped to the single configured backend origin.
///
/// Only cookie name/value pairs are kept — no passwords, no server secrets.
class SessionStore {
  SessionStore._();

  static final SessionStore instance = SessionStore._();

  static const String _storageKey = 'session.cookies.v1';

  final Map<String, String> _cookies = {};
  SessionCookieStorage _storage = const _SecureCookieStorage();
  bool _loaded = false;

  /// Keeps the jar in RAM only. Tests use this to stay deterministic.
  void useInMemoryStorage() => _storage = _MemoryCookieStorage();

  /// Single `Cookie` request header value, or null while signed out.
  String? get cookieHeader => _cookies.isEmpty
      ? null
      : _cookies.entries.map((e) => '${e.key}=${e.value}').join('; ');

  String? value(String name) => _cookies[name];

  /// Whether a NextAuth session cookie is present. Never proves the session is
  /// still valid server-side — only `GET /api/auth/session` does that. The
  /// backend chunks a long cookie as `…session-token.0`, so match any name.
  bool get hasSessionCookie =>
      _cookies.keys.any((name) => name.contains('session-token'));

  /// Loads cookies saved by an earlier run. Idempotent.
  Future<void> load() async {
    if (_loaded) return;
    _loaded = true;
    try {
      final stored = await _storage.read(_storageKey);
      if (stored == null) return;
      final decoded = jsonDecode(stored);
      if (decoded is! Map) return;
      _cookies.addAll(decoded.map((k, v) => MapEntry('$k', '$v')));
    } catch (_) {
      // Missing plugin (tests) or unreadable data simply means signed out.
    }
  }

  /// Stores every `Set-Cookie` value from a response, dropping expired ones.
  ///
  /// [setCookies] must already be one entry per cookie; the raw `headers` map
  /// joins several Set-Cookie values with commas, which would corrupt them.
  void absorb(List<String> setCookies) {
    if (setCookies.isEmpty) return;
    for (final raw in setCookies) {
      final io.Cookie cookie;
      try {
        cookie = io.Cookie.fromSetCookieValue(raw);
      } on FormatException {
        continue;
      }
      if (cookie.name.isEmpty) continue;
      final expired =
          (cookie.maxAge != null && cookie.maxAge! <= 0) ||
          (cookie.expires != null && cookie.expires!.isBefore(DateTime.now()));
      if (expired) {
        _cookies.remove(cookie.name);
      } else {
        _cookies[cookie.name] = cookie.value;
      }
    }
    unawaited(_persist());
  }

  /// Signs out locally: NextAuth uses stateless JWTs, so there is nothing to
  /// revoke server-side once the device drops its cookies.
  Future<void> clear() async {
    _cookies.clear();
    _loaded = true;
    try {
      await _storage.delete(_storageKey);
    } catch (_) {
      // Nothing persisted, nothing to delete.
    }
  }

  Future<void> _persist() async {
    try {
      await _storage.write(_storageKey, jsonEncode(_cookies));
    } catch (_) {
      // In-memory only when secure storage is unavailable.
    }
  }
}

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';

import '../../notifications/services/fcm_push_service.dart';
import '../models/experience_level.dart';
import '../models/user_model.dart';
import '../models/work_category.dart';
import '../repositories/auth_repository.dart';
import '../services/google_sign_in_service.dart';

enum AuthStatus { idle, loading, success, error }

class AuthController extends ChangeNotifier {
  /// Built with the device's own Google SDK. Tests pass a supplier that answers
  /// without one, because a widget test has no platform channel to call.
  AuthController({GoogleIdTokenSupplier? googleIdToken})
    : _googleIdToken = googleIdToken ?? _googleSignInService.requestIdToken;

  /// Reported when Google's SDK has no way to run here, i.e. a desktop build or
  /// an unsupported platform. On a phone the button works.
  static const String googleSignInUnavailable =
      'Google sign-in is not available on this device. Please use your email and password.';

  static final GoogleSignInService _googleSignInService = GoogleSignInService();

  final GoogleIdTokenSupplier _googleIdToken;
  final AuthRepository _authRepository = AuthRepository();

  AuthStatus _status = AuthStatus.idle;
  String _errorMessage = '';
  UserModel? _user;
  List<WorkCategory> _categories = const [];
  String _categoriesError = '';
  bool _categoriesLoading = false;

  AuthStatus get status => _status;
  String get errorMessage => _errorMessage;
  UserModel? get user => _user;
  bool get isLoading => _status == AuthStatus.loading;
  bool get isAuthenticated => _user != null;

  List<WorkCategory> get categories => _categories;
  String get categoriesError => _categoriesError;
  bool get categoriesLoading => _categoriesLoading;

  /// Registers an account. A successful call does NOT sign the user in: the
  /// backend verifies the email before it will issue a session.
  Future<bool> signup({
    required String name,
    required String email,
    required String password,
    required List<String> interestSlugs,
    required ExperienceLevel experienceLevel,
    String? phone,
    String? city,
    String? country,
    String? postalCode,
  }) async {
    if (isLoading) return false;
    _start();
    try {
      _user = await _authRepository.signup(
        name: name,
        email: email,
        password: password,
        interestSlugs: interestSlugs,
        experienceLevel: experienceLevel,
        phone: phone,
        city: city,
        country: country,
        postalCode: postalCode,
      );
      // The account exists but has no session, so nothing here may be treated
      // as signed in.
      _user = null;
      _finish(AuthStatus.success);
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      _fail(e);
      return false;
    }
  }

  Future<bool> login({required String email, required String password}) async {
    if (isLoading) return false;
    _start();
    try {
      _user = await _authRepository.login(email: email, password: password);
      unawaited(FcmPushService.instance.setAuthenticated(true));
      _finish(AuthStatus.success);
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      _fail(e);
      return false;
    }
  }

  /// Signs the device in with the Google account it picks, and leaves it signed
  /// in: the backend answers the token with the NextAuth session cookie, which
  /// the existing jar stores and replays, so every later request is already
  /// authenticated. Returns false when the user cancels (silently, since
  /// nothing went wrong) or when Google or the backend refuses.
  Future<bool> signInWithGoogle() async {
    if (isLoading) return false;
    _start();
    try {
      _user = await _authRepository.signInWithGoogle(await _googleIdToken());
      unawaited(FcmPushService.instance.setAuthenticated(true));
      _finish(AuthStatus.success);
      return true;
    } on GoogleSignInCanceled {
      _user = null;
      _status = AuthStatus.idle;
      _errorMessage = '';
      notifyListeners();
      return false;
    } on GoogleSignInUnsupported {
      _user = null;
      _failState(googleSignInUnavailable);
      return false;
    } catch (e) {
      _user = null;
      _fail(e);
      return false;
    }
  }

  /// Loads the session the saved cookies still describe, on app reopen.
  /// Returns null when the device is signed out or the session expired.
  Future<UserModel?> restoreSession() async {
    try {
      final user = await _authRepository.currentSession();
      _user = user;
      unawaited(FcmPushService.instance.setAuthenticated(user != null));
      _finish(user == null ? AuthStatus.idle : AuthStatus.success);
      return user;
    } catch (e) {
      _user = null;
      unawaited(FcmPushService.instance.setAuthenticated(false));
      _finish(AuthStatus.idle);
      return null;
    }
  }

  Future<List<WorkCategory>> loadCategories() async {
    if (_categoriesLoading) return _categories;
    if (_categories.isNotEmpty) return _categories;
    _categoriesLoading = true;
    _categoriesError = '';
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      _categories = await _authRepository.categories();
      if (_categories.isEmpty) {
        _categoriesError = 'No work fields are available yet.';
      }
    } catch (e) {
      _categoriesError = _messageFrom(e);
    }
    _categoriesLoading = false;
    notifyListeners();
    return _categories;
  }

  /// Asks the backend to email a 6-digit code. The returned string is the
  /// backend's own confirmation wording.
  Future<String?> forgotPassword({required String email}) async {
    if (isLoading) return null;
    _start();
    try {
      final message = await _authRepository.forgotPassword(email: email);
      _finish(AuthStatus.success);
      return message;
    } catch (e) {
      _fail(e);
      return null;
    }
  }

  /// Exchanges the emailed code for a short-lived reset token.
  Future<String?> verifyOtp({
    required String email,
    required String otp,
  }) async {
    if (isLoading) return null;
    _start();
    try {
      final token = await _authRepository.verifyOtp(email: email, otp: otp);
      _finish(AuthStatus.success);
      return token;
    } catch (e) {
      _fail(e);
      return null;
    }
  }

  Future<bool> resetPassword({
    required String token,
    required String password,
  }) async {
    if (isLoading) return false;
    _start();
    try {
      await _authRepository.resetPassword(token: token, password: password);
      _finish(AuthStatus.success);
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      _fail(e);
      return false;
    }
  }

  Future<bool> resendVerification({required String email}) async {
    if (isLoading) return false;
    _start();
    try {
      await _authRepository.resendVerification(email: email);
      _finish(AuthStatus.success);
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      _fail(e);
      return false;
    }
  }

  Future<void> logout() async {
    await FcmPushService.instance.signOut();
    _user = null;
    _status = AuthStatus.idle;
    _errorMessage = '';
    notifyListeners();
    await _authRepository.logout();
  }

  void _start() {
    _status = AuthStatus.loading;
    _errorMessage = '';
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void _finish(AuthStatus value) {
    _status = value;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void _fail(Object e) {
    _failState(_messageFrom(e));
  }

  void _failState(String message) {
    _errorMessage = message;
    _status = AuthStatus.error;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  String _messageFrom(Object e) => e.toString();
}

SOURCE FILE: D:\WorkingTrust\lib\features\auth\services\auth_service.dart
SHA256: 9DDE2DAE36CF2CD2D168C3CBB5CE75C0473EABFAAC027CEE4714290F135480CB

import '../../../app/config/app_config.dart';
import '../../../core/network/api_client.dart';
import '../../../core/network/api_endpoints.dart';
import '../../../core/network/network_exceptions.dart';
import '../../../core/network/session_store.dart';
import '../models/experience_level.dart';
import '../models/user_model.dart';
import '../models/work_category.dart';

/// Talks to the Next.js 15 backend: NextAuth cookie sessions plus the
/// registration and email-verification routes around it.
///
/// There is no Bearer token anywhere in this app — the backend only accepts
/// NextAuth cookies, and [SessionStore] replays them on every request.
class AuthService {
  final ApiClient _apiClient = ApiClient();

  /// `POST /api/auth/register`. Creates the account but establishes no
  /// session: the backend requires the emailed verification link first.
  Future<UserModel> register({
    required String name,
    required String email,
    required String password,
    required List<String> interestSlugs,
    required ExperienceLevel experienceLevel,
    String? phone,
    String? city,
    String? country,
    String? postalCode,
  }) async {
    final body = <String, dynamic>{
      'name': name,
      'email': email,
      'password': password,
      'interestSlugs': interestSlugs,
      'experienceLevel': experienceLevel.value,
    };
    // The schema treats these as optional or empty string; send only what the
    // user actually filled in.
    if (phone != null && phone.trim().isNotEmpty) {
      body['phone'] = phone.trim();
    }
    if (city != null && city.trim().isNotEmpty) body['city'] = city.trim();
    if (country != null && country.trim().isNotEmpty) {
      body['country'] = country.trim();
    }
    if (postalCode != null && postalCode.trim().isNotEmpty) {
      body['postalCode'] = postalCode.trim();
    }

    final response = await _apiClient.post(ApiEndpoints.register, body: body);
    final user = (response as Map<String, dynamic>?)?['user'];
    if (user is! Map<String, dynamic>) {
      throw NetworkException('The server did not return the new account.');
    }
    return UserModel.fromJson(user);
  }

  /// NextAuth credentials sign-in, in the three steps the protocol requires.
  ///
  /// A 200/302 alone never means logged in — the session is always re-read
  /// from the backend before this returns.
  Future<UserModel> login({
    required String email,
    required String password,
  }) async {
    final csrfToken = await _csrfToken();

    // NextAuth answers with a 302 whose Location we would otherwise have to
    // follow; json=true turns it into a JSON body carrying that same URL while
    // keeping the Set-Cookie headers, so no redirect is ever followed.
    final response = await _apiClient.send(
      'POST',
      ApiEndpoints.credentialsCallback,
      formEncoded: true,
      body: {
        'csrfToken': csrfToken,
        'email': email,
        'password': password,
        'callbackUrl': '${AppConfig.backendUrl}/dashboard',
        'json': 'true',
      },
    );

    final redirect = response.json?['url'];
    if (redirect is String) {
      final error = Uri.parse(redirect).queryParameters['error'];
      if (error != null) throw NetworkException(_describeFailure(error));
    } else if (response.isSuccess) {
      throw NetworkException(
        'The server did not confirm the sign-in response.',
        statusCode: response.statusCode,
      );
    }

    // Whatever the callback claimed, cookies are only a session if the backend
    // still agrees.
    final session = await readSession();
    if (session == null) {
      throw NetworkException(
        'Sign-in did not complete. Please try again.',
        statusCode: response.statusCode,
      );
    }
    return session;
  }

  /// `POST /api/auth/mobile-google` — trades a Google ID token for a session.
  ///
  /// The route answers with the same NextAuth session cookie the website gets,
  /// which [SessionStore] absorbs on the way back, so the cookie lands in this
  /// app's jar rather than in a browser's. As with the credentials flow, the
  /// response alone proves nothing: the session is read back from the backend
  /// before this returns.
  Future<UserModel> signInWithGoogleIdToken(String idToken) async {
    await _apiClient.post(
      ApiEndpoints.mobileGoogle,
      body: {'idToken': idToken},
    );

    final session = await readSession();
    if (session == null) {
      throw NetworkException(
        'Google sign-in did not complete. Please try again.',
      );
    }
    return session;
  }

  /// `GET /api/auth/session`. Returns null when the cookie is missing,
  /// rejected or past its expiry.
  Future<UserModel?> readSession() async {
    // A NextAuth session only exists as a cookie this device holds, so with no
    // session cookie there is nothing to ask about.
    await SessionStore.instance.load();
    if (!SessionStore.instance.hasSessionCookie) return null;

    final response = await _apiClient.send('GET', ApiEndpoints.session);
    final user = response.json?['user'];
    if (user is! Map<String, dynamic> ||
        (user['email']?.toString() ?? '').isEmpty) {
      // The server no longer recognises us — expired or signed out elsewhere.
      await SessionStore.instance.clear();
      return null;
    }
    return UserModel.fromJson(user);
  }

  Future<String> _csrfToken() async {
    final response = await _apiClient.get(ApiEndpoints.csrf);
    final token = (response as Map<String, dynamic>?)?['csrfToken'];
    if (token is! String || token.isEmpty) {
      throw NetworkException('The server did not return a CSRF token.');
    }
    return token;
  }

  /// NextAuth puts the thrown `authorize()` message into the error query
  /// parameter, so the backend's own wording reaches us verbatim.
  String _describeFailure(String error) {
    if (error == 'CredentialsSignin') {
      return 'Incorrect email or password.';
    }
    if (error == 'AccessDenied') {
      return 'Your account cannot sign in. Contact support.';
    }
    if (error == 'Configuration') {
      return 'Sign-in is not configured correctly on the server.';
    }
    return error;
  }

  /// Drops the session. NextAuth sessions are stateless JWTs held only by this
  /// device, so deleting the cookies is the logout.
  Future<void> logout() async {
    await SessionStore.instance.clear();
  }

  /// `POST /api/auth/verify-email` — the token from the emailed link.
  Future<String> verifyEmailToken(String token) async {
    final response = await _apiClient.post(
      ApiEndpoints.verifyEmail,
      body: {'token': token},
    );
    return _message(response, 'Email verified.');
  }

  /// `POST /api/auth/resend-verification`
  Future<String> resendVerification(String email) async {
    final response = await _apiClient.post(
      ApiEndpoints.resendVerification,
      body: {'email': email},
    );
    return _message(response, 'A new verification email is on its way.');
  }

  /// `POST /api/auth/forgot-password` — emails a 6-digit code.
  Future<String> forgotPassword(String email) async {
    final response = await _apiClient.post(
      ApiEndpoints.forgotPassword,
      body: {'email': email},
    );
    return _message(response, 'Check your inbox for a verification code.');
  }

  /// `POST /api/auth/verify-otp` — returns the one-hour reset token.
  Future<String> verifyOtp({required String email, required String otp}) async {
    final response = await _apiClient.post(
      ApiEndpoints.verifyOtp,
      body: {'email': email, 'otp': otp},
    );
    final token = (response as Map<String, dynamic>?)?['resetToken'];
    if (token is! String || token.isEmpty) {
      throw NetworkException('The server did not return a reset token.');
    }
    return token;
  }

  /// `POST /api/auth/reset-password`
  Future<String> resetPassword({
    required String token,
    required String password,
  }) async {
    final response = await _apiClient.post(
      ApiEndpoints.resetPassword,
      body: {'token': token, 'password': password},
    );
    return _message(response, 'Password updated. You can log in now.');
  }

  /// `GET /api/categories` — public list feeding the registration interests.
  Future<List<WorkCategory>> fetchCategories() async {
    final response = await _apiClient.get(ApiEndpoints.categories);
    final categories = (response as Map<String, dynamic>?)?['categories'];
    if (categories is! List) return const [];
    return [
      for (final item in categories)
        if (item is Map<String, dynamic>) WorkCategory.fromJson(item),
    ];
  }

  String _message(dynamic response, String fallback) {
    if (response is Map<String, dynamic>) {
      final message = response['message'];
      if (message is String && message.trim().isNotEmpty) {
        return message.trim();
      }
    }
    return fallback;
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';

import '../../../core/network/session_store.dart';
import '../../settings/models/account_settings.dart';
import '../../settings/services/account_cache.dart';
import '../../auth/models/user_model.dart';
import '../../auth/repositories/auth_repository.dart';
import '../models/profile_entries.dart';
import '../repositories/profile_repository.dart';
import '../services/profile_cache.dart';

/// Where the profile stands between the device and the backend.
enum ProfileStatus {
  /// No session cookie, or the session the cookie described has ended.
  signedOut,

  /// The session request is in flight.
  loading,

  /// The session answered with a user, so [ProfileController.name] and
  /// friends are real.
  ready,

  /// The request failed: the retry control is shown instead of the data.
  failed,
}

/// The single in-memory profile every screen reads from.
///
/// Identity — name, email and role — comes from the signed-in session, and
/// everything else the backend holds comes from `GET /api/profile`: title,
/// rate, overview, skills, the stored photo, the intro link and all five
/// section tables. The server is the source of truth; the screens show the
/// account's cached document the moment the session lands and the read replaces
/// it as soon as it answers.
///
/// Writes stay through their own routes — `PATCH /api/profile` for the identity
/// and `/api/profile/{section}` for a row — and each answer is applied to the
/// state so a save is reflected without another read. A read that fails for any
/// reason leaves whatever is already on screen alone.
class ProfileController extends ChangeNotifier {
  ProfileController._();

  static final ProfileController instance = ProfileController._();

  final AuthRepository _authRepository = AuthRepository();
  final ProfileRepository _profileRepository = ProfileRepository();
  final ProfileCache _cache = ProfileCache.instance;

  /// The per-account copy of the profile columns no read endpoint reports.
  final AccountCache _accountCache = AccountCache.instance;

  ProfileStatus _status = ProfileStatus.signedOut;
  String _errorMessage = '';
  bool _saving = false;

  /// The profile read in flight, and the account it was read for, so two screens
  /// opened together — or two taps — cost one request instead of several.
  Future<void>? _syncTask;
  String _syncAccount = '';

  /// The account the last session read described, which is the id every cached
  /// document is filed under.
  String userId = '';
  String name = '';
  String email = '';
  String role = '';
  String title = '';
  String location = '';
  String hourlyRate = '';
  String bio = '';

  /// The avatar the backend stores for this account, if the session carries
  /// one. A photo picked on this device temporarily wins over it.
  String? avatarUrl;
  Uint8List? avatarBytes;

  /// Sections of the profile, filled by `GET /api/profile` and kept in step with
  /// the answers of their own write routes. Every row carries the id the server
  /// gave it, which is what lets it be edited or removed later.
  final List<PortfolioItem> portfolio = [];
  final List<String> skills = [];
  final List<Language> languages = [];
  final List<Education> education = [];
  final List<Certification> certifications = [];
  final List<Employment> employment = [];
  final List<WorkHistoryJob> workHistory = [];

  /// The intro link `POST /api/profile/video-intro` stored. The route takes a
  /// URL, never a file, so this is the whole of what the app can show.
  String videoIntro = '';

  /// The columns `PATCH /api/profile` writes from the Settings screen and
  /// `GET /api/profile` never reports. They are the values this account last
  /// saved — visibility, project preference, earning privacy, experience level,
  /// category slugs and the three links.
  ProfileSettings settings = defaultProfileSettings;

  ProfileStatus get status => _status;
  String get errorMessage => _errorMessage;

  /// Only what the session request itself said. A write that was refused
  /// belongs next to the control that triggered it, not under the identity
  /// block, where it would sit beside a retry that cannot fix it.
  String get sessionError =>
      _status == ProfileStatus.failed ? _errorMessage : '';
  bool get isLoading => _status == ProfileStatus.loading;
  bool get isSaving => _saving;
  bool get isSignedIn => _status == ProfileStatus.ready;

  String get initials {
    final parts = name.trim().split(RegExp(r'\s+'));
    if (parts.isEmpty || parts.first.isEmpty) return 'U';
    if (parts.length == 1) return parts.first[0].toUpperCase();
    return (parts[0][0] + parts[parts.length - 1][0]).toUpperCase();
  }

  /// Reads the session the stored cookies describe, once per sign-in. The
  /// request goes through the same auth layer the splash uses, so the cookie
  /// jar and the `[API]` log stay in one place.
  Future<void> load({bool force = false}) async {
    if (_status == ProfileStatus.loading) return;
    if (_status == ProfileStatus.ready && !force) {
      // The identity is already on screen from this session; only the server's
      // copy of the rest can still be newer.
      await _sync();
      return;
    }
    // The splash's session check is what pulls a saved cookie into the jar, so
    // by the time a profile opens there is either a cookie to ask with or a
    // device that has never signed in.
    if (!SessionStore.instance.hasSessionCookie) {
      _wipe();
      _status = ProfileStatus.signedOut;
      notifyListeners();
      return;
    }
    _status = ProfileStatus.loading;
    _errorMessage = '';
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      final user = await _authRepository.currentSession();
      if (user == null) {
        // The existing session-expiration path: no session means no profile,
        // and nothing the previous one described is left on screen.
        _wipe();
        _status = ProfileStatus.signedOut;
      } else {
        // Rows this device was holding for a different account come off before
        // the new session is shown; an unnamed state has nothing to take back.
        if (userId.isNotEmpty && userId != user.id) _wipe();
        final reported = _apply(user);
        _status = ProfileStatus.ready;
        await _restore();
        // A photo the session reported is the only copy any read endpoint has
        // ever given this device, so it is filed under the account at once:
        // the next sign-in's session may report none.
        if (reported) await _persist();
        await _sync();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      _errorMessage = _messageFrom(e);
      _status = ProfileStatus.failed;
    }
    notifyListeners();
  }

  /// Writes the identity block to `PATCH /api/profile`.
  ///
  /// The route replaces the whole profile and insists on a valid `name`, so
  /// the signed-in name and the skill list travel with every save — and so does
  /// [_profileContext], which carries the columns no read reports: leaving them
  /// out of a body would clear them on the server.
  Future<bool> saveIdentity({
    String? name,
    required String title,
    required String hourlyRate,
    required String bio,
  }) async {
    if (_saving) return false;
    if (_status != ProfileStatus.ready) {
      _errorMessage = 'Sign in again to save these changes.';
      notifyListeners();
      return false;
    }
    final submitted = (name ?? this.name).trim();
    if (submitted.length < 2) {
      // The route validates `name` first and answers 400 for the whole body.
      _errorMessage = 'Enter a name with at least 2 characters.';
      notifyListeners();
      return false;
    }
    _saving = true;
    _errorMessage = '';
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      final saved = await _profileRepository.save(
        name: submitted,
        title: title,
        bio: bio,
        hourlyRate: _parseRate(hourlyRate),
        skills: skills,
        context: await _profileContext(),
      );
      // The route's own `{user: …}` answer is the confirmation, so the state
      // follows it rather than the request.
      this.name = saved.name;
      this.title = title;
      this.hourlyRate = hourlyRate;
      this.bio = bio;
      if (saved.avatarUrl.isNotEmpty) avatarUrl = saved.avatarUrl;
      await _persist();
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      _errorMessage = _messageFrom(e);
      return false;
    } finally {
      _saving = false;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  /// Writes the Settings profile block to the same `PATCH /api/profile` route.
  ///
  /// The route replaces the profile, so the identity fields on screen travel
  /// with the settings rather than being left out of the body. Nothing is
  /// applied before the route answers: a save the server refused — for a name it
  /// cannot accept or a value over its limit — keeps the previous settings on
  /// screen and reports its answer.
  Future<bool> saveSettings(ProfileSettings draft) async {
    if (_saving) return false;
    if (_status != ProfileStatus.ready) {
      _errorMessage = 'Sign in again to save these changes.';
      notifyListeners();
      return false;
    }
    final error = _settingsError(draft);
    if (error != null) {
      _errorMessage = error;
      notifyListeners();
      return false;
    }
    _saving = true;
    _errorMessage = '';
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      final context = await _profileContext();
      await _profileRepository.save(
        name: name,
        title: title,
        bio: bio,
        hourlyRate: _parseRate(hourlyRate),
        skills: skills,
        context: (
          phone: context.phone,
          location: context.location,
          settings: draft,
        ),
      );
      settings = draft;
      if (userId.isNotEmpty) await _accountCache.saveSettings(userId, draft);
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      _errorMessage = _messageFrom(e);
      return false;
    } finally {
      _saving = false;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  /// Keeps the edited skill list and asks the backend to store it. `skills` is
  /// a plain text column on the profile, so the list travels through the same
  /// replace-request as the identity. Nothing is requested while signed out.
  Future<void> saveSkills(List<String> replacement) async {
    skills
      ..clear()
      ..addAll(replacement);
    notifyListeners();
    if (_status != ProfileStatus.ready) return;
    await saveIdentity(title: title, hourlyRate: hourlyRate, bio: bio);
  }

  /// Uploads to the avatar route, then shows the stored photo everywhere.
  Future<bool> uploadAvatar(
    Uint8List bytes, {
    String filename = 'avatar.jpg',
    String mimeType = 'image/jpeg',
  }) async {
    if (_status != ProfileStatus.ready) {
      _errorMessage = 'Sign in again to upload a photo.';
      notifyListeners();
      return false;
    }
    try {
      final url = await _profileRepository.uploadAvatar(
        bytes,
        filename: filename,
        mimeType: mimeType,
      );
      avatarBytes = bytes;
      avatarUrl = url.isEmpty ? null : url;
      _errorMessage = '';
      // The uploaded URL is the only copy this device can read back after a
      // sign-in, so it is stored with the account it belongs to.
      await _persist();
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      _errorMessage = _messageFrom(e);
      return false;
    } finally {
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  Future<bool> removeAvatar() async {
    if (_status != ProfileStatus.ready) {
      _errorMessage = 'Sign in again to remove the photo.';
      notifyListeners();
      return false;
    }
    try {
      await _profileRepository.removeAvatar();
      avatarUrl = null;
      avatarBytes = null;
      _errorMessage = '';
      await _persist();
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      _errorMessage = _messageFrom(e);
      return false;
    } finally {
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  // Section rows -------------------------------------------------------------

  Future<bool> saveLanguage(Language row, {int index = -1}) => _writeRow(
    languages,
    index,
    row,
    _profileRepository.saveLanguage,
    'save this language',
  );

  Future<bool> removeLanguage(int index) =>
      _removeRow(languages, index, _profileRepository.removeLanguage);

  Future<bool> saveEducation(Education row, {int index = -1}) => _writeRow(
    education,
    index,
    row,
    _profileRepository.saveEducation,
    'save this education',
  );

  Future<bool> removeEducation(int index) =>
      _removeRow(education, index, _profileRepository.removeEducation);

  Future<bool> saveCertification(Certification row, {int index = -1}) =>
      _writeRow(
        certifications,
        index,
        row,
        _profileRepository.saveCertification,
        'save this certification',
      );

  Future<bool> removeCertification(int index) =>
      _removeRow(certifications, index, _profileRepository.removeCertification);

  Future<bool> saveEmployment(Employment row, {int index = -1}) => _writeRow(
    employment,
    index,
    row,
    _profileRepository.saveEmployment,
    'save this job',
  );

  Future<bool> removeEmployment(int index) =>
      _removeRow(employment, index, _profileRepository.removeEmployment);

  /// Uploads the project's file to the media route, then creates the row that
  /// points at it. A new project joins the grid at the front, where the screen
  /// shows it.
  Future<bool> publishPortfolio(PortfolioDraft draft) async {
    if (!_openWrite('publish this project')) return false;
    try {
      portfolio.insert(0, await _profileRepository.addPortfolio(draft));
      await _persist();
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      _errorMessage = _messageFrom(e);
      return false;
    } finally {
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  Future<bool> saveVideoIntro(String url) async {
    final link = url.trim();
    if (!_openWrite('save the video intro')) return false;
    try {
      videoIntro = await _profileRepository.saveVideoIntro(link);
      await _persist();
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      _errorMessage = _messageFrom(e);
      return false;
    } finally {
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  Future<bool> removeVideoIntro() async {
    if (!_openWrite('remove the video intro')) return false;
    try {
      await _profileRepository.removeVideoIntro();
      videoIntro = '';
      await _persist();
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      _errorMessage = _messageFrom(e);
      return false;
    } finally {
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  /// Sends [row] to its section route and only lists the answer. A row the
  /// server refused — for a date it cannot read, or a field over its limit —
  /// stays off the list, so the app never shows a record that was not stored.
  /// [index] is -1 for a new row, which is appended.
  Future<bool> _writeRow<E>(
    List<E> rows,
    int index,
    E row,
    Future<E> Function(E) request,
    String action,
  ) async {
    if (!_openWrite(action)) return false;
    try {
      final saved = await request(row);
      if (index < 0) {
        rows.add(saved);
      } else {
        rows[index] = saved;
      }
      await _persist();
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      _errorMessage = _messageFrom(e);
      return false;
    } finally {
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  Future<bool> _removeRow<E>(
    List<E> rows,
    int index,
    Future<void> Function(E) request,
  ) async {
    if (index < 0 || index >= rows.length) return false;
    if (!_openWrite('remove this entry')) return false;
    try {
      await request(rows[index]);
      rows.removeAt(index);
      await _persist();
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      _errorMessage = _messageFrom(e);
      return false;
    } finally {
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  /// A section write needs the session the identity writes use: the routes
  /// answer 401 without the cookie, and a nameless row cannot be addressed
  /// again afterwards.
  bool _openWrite(String action) {
    if (_status == ProfileStatus.ready) {
      _errorMessage = '';
      return true;
    }
    _errorMessage = 'Sign in again to $action.';
    notifyListeners();
    return false;
  }

  void update({
    String? name,
    String? email,
    String? title,
    String? location,
    String? hourlyRate,
    String? bio,
  }) {
    if (name != null) this.name = name;
    if (email != null) this.email = email;
    if (title != null) this.title = title;
    if (location != null) this.location = location;
    if (hourlyRate != null) this.hourlyRate = hourlyRate;
    if (bio != null) this.bio = bio;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  /// Passing null restores the session's photo.
  void setAvatar(Uint8List? bytes) {
    avatarBytes = bytes;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  /// Drops everything belonging to this account so the next person to sign in
  /// cannot see it.
  ///
  /// The saved document stays on the device: it is filed under this account's
  /// id, so another account can never read it, and it is what a sign-in shows
  /// before the server's copy arrives — or when there is no network to fetch it
  /// from.
  void clear() {
    _wipe();
    _saving = false;
    _status = ProfileStatus.signedOut;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  /// Takes the account's data out of memory. The id goes with it, so nothing
  /// can be written to the next account's document by accident.
  void _wipe() {
    userId = '';
    name = '';
    email = '';
    role = '';
    title = '';
    location = '';
    hourlyRate = '';
    bio = '';
    avatarUrl = null;
    avatarBytes = null;
    _errorMessage = '';
    portfolio.clear();
    skills.clear();
    languages.clear();
    education.clear();
    certifications.clear();
    employment.clear();
    workHistory.clear();
    videoIntro = '';
    settings = defaultProfileSettings;
  }

  /// Applies the session's identity, answering whether it reported a photo.
  ///
  /// Returns false for an absent or empty `image`: the NextAuth JWT is built at
  /// sign-in and carries no avatar for a credentials login, so an empty field
  /// says nothing about the stored photo — only a URL the server actually sent
  /// may replace it.
  bool _apply(UserModel user) {
    userId = user.id;
    name = user.name;
    email = user.email;
    role = user.role ?? '';
    final photo = user.avatar;
    if (photo == null || photo.isEmpty) return false;
    avatarUrl = photo;
    return true;
  }

  /// Replaces what is on screen with the account's saved profile.
  ///
  /// Nothing but the session cookie scopes the read, so it answers for whoever
  /// is signed in right now, and an answer that arrives after the account moved
  /// on is dropped instead of applied. Any failure — no network, a refused or
  /// expired session, a body that is not a profile — leaves the cached profile
  /// standing: the request has already logged what went wrong.
  Future<void> _sync() async {
    if (userId.isEmpty || _status != ProfileStatus.ready) return;
    final account = userId;
    final pending = _syncTask;
    if (pending != null && _syncAccount == account) return pending;
    final task = _readProfile(account);
    _syncTask = task;
    _syncAccount = account;
    try {
      await task;
    } finally {
      if (identical(_syncTask, task)) {
        _syncTask = null;
        _syncAccount = '';
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _readProfile(String account) async {
    final ProfileSnapshot saved;
    try {
      saved = await _profileRepository.fetchProfile();
    } catch (_) {
      return;
    }
    if (userId != account || _status != ProfileStatus.ready) return;
    _applyServerProfile(saved);
    await _persist();
    notifyListeners();
  }

  /// Makes the server's answer the state, field for field.
  ///
  /// The photo is the one field an absent answer does not clear: a Google
  /// sign-in reports its own picture through the session, and an account that
  /// never uploaded one stores no `avatarUrl` at all.
  void _applyServerProfile(ProfileSnapshot saved) {
    title = saved.title;
    hourlyRate = saved.hourlyRate;
    bio = saved.bio;
    skills
      ..clear()
      ..addAll(saved.skills);
    languages
      ..clear()
      ..addAll(saved.languages);
    education
      ..clear()
      ..addAll(saved.education);
    certifications
      ..clear()
      ..addAll(saved.certifications);
    employment
      ..clear()
      ..addAll(saved.employment);
    portfolio
      ..clear()
      ..addAll(saved.portfolio);
    videoIntro = saved.videoIntro;
    if (saved.avatarUrl.isNotEmpty) avatarUrl = saved.avatarUrl;
  }

  /// Puts back what this device saved for the account the session described, so
  /// a profile is on screen before the read of the server's copy finishes. The
  /// session keeps its authority over name, email and role, and [_sync] replaces
  /// everything else the moment it answers.
  Future<void> _restore() async {
    if (userId.isEmpty) return;
    // The settings columns come from their own document, which an account can
    // have written from the Settings screen without ever touching the profile
    // sections below.
    settings =
        (await _accountCache.restoreContext(userId)).settings ??
        defaultProfileSettings;
    final saved = await _cache.restore(userId);
    // A sign-in that landed on another account must not have the previous
    // one's document applied to it.
    if (saved == null || _status != ProfileStatus.ready || userId.isEmpty) {
      return;
    }
    title = saved.title;
    hourlyRate = saved.hourlyRate;
    bio = saved.bio;
    skills
      ..clear()
      ..addAll(saved.skills);
    languages
      ..clear()
      ..addAll(saved.languages);
    education
      ..clear()
      ..addAll(saved.education);
    certifications
      ..clear()
      ..addAll(saved.certifications);
    employment
      ..clear()
      ..addAll(saved.employment);
    portfolio
      ..clear()
      ..addAll(saved.portfolio);
    videoIntro = saved.videoIntro;
    if ((avatarUrl == null || avatarUrl!.isEmpty) &&
        saved.avatarUrl.isNotEmpty) {
      avatarUrl = saved.avatarUrl;
    }
    notifyListeners();
  }

  /// Keeps the account's document in step with the state the server confirmed.
  Future<void> _persist() async {
    if (userId.isEmpty) return;
    await _cache.save(userId, (
      title: title,
      hourlyRate: hourlyRate,
      bio: bio,
      skills: List<String>.of(skills),
      avatarUrl: avatarUrl ?? '',
      videoIntro: videoIntro,
      languages: List<Language>.of(languages),
      education: List<Education>.of(education),
      certifications: List<Certification>.of(certifications),
      employment: List<Employment>.of(employment),
      portfolio: List<PortfolioItem>.of(portfolio),
    ));
  }

  /// The columns no read reports, for the account on screen and as fresh as the
  /// keystore has them, so a location saved a moment ago travels with the next
  /// write rather than an older copy of it. A part this device never held stays
  /// null, which keeps its columns out of the request rather than sending blanks
  /// over values the account set somewhere else.
  Future<ProfileContext> _profileContext() {
    if (userId.isEmpty) {
      return Future.value((phone: null, location: null, settings: null));
    }
    return _accountCache.restoreContext(userId);
  }

  /// What the route would refuse, said before the request goes out.
  ///
  /// `PATCH /api/profile` answers a rejected body with one generic
  /// "Invalid profile data", so a field over its limit is worth naming here —
  /// and the category rules are the ones the web's own form applies.
  String? _settingsError(ProfileSettings draft) {
    if (name.trim().length < 2) {
      return 'Enter a name with at least 2 characters.';
    }
    if (draft.interestSlugs.isEmpty) {
      return 'Select at least one category.';
    }
    if (draft.interestSlugs.length > categorySelectionLimit) {
      return 'You can pick up to $categorySelectionLimit categories.';
    }
    for (final link in [draft.githubUrl, draft.websiteUrl, draft.youtubeUrl]) {
      if (link.length > 300) {
        return 'A link cannot be longer than 300 characters.';
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  /// The route stores the rate as a number while this field is free text, so
  /// the first amount in the typed value is what gets sent.
  double? _parseRate(String text) =>
      double.tryParse(RegExp(r'\d+(\.\d+)?').firstMatch(text)?.group(0) ?? '');

  String _messageFrom(Object e) => '$e'.replaceFirst('Exception: ', '');
}
