import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';

import '../../../core/network/network_exceptions.dart';
import '../../../core/network/session_store.dart';
import '../../finance/controller/proposals_controller.dart';
import '../../finance/controller/wallet_controller.dart';
import '../../profile/controller/profile_controller.dart';
import '../models/project_model.dart';
import '../services/find_work_service.dart';
import '../services/proposal_attachment_service.dart';

enum FindWorkStatus { idle, loading, ready, empty, error }

/// Whether a proposal reached the backend, in the backend's own words.
typedef BidOutcome = ({bool sent, String message, int spent});

/// The Find Work board, read from `GET /api/tasks`.
///
/// The board is one request: the route has no pagination, so every open
/// project arrives at once and the keyword and category filters are the
/// server's own query parameters. What is left to do on the device is the
/// ordering and the two switches the route does not offer — experience level,
/// payment verification, and the saved list.
///
/// It reads on the first open, on pull-to-refresh, when the app comes back to
/// the foreground, and after a proposal is accepted. Never on a timer.
class FindWorkController extends ChangeNotifier {
  FindWorkController._();

  /// One board for the app: the proposal form has to mark the project it
  /// applied on and reload the list the card came from.
  static final FindWorkController instance = FindWorkController._();

  static const List<String> tabs = ['Most Recent', 'Best Matches', 'Save Jobs'];

  /// `Task.difficulty`, in the backend's own three tokens.
  static const List<String> levelOptions = ['Small', 'Medium', 'Large'];

  /// How long typing pauses before a keyword search is sent, so a whole word
  /// does not cost one request per letter.
  static const Duration searchDelay = Duration(milliseconds: 400);

  final FindWorkService _service = FindWorkService.instance;

  final List<ProjectModel> _projects = [];
  final List<CategoryModel> _categories = [];
  final Set<String> _levels = {};

  /// Bookmarks and applications are device state: the backend serves no saved
  /// list and no list of this account's bids, so these two sets are what let
  /// the flags survive a reload of the board.
  final Set<String> _savedIds = {};
  final Set<String> _appliedIds = {};

  Timer? _debounce;
  bool _fetching = false;
  bool _stale = false;
  bool _categoriesRequested = false;
  bool _loadingCategories = false;
  String _loadedKey = '';
  String _query = '';
  String _categorySlug = '';
  int _tab = 0;
  bool _verifiedOnly = false;
  FindWorkStatus _status = FindWorkStatus.idle;
  String _errorMessage = '';

  /// Bumped by [clear]. A read that belongs to an account the device has
  /// already left must not write its projects into the next one's board, and
  /// must not hand its busy flag over to the read that replaced it.
  int _generation = 0;

  FindWorkStatus get status => _status;
  String get errorMessage => _errorMessage;
  int get tab => _tab;
  String get query => _query;
  Set<String> get levels => _levels;
  bool get verifiedOnly => _verifiedOnly;
  bool get isLoading => _status == FindWorkStatus.loading;
  bool get isRefreshing => _fetching;
  List<CategoryModel> get categories => List.unmodifiable(_categories);
  bool get loadingCategories => _loadingCategories;
  String get categorySlug => _categorySlug;

  bool get hasFilters =>
      _levels.isNotEmpty || _verifiedOnly || _categorySlug.isNotEmpty;

  /// The selected category, or null for the whole board.
  CategoryModel? get category {
    for (final item in _categories) {
      if (item.slug == _categorySlug) return item;
    }
    return null;
  }

  int get savedCount => _savedIds.length;

  /// The projects on screen, after the device-side switches. The server's own
  /// newest-first order is kept.
  List<ProjectModel> get results {
    final matches = _projects.where(_visible).toList();
    return _tab == 1 ? _ranked(matches) : matches;
  }

  bool _visible(ProjectModel project) {
    if (_levels.isNotEmpty && !_levels.contains(project.level)) return false;
    if (_verifiedOnly && !project.paymentVerified) return false;
    if (_tab == 2 && !project.saved) return false;
    return true;
  }

  /// Words from the account's job title, which is what the web's "Matches"
  /// tab compares against. The board already carries the whole open set, so
  /// this comparison runs on the device instead of a second endpoint.
  List<String> get _titleWords {
    final title = ProfileController.instance.title.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (title.isEmpty) return const [];
    return title
        .split(RegExp(r'[\s,/-]+'))
        .where((word) => word.length >= 3)
        .toList();
  }

  bool get _hasTitle => _titleWords.isNotEmpty;

  /// The honest empty-state line for the tab that is on screen.
  String get emptyMessage {
    if (_tab == 1) {
      return _hasTitle
          ? 'No open project matches your profile title.'
          : 'Add your job title in your profile to rank matches.';
    }
    if (_tab == 2) return 'No saved projects yet.';
    if (_query.trim().isNotEmpty || _categorySlug.isNotEmpty) {
      return 'No open project matches this search.';
    }
    return 'No open projects right now.';
  }

  /// Scores each project once. The board can hold every open project at once,
  /// so comparing two of them must not rebuild their text a third time.
  List<ProjectModel> _ranked(List<ProjectModel> projects) {
    final words = _titleWords;
    final scored = <({int hits, ProjectModel project})>[];
    for (final project in projects) {
      final haystack =
          '${project.title} ${project.category} ${project.description}'
              .toLowerCase();
      var hits = 0;
      for (final word in words) {
        if (haystack.contains(word)) hits++;
      }
      if (hits > 0) scored.add((hits: hits, project: project));
    }
    scored.sort((a, b) => b.hits.compareTo(a.hits));
    return [for (final row in scored) row.project];
  }

  /// The keyword, category and order the projects on screen were fetched with.
  String get _key => '${_query.trim().toLowerCase()}|$_categorySlug';

  /// Reads the board. A request already in flight is noted instead of stacked:
  /// when it lands, one more read is made if the filters moved underneath it,
  /// so the fastest typist still ends up with the list for their last word.
  Future<void> load({bool force = false}) async {
    final key = _key;
    if (_fetching) {
      if (key != _loadedKey) _stale = true;
      return;
    }
    if (!force && key == _loadedKey && _status != FindWorkStatus.error) return;

    final generation = _generation;
    _fetching = true;
    if (_projects.isEmpty) {
      _status = FindWorkStatus.loading;
      _errorMessage = '';
      notifyListeners();
    }
    try {
      final fetched = await _service.tasks(
        query: _query,
        categorySlug: _categorySlug,
      );
      if (generation != _generation) return;
      _projects
        ..clear()
        ..addAll([
          for (final project in fetched)
            if (project.id.isNotEmpty) _restore(project),
        ]);
      _loadedKey = key;
      _status = _projects.isEmpty ? FindWorkStatus.empty : FindWorkStatus.ready;
      _errorMessage = '';
    } on NetworkException catch (e) {
      if (generation != _generation) return;
      _errorMessage = e.message;
      // A failed reload leaves the list that is already on screen alone.
      if (_projects.isEmpty) _status = FindWorkStatus.error;
    } catch (e) {
      if (generation != _generation) return;
      _errorMessage = '$e';
      if (_projects.isEmpty) _status = FindWorkStatus.error;
    } finally {
      if (generation == _generation) {
        _fetching = false;
        notifyListeners();
      }
    }
    if (_stale) {
      _stale = false;
      await load();
    }
  }

  /// Pull-to-refresh and the return from a submitted proposal: the board is
  /// read again even if nothing on the device changed. While a read is already
  /// running this waits for that one instead of stacking a second identical
  /// request on the server.
  Future<void> refresh() async {
    if (_fetching) return;
    await load(force: true);
  }

  /// A deeper screen changed one of these projects in place.
  void notify() => notifyListeners();

  ProjectModel _restore(ProjectModel project) {
    project
      ..saved = _savedIds.contains(project.id)
      ..applied = _appliedIds.contains(project.id);
    return project;
  }

  ProjectModel byId(String id) => _projects.firstWhere((p) => p.id == id);

  void setQuery(String value) {
    if (_query == value) return;
    _query = value;
    _debounce?.cancel();
    _debounce = Timer(searchDelay, load);
  }

  /// The search button: skip the wait. An unchanged word is not asked twice.
  Future<void> searchNow() {
    _debounce?.cancel();
    return load();
  }

  void setTab(int index) {
    if (_tab == index) return;
    _tab = index;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void toggleLevel(String level) {
    if (!_levels.add(level)) _levels.remove(level);
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void setVerifiedOnly(bool value) {
    _verifiedOnly = value;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  /// A category is the server's filter, so picking one means reading again.
  void setCategory(String slug) {
    if (_categorySlug == slug) return;
    _categorySlug = slug;
    notifyListeners();
    load();
  }

  /// `GET /api/categories` is read when the filter sheet opens, not at
  /// startup — the board itself does not need it.
  Future<void> loadCategories() async {
    if (_categoriesRequested || _loadingCategories) return;
    final generation = _generation;
    _categoriesRequested = true;
    _loadingCategories = true;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      final rows = await _service.categories();
      if (generation != _generation) return;
      _categories.addAll(rows);
    } on NetworkException catch (e) {
      if (generation != _generation) return;
      // The board still works without the list; the sheet just says so.
      _errorMessage = e.message;
      _categoriesRequested = false;
    } finally {
      if (generation == _generation) {
        _loadingCategories = false;
        notifyListeners();
      }
    }
  }

  void clearFilters() {
    _levels.clear();
    _verifiedOnly = false;
    final filtered = _categorySlug.isNotEmpty;
    _categorySlug = '';
    notifyListeners();
    if (filtered) load();
  }

  void toggleSave(String id) {
    if (!_savedIds.add(id)) _savedIds.remove(id);
    byId(id).saved = _savedIds.contains(id);
    notifyListeners();
  }

  /// Uploads the picked files through the backend's media route, then sends the
  /// proposal. Success is only reported when `POST /api/tasks/{id}/bid` answers
  /// 201 with the row it stored.
  Future<BidOutcome> apply(
    ProjectModel project, {
    required String proposal,
    double? bidAmount,
    List<ProposalAttachment> attachments = const [],
    List<String> portfolioItemIds = const [],
  }) async {
    if (!SessionStore.instance.hasSessionCookie) {
      return (sent: false, message: 'Sign in to send a proposal.', spent: 0);
    }
    _errorMessage = '';
    try {
      final urls = <String>[];
      for (final file in attachments) {
        urls.add(
          await _service.uploadAttachment(
            bytes: file.bytes,
            filename: file.name,
            mimeType: file.mimeType,
          ),
        );
      }
      final receipt = await _service.bid(
        taskId: project.id,
        proposal: proposal,
        bidAmount: bidAmount,
        attachmentUrls: urls,
        portfolioItemIds: portfolioItemIds,
      );
      _appliedIds.add(project.id);
      project.applied = true;
      // The proposal count is the server's, so the board is read again rather
      // than written down by hand.
      unawaited(load(force: true));
      // The bid spent connects and put a row on the proposals list, so both
      // screens read their own routes again rather than being written down —
      // including the wallet, which is the only place the app learns what the
      // account has left to spend.
      unawaited(WalletController.instance.refresh());
      unawaited(ProposalsController.instance.refresh());
      return (
        sent: true,
        message: 'Proposal sent.',
        spent: receipt.connectsSpent,
      );
    } on NetworkException catch (e) {
      return (
        sent: false,
        message: e.statusCode == 401
            ? 'Your session has expired. Please log in again.'
            : e.message,
        spent: 0,
      );
    } catch (e) {
      return (sent: false, message: '$e', spent: 0);
    }
  }

  /// Signs the board out. Bookmarks and application marks are device state, so
  /// the next account on this device must not inherit the previous one's.
  void clear() {
    _debounce?.cancel();
    _generation++;
    _fetching = false;
    _stale = false;
    _projects.clear();
    _categories.clear();
    _levels.clear();
    _savedIds.clear();
    _appliedIds.clear();
    _categoriesRequested = false;
    _loadedKey = '';
    _query = '';
    _categorySlug = '';
    _tab = 0;
    _verifiedOnly = false;
    _status = FindWorkStatus.idle;
    _errorMessage = '';
    notifyListeners();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _debounce?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }
}

/// One open project, shaped by what `GET /api/tasks` actually answers.
///
/// The backend selects a fixed list of columns (`src/lib/task-search.ts`), so
/// this model holds exactly those. Anything the board does not send — a hiring
/// rate, an attachment count, who interviewed whom — is absent here rather than
/// guessed at, because there is no route that would fill it.
class ProjectModel {
  ProjectModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.title,
    required this.description,
    required this.postedAt,
    required this.level,
    required this.category,
    required this.proposals,
    required this.budget,
    required this.duration,
    required this.proposalCost,
    required this.clientName,
    required this.clientLocation,
    this.clientAvatar,
    required this.clientRating,
    this.clientSpent,
    required this.paymentVerified,
    this.saved = false,
    this.applied = false,
  });

  final String id;
  final String title;
  final String description;

  /// The task's `createdAt`. The board is ordered newest first by the server,
  /// so this is also what [postedIn] is measured against.
  final DateTime postedAt;

  /// `difficulty`, in the backend's own words: Small, Medium or Large.
  final String level;

  /// The single category the task belongs to. It is the only skill-shaped value
  /// the board sends, so it is also what the cards tag.
  final String category;

  /// `submittedProposals`, which the server increments on every accepted bid.
  final int proposals;

  /// `earningAmount`.
  final double budget;

  /// The deadline, written the way the web writes it from `deadlineValue`,
  /// `deadlineUnit` and `deadlineHours`.
  final String duration;

  /// `connectsCost` — what applying on this project costs.
  final int proposalCost;

  final String clientName;
  final String clientLocation;
  final String? clientAvatar;

  /// `clientRating`, falling back to the rating on the client account.
  final double clientRating;

  /// `clientTotalSpent`, which the server may leave null.
  final double? clientSpent;

  final bool paymentVerified;

  /// Bookmarked on this device only: the backend has no saved-project route, so
  /// a bookmark is gone once the app is reinstalled and does not follow the
  /// account to another device.
  bool saved;

  /// Bid sent from this device. There is no route that reads this account's bids
  /// back, so the flag is re-supplied by [FindWorkController] after each reload
  /// rather than coming from the server.
  bool applied;

  factory ProjectModel.fromJson(Map<dynamic, dynamic> json) {
    final deadlineValue = _number(json['deadlineValue']);
    final hours = _number(json['deadlineHours']) ?? 0;
    // `formatProjectTimeline`: an explicit value carries its own unit, and
    // without one the deadline is the plain `deadlineHours` count in hours.
    final rawUnit = deadlineValue == null
        ? 'hour'
        : (json['deadlineUnit']?.toString().toLowerCase() ?? 'hour');
    // The web strips a trailing plural before adding its own.
    final unit = rawUnit.endsWith('s')
        ? rawUnit.substring(0, rawUnit.length - 1)
        : rawUnit;
    final value = (deadlineValue ?? hours).round();
    final duration = value == 1 ? '$value $unit' : '$value ${unit}s';
    final rating = _number(json['clientRating']);
    return ProjectModel(
      id: _text(json['id']) ?? '',
      title: _text(json['title']) ?? 'Untitled project',
      description: _text(json['description']) ?? '',
      postedAt:
          DateTime.tryParse(json['createdAt']?.toString() ?? '')?.toUtc() ??
          DateTime.now().toUtc(),
      level: _difficulty(json['difficulty']),
      category: _categoryName(json['category']),
      proposals: _number(json['submittedProposals'])?.round() ?? 0,
      budget: _number(json['earningAmount']) ?? 0,
      duration: duration,
      proposalCost: _number(json['connectsCost'])?.round() ?? 0,
      clientName: _text(json['clientName']) ?? 'WorkingTrust Client',
      clientLocation: _text(json['clientCountry']) ?? 'Location not stated',
      clientAvatar: _text(json['clientAvatarUrl']),
      clientRating: rating ?? _accountRating(json['client']),
      clientSpent: _number(json['clientTotalSpent']),
      paymentVerified: json['paymentVerified'] == true,
    );
  }

  String get price => '\$ ${budget.toStringAsFixed(2)}';

  /// The cards tag skills, and the board's only skill-shaped value is the
  /// project's category.
  List<String> get skills => category.isEmpty ? const [] : [category];

  /// The day the project was posted, in the device's calendar reading of the
  /// server's UTC `createdAt`.
  String get postedDate => '${postedAt.day}/${postedAt.month}/${postedAt.year}';

  /// The tag the cards and the detail header show.
  String get postedIn {
    final age = DateTime.now().toUtc().difference(postedAt);
    if (age.inMinutes < 1) return 'Just posted';
    if (age.inHours < 1) return 'Posted ${age.inMinutes} min ago';
    if (age.inDays < 1) return 'Posted ${age.inHours} hr ago';
    if (age.inDays < 7) return 'Posted ${age.inDays} d ago';
    return 'Posted $postedDate';
  }

  /// The client's lifetime spend, or null when the task carries no figure.
  String? get spentLabel {
    final spent = clientSpent;
    if (spent == null) return null;
    return spent >= 1000
        ? '\$${(spent / 1000).toStringAsFixed(spent >= 10000 ? 0 : 1)}k'
        : '\$${spent.toStringAsFixed(0)}';
  }

  static double? _number(Object? value) =>
      value is num ? value.toDouble() : null;

  static String? _text(Object? value) {
    if (value == null) return null;
    final text = value.toString().trim();
    return text.isEmpty ? null : text;
  }

  /// The board's `difficulty` column is free text with three known tokens, so
  /// an unfamiliar one is shown in the server's own words rather than mapped to
  /// a guess.
  static String _difficulty(Object? value) {
    final raw = _text(value);
    if (raw == null) return '';
    return switch (raw.toUpperCase()) {
      'SMALL' => 'Small',
      'MEDIUM' => 'Medium',
      'LARGE' => 'Large',
      _ => '${raw[0].toUpperCase()}${raw.substring(1).toLowerCase()}',
    };
  }

  static String _categoryName(Object? value) {
    if (value is Map) return _text(value['name']) ?? '';
    return '';
  }

  static double _accountRating(Object? value) {
    if (value is Map && value['rating'] is num) {
      return (value['rating'] as num).toDouble();
    }
    return 0;
  }
}

/// One row of `GET /api/categories`, which the board's category filter is
/// built from. The route selects id, name, slug and description only.
class CategoryModel {
  const CategoryModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.slug,
    this.description = '',
  });

  final String id;
  final String name;
  final String slug;
  final String description;

  factory CategoryModel.fromJson(Map<dynamic, dynamic> json) => CategoryModel(
    id: ProjectModel._text(json['id']) ?? '',
    name: ProjectModel._text(json['name']) ?? 'Category',
    slug: ProjectModel._text(json['slug']) ?? '',
    description: ProjectModel._text(json['description']) ?? '',
  );
}

class _FindWorkScreenState extends State<FindWorkScreen>
    with WidgetsBindingObserver {
  final FindWorkController _controller = FindWorkController.instance;
  final TextEditingController _searchController = TextEditingController();

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addObserver(this);
    // The board is read when the tab is opened and when the app comes back to
    // the foreground — never on a timer.
    _controller.load();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    WidgetsBinding.instance.removeObserver(this);
    _searchController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  /// A project posted while the app was away shows up on the way back in.
  @override

    if (state == AppLifecycleState.resumed) _controller.refresh();

        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(
          top: Radius.circular(AppDimensions.radiusXl),
        ),
      ),

        listenable: _controller,

          child: ConstrainedBox(
            constraints: BoxConstraints(
              maxHeight: MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).height * 0.85,
            ),

                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ListenableBuilder(
      listenable: _controller,
      builder: (context, _) {

/// The board's count and the filters currently applied to it.
class _BoardHeader extends StatelessWidget {

          Padding(

          ),

      ],
    );

/// The area under the filters: one read of the board, in whichever state it is
/// in. Pull-to-refresh and the retry button are the only ways to ask again.
/// It answers with a sliver, so the rows it holds are built as they are
/// scrolled to rather than all when the response lands.
class _Board extends StatelessWidget {
  final FindWorkStatus status;
  final List<ProjectModel> results;

  const _Board({required this.status, required this.results});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final controller = FindWorkController.instance;
    return switch (status) {
      FindWorkStatus.loading => const SliverToBoxAdapter(
        child: Padding(
          padding: EdgeInsets.all(AppDimensions.xl),
          child: AppLoader(),
        ),
      ),
      FindWorkStatus.error => SliverToBoxAdapter(
        child: AppErrorWidget(
          message: controller.errorMessage,
          onRetry: controller.refresh,
        ),
      ),
      FindWorkStatus.empty || FindWorkStatus.idle || FindWorkStatus.ready =>
        results.isEmpty
            ? SliverToBoxAdapter(
                child: _NoResults(message: controller.emptyMessage),
              )
            : _ProjectRows(
                projects: results,
                onOpen: (project) => _open(context, project),
                onSave: (project) => controller.toggleSave(project.id),
              ),
    };
  }

  Future<void> _open(BuildContext context, ProjectModel project) async {
    await Navigator.pushNamed(
      context,
      RouteNames.projectDetail,
      arguments: project,
    );
    // A proposal sent deeper in the flow reloads the board itself; a bookmark
    // made there only needs this list to repaint.
    FindWorkController.instance.notify();
  }
}

/// The projects in rows of one, two or three columns, built a row at a time.
class _ProjectRows extends StatelessWidget {
  final List<ProjectModel> projects;
  final ValueChanged<ProjectModel> onOpen;
  final ValueChanged<ProjectModel> onSave;

  const _ProjectRows({
    required this.projects,
    required this.onOpen,
    required this.onSave,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    // The scroll view is padded by md on each side, which is the width the old
    // Column was laid out in and therefore what decides the column count.
    final width = MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).width - AppDimensions.md * 2;
    final columns = width >= 1100 ? 3 : (width >= 640 ? 2 : 1);
    return SliverList.builder(
      itemCount: (projects.length / columns).ceil(),
      itemBuilder: (context, row) => _ProjectRow(
        projects: projects,
        start: row * columns,
        columns: columns,
        onOpen: onOpen,
        onSave: onSave,
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _ProjectRow extends StatelessWidget {
  final List<ProjectModel> projects;
  final int start;
  final int columns;
  final ValueChanged<ProjectModel> onOpen;
  final ValueChanged<ProjectModel> onSave;

  const _ProjectRow({
    required this.projects,
    required this.start,
    required this.columns,
    required this.onOpen,
    required this.onSave,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final slice = projects.skip(start).take(columns).toList();
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: AppDimensions.md),
      child: IntrinsicHeight(
        child: Row(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            for (int i = 0; i < columns; i++)
              Expanded(
                child: Padding(
                  padding: EdgeInsets.only(left: i == 0 ? 0 : AppDimensions.md),
                  child: i < slice.length
                      ? ProjectCard(
                          project: slice[i],
                          onTap: () => onOpen(slice[i]),
                          onSave: () => onSave(slice[i]),
                        )
                      : const SizedBox(),
                ),
              ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _SearchRow extends StatelessWidget {
  final TextEditingController controller;
  final ValueChanged<String> onChanged;
  final Future<void> Function() onSearch;

  const _SearchRow({
    required this.controller,
    required this.onChanged,
    required this.onSearch,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final field = AppTextField(
      controller: controller,
      hint: 'Search Projects here...',
      borderRadius: 30,
      onChanged: onChanged,
      prefixIcon: const Icon(
        Icons.search_rounded,
        size: 20,
        color: AppColors.textHint,
      ),
    );
    final button = AppButton(
      text: 'Search Projects',
      width: 170,
      borderRadius: 30,
      backgroundColor: AppColors.accent,
      textColor: AppColors.primary,
      onPressed: () {
        FocusScope.of(context).unfocus();
        onSearch();
      },
    );

    return LayoutBuilder(
      builder: (context, constraints) {
        if (constraints.maxWidth < 480) {
          return Column(
            children: [
              field,
              const SizedBox(height: AppDimensions.sm),
              Align(
                alignment: Alignment.centerRight,
                child: ConstrainedBox(
                  constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 170),
                  child: button,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          );
        }
        return Row(
          children: [
            Expanded(child: field),
            const SizedBox(width: AppDimensions.md),
            button,
          ],
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

class _FilterRow extends StatelessWidget {
  final FindWorkController controller;
  final VoidCallback onFilters;

  const _FilterRow({required this.controller, required this.onFilters});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
      children: [
        // A segmented pill bar reads as one control with three choices, which
        // the underline tabs did not.
        Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimensions.xs + 2),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: AppColors.surfaceVariant,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimensions.radiusXl),
            border: Border.all(color: AppColors.border),
          ),
          child: Row(
            children: [
              for (
                int index = 0;
                index < FindWorkController.tabs.length;
                index++
              ) ...[
                if (index > 0) const SizedBox(width: AppDimensions.xs),
                Expanded(
                  child: _PillTab(
                    label: index == 2
                        ? 'Save Jobs (${controller.savedCount})'
                        : FindWorkController.tabs[index],
                    active: controller.tab == index,
                    onTap: () => controller.setTab(index),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ],
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: AppDimensions.md),
        Row(
          children: [
            AppTag(
              'Filters',
              icon: Icons.tune,
              selected: controller.hasFilters,
              onTap: onFilters,
            ),
            const Spacer(),
            if (controller.hasFilters)
              TextButton(
                onPressed: controller.clearFilters,
                child: const Text('Clear filters'),
              ),
          ],
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

/// The bid balance, on the hero panel where a proposal is about to spend it.
///
/// The number is the connects `GET /api/wallet` counted for this account. The
/// board itself has no connects column, so this chip asks that route when the
/// panel opens and shows a dash until it answers.
class _BidsChip extends StatefulWidget {
  const _BidsChip();

  @override
  State<_BidsChip> createState() => _BidsChipState();
}

class _BidsChipState extends State<_BidsChip> {
  final WalletController _wallet = WalletController.instance;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      if (mounted) _wallet.load();
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ListenableBuilder(
      listenable: _wallet,
      builder: (context, _) => Material(
        color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.16),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimensions.radiusXl),
        child: InkWell(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimensions.radiusXl),
          onTap: () => showBuyBidsSheet(context),
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
              horizontal: AppDimensions.md,
              vertical: AppDimensions.sm,
            ),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  'Bids: ${_wallet.value.bidsLabel}',
                  style: AppTextStyles.labelLarge.copyWith(
                    color: AppColors.textOnPrimary,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 2),
                Row(
                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                  children: [

                      'Buy Bids',

                        fontSize: 12,

                        color: AppColors.accent,

                    const SizedBox(width: AppDimensions.xs),
                    const Icon(
                      Icons.arrow_outward,

                      color: AppColors.accent,

          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _PillTab extends StatelessWidget {
  final String label;
  final bool active;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  const _PillTab({
    required this.label,
    required this.active,
    required this.onTap,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Material(
      color: active ? AppColors.surface : Colors.transparent,
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimensions.radiusXl),
      child: InkWell(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimensions.radiusXl),
        onTap: onTap,
        child: Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
            horizontal: AppDimensions.sm,
            vertical: AppDimensions.sm + 2,
          ),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimensions.radiusXl),
            border: Border.all(
              color: active ? AppColors.primary : Colors.transparent,
            ),
          ),
          // Three labels share one row, and the longest of them is wider than a
          // third of a narrow phone, so the text gives up size rather than
          // pushing the row out of the card.
          child: FittedBox(
            fit: BoxFit.scaleDown,
            child: Text(
              label,
              maxLines: 1,
              style: AppTextStyles.bodyMedium.copyWith(
                fontWeight: active ? FontWeight.w700 : FontWeight.w500,
                color: active ? AppColors.primary : AppColors.textSecondary,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

            ),

              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              style: AppTextStyles.bodySmall,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../../app/routes/route_names.dart';
import '../../../app/theme/app_colors.dart';
import '../../../app/theme/app_dimensions.dart';
import '../../../app/theme/app_text_styles.dart';

import '../../../core/widgets/app_button.dart';
import '../../../core/widgets/app_card.dart';
import '../../../core/widgets/app_tag.dart';
import '../../../core/widgets/icon_badge.dart';
import '../controller/find_work_controller.dart';
import '../models/project_model.dart';

class ProjectDetailScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final ProjectModel project;

  const ProjectDetailScreen({super.key, required this.project});

  @override
  State<ProjectDetailScreen> createState() => _ProjectDetailScreenState();
}

class _ProjectDetailScreenState extends State<ProjectDetailScreen> {
  /// The proposal form owns the submission; this screen only reflects the
  /// result once the form pops back.
  Future<void> _apply() async {
    final messenger = ScaffoldMessenger.of(context);
    final cost = widget.project.proposalCost;
    final submitted = await Navigator.pushNamed(
      context,
      RouteNames.submitProposal,
      arguments: widget.project,
    );
    if (submitted != true || !mounted) return;
    setState(() {});
    messenger.showSnackBar(
      SnackBar(
        content: Text('Proposal sent. $cost Bids deducted.'),
        behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
      ),
    );
  }

  void _toggleSave() {
    // The board owns the bookmark set, so the list's count stays correct.
    FindWorkController.instance.toggleSave(widget.project.id);
    setState(() {});
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final project = widget.project;
    final mainChildren = <Widget>[
      _Overview(project: project),
      const SizedBox(height: AppDimensions.md),
      _Expertise(project: project),
      const SizedBox(height: AppDimensions.md),
      _Activity(project: project),
    ];
    final sideChildren = <Widget>[
      _ApplyCard(project: project, onApply: _apply),
      const SizedBox(height: AppDimensions.md),
      _ClientCard(project: project),
    ];

    Widget scroll(List<Widget> children) => RefreshIndicator(
      onRefresh: () => FindWorkController.instance.load(force: true),
      child: SingleChildScrollView(
        physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimensions.md),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: children,
        ),
      ),
    );

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppColors.background,
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: Colors.white,
        foregroundColor: AppColors.textPrimary,
        elevation: 0,
        title: const Text('Project Details'),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            onPressed: _toggleSave,
            icon: Icon(
              project.saved
                  ? Icons.bookmark_added
                  : Icons.bookmark_add_outlined,
              color: project.saved
                  ? AppColors.primary
                  : AppColors.textSecondary,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: SafeArea(
        child: LayoutBuilder(
          builder: (context, constraints) {
            if (constraints.maxWidth < 900) {
              return scroll([...mainChildren, ...sideChildren]);
            }
            return Row(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Expanded(flex: 3, child: scroll(mainChildren)),
                SizedBox(width: 360, child: scroll(sideChildren)),
              ],
            );
          },
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _Overview extends StatelessWidget {
  final ProjectModel project;
  const _Overview({required this.project});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AppCard(
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          AppTag(project.postedIn, highlight: true),
          const SizedBox(height: AppDimensions.md),
          Text(
            project.title,
            style: AppTextStyles.heading2.copyWith(
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
              height: 1.25,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AppDimensions.lg),
          Text(
            'Summary',
            style: AppTextStyles.heading4.copyWith(color: AppColors.primary),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AppDimensions.sm),
          // The board sends one description; the web splits its `requirements`
          // and `deliverables` columns out, and neither is in this response.
          for (final paragraph in project.description.split('\n'))
            if (paragraph.trim().isNotEmpty)
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: AppDimensions.md),
                child: Text(
                  paragraph.trim(),
                  style: AppTextStyles.bodyMedium.copyWith(
                    color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                    height: 1.7,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
          const SizedBox(height: AppDimensions.md),
          Wrap(
            spacing: AppDimensions.lg,
            runSpacing: AppDimensions.md,
            children: [
              _Meta(icon: Icons.attach_money_outlined, label: project.price),
              if (project.level.isNotEmpty)
                _Meta(
                  icon: Icons.workspace_premium_outlined,
                  label: project.level,
                ),
              _Meta(icon: Icons.schedule_outlined, label: project.duration),
              if (project.category.isNotEmpty)
                _Meta(icon: Icons.folder_outlined, label: project.category),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';

import '../../../app/theme/app_colors.dart';
import '../../../app/theme/app_dimensions.dart';
import '../../../app/theme/app_text_styles.dart';
import '../../../core/widgets/app_button.dart';
import '../../../core/widgets/app_card.dart';
import '../../../core/widgets/app_tag.dart';
import '../../../core/widgets/app_text_field.dart';
import '../../../core/widgets/icon_badge.dart';
import '../../finance/controller/wallet_controller.dart';
import '../../profile/controller/profile_controller.dart';
import '../../profile/models/profile_entries.dart';
import '../../profile/widgets/buy_bids_sheet.dart';
import '../controller/find_work_controller.dart';
import '../models/project_model.dart';
import '../services/find_work_service.dart';
import '../services/proposal_attachment_service.dart';

const double _serviceFee = 0.20;
const int _maxAttachments = FindWorkService.attachmentMax;
const int _maxPortfolio = FindWorkService.portfolioMax;
const List<String> _fontNames = ['Default', 'Serif', 'Monospace'];
const List<String?> _fontFamilies = [null, 'serif', 'monospace'];

String _money(double value) => '\$${value.toStringAsFixed(2)}';

/// The proposal form a freelancer fills before a job application goes out.
/// Opening it never marks the job as applied; only an accepted bid does, and
/// that is decided by `POST /api/tasks/{id}/bid`.
class SubmitProposalScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final ProjectModel project;

  const SubmitProposalScreen({super.key, required this.project});

  @override
  State<SubmitProposalScreen> createState() => _SubmitProposalScreenState();
}

class _SubmitProposalScreenState extends State<SubmitProposalScreen> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _cover = TextEditingController();
  late final TextEditingController _rate;
  final List<ProposalAttachment> _attachments = [];
  final List<String> _portfolio = [];
  int _font = 0;
  bool _submitting = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _rate = TextEditingController(text: _profileRate.toStringAsFixed(2));
    // The card under the form says what this proposal leaves on the account,
    // and only the wallet route knows what was on it before.
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      if (mounted) WalletController.instance.load();
    });
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _rate.dispose();
    _cover.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  /// The seeded profile rate pre-fills the bid so the form is never empty.
  double get _profileRate {
    final match = RegExp(r'\d+(\.\d+)?')
        .firstMatch(ProfileController.instance.hourlyRate);
    final rate = double.tryParse(match?.group(0) ?? '') ?? 0;
    // The backend refuses a rate under its minimum, so the form never starts
    // below it.
    return rate < FindWorkService.amountMin ? FindWorkService.amountMin : rate;
  }

  double get _bidRate => double.tryParse(_rate.text.trim()) ?? 0;

  int? get _remaining {
    final held = WalletController.instance.value.connects;
    return held == null ? null : held - widget.project.proposalCost;
  }

  /// Uploads what the form holds and asks the backend for the bid. The success
  /// message only appears when the route answers 201.
  Future<void> _submit() async {
    FocusScope.of(context).unfocus();
    if (_submitting || !(_formKey.currentState?.validate() ?? false)) return;
    final messenger = ScaffoldMessenger.of(context);
    final controller = FindWorkController.instance;
    setState(() => _submitting = true);
    final outcome = await controller.apply(
      widget.project,
      proposal: _cover.text,
      bidAmount: _bidRate,
      attachments: List<ProposalAttachment>.of(_attachments),
      portfolioItemIds: List<String>.of(_portfolio),
    );
    if (!mounted) return;
    setState(() => _submitting = false);
    if (!outcome.sent) {
      messenger.showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text(outcome.message),
          behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
          action: outcome.message.contains('connects')
              ? SnackBarAction(
                  label: 'Buy Bids',
                  onPressed: () => showBuyBidsSheet(context),
                )
              : null,
        ),
      );
      return;
    }
    Navigator.pop(context, true);
  }

  Future<void> _attachFiles() async {
    final messenger = ScaffoldMessenger.of(context);
    final slots = _maxAttachments - _attachments.length;
    if (slots <= 0) {
      messenger.showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(
          content: Text('A proposal can carry up to $_maxAttachments files.'),
          behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
        ),
      );
      return;
    }
    try {
      final picked = await ProposalAttachmentService.instance.pick(
        slots: slots,
      );
      if (picked.isEmpty) return;
      setState(() => _attachments.addAll(picked));
    } catch (_) {
      messenger.showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(
          content: Text('This device could not open the file picker.'),
          behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  Future<void> _pickPortfolio() async {
    final selected = await showModalBottomSheet<List<String>>(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(
          top: Radius.circular(AppDimensions.radiusLg),
        ),
      ),
      builder: (context) =>
          _PortfolioSheet(initial: _portfolio, max: _maxPortfolio),
    );
    if (selected == null) return;
    setState(() {
      _portfolio
        ..clear()
        ..addAll(selected);
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final project = widget.project;
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppColors.background,
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: Colors.white,
        foregroundColor: AppColors.textPrimary,
        elevation: 0,
        title: const Text('Submit a proposal'),
      ),
      body: SafeArea(
        child: Form(
          key: _formKey,
          child: ListView(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimensions.md),
            children: [
              Center(
                child: ConstrainedBox(
                  constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 720),
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                    children: [
                      _JobDetailsCard(
                        project: project,
                        onViewJob: () => Navigator.pop(context),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: AppDimensions.md),
                      ListenableBuilder(
                        listenable: WalletController.instance,
                        builder: (context, _) => _ProposalDetailsCard(
                          project: project,
                          remaining: _remaining,
                          rate: _rate,
                          profileRate: _profileRate,
                          receive: '${_money(_bidRate * (1 - _serviceFee))}/hr',
                          onRateChanged: () => setState(() {}),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: AppDimensions.md),
                      _AdditionalDetailsCard(
                        cover: _cover,
                        attachments: _attachments,
                        font: _font,
                        onFont: (index) => setState(() => _font = index),
                        onAttach: _attachFiles,
                        onRemove: (index) =>
                            setState(() => _attachments.removeAt(index)),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: AppDimensions.md),
                      _PortfolioCard(
                        selected: _portfolio,
                        onAdd: _pickPortfolio,
                        onRemove: (id) => setState(() => _portfolio.remove(id)),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: AppDimensions.lg),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
      bottomNavigationBar: SafeArea(
        top: false,
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimensions.md),
          // A Column that shrink-wraps: Center would make the footer fill the
          // body height and squeeze the scrollable area to zero.
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              ConstrainedBox(
                constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 720),
                child: AppButton(
                  text: _submitting ? 'Sending proposal...' : 'Submit Proposal',
                  backgroundColor: AppColors.primary,
                  textColor: Colors.white,
                  borderRadius: AppDimensions.radiusXl,
                  onPressed: _submitting ? null : _submit,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _JobDetailsCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final ProjectModel project;
  final VoidCallback onViewJob;

  const _JobDetailsCard({required this.project, required this.onViewJob});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AppCard(
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              const Expanded(
                child: Text('Job details', style: AppTextStyles.heading4),
              ),
              TextButton.icon(
                onPressed: onViewJob,
                style: TextButton.styleFrom(
                  foregroundColor: AppColors.primary,

                    horizontal: AppDimensions.sm,
                  ),
                  minimumSize: Size.zero,
                  tapTargetSize: MaterialTapTargetSize.shrinkWrap,
                ),
                icon: const Icon(Icons.open_in_new, size: AppDimensions.iconSm),
                label: const Text('View job', style: AppTextStyles.labelMedium),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          Text(
            project.title,
            style: AppTextStyles.bodyLarge.copyWith(
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
              height: 1.4,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AppDimensions.xs + 2),
          Text(
            project.description,
            maxLines: 3,
            overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
            style: AppTextStyles.bodySmall.copyWith(height: 1.6),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AppDimensions.md),
          LayoutBuilder(
            builder: (context, constraints) {
              final cells = <Widget>[
                _MetaCell(
                  icon: Icons.workspace_premium_outlined,
                  label: 'Level',
                  value: project.level,
                ),
                _MetaCell(
                  icon: Icons.schedule_outlined,
                  label: 'Duration',
                  value: project.duration,
                ),
                _MetaCell(
                  icon: Icons.attach_money_outlined,
                  label: 'Budget',
                  value: project.price,
                ),
              ];
              if (constraints.maxWidth < 420) {
                return Wrap(
                  spacing: AppDimensions.sm,
                  runSpacing: AppDimensions.sm,
                  children: [
                    for (final cell in cells) SizedBox(width: 150, child: cell),
                  ],
                );
              }
              return Row(
                children: [
                  for (int index = 0; index < cells.length; index++) ...[
                    if (index > 0) const SizedBox(width: AppDimensions.sm),
                    Expanded(child: cells[index]),
                  ],
                ],
              );
            },
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

}

class _ProposalDetailsCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final ProjectModel project;
  final int? remaining;
  final TextEditingController rate;
  final double profileRate;
  final String receive;
  final VoidCallback onRateChanged;

  const _ProposalDetailsCard({
    required this.project,
    required this.remaining,
    required this.rate,
    required this.profileRate,
    required this.receive,
    required this.onRateChanged,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AppCard(
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          const Text('Proposal Details', style: AppTextStyles.heading4),
          const SizedBox(height: AppDimensions.md),
          Text.rich(
            TextSpan(
              text: 'This proposal requires:  ',
              style: AppTextStyles.bodyMedium.copyWith(
                color: AppColors.textSecondary,
              ),
              children: [
                TextSpan(
                  text: '${project.proposalCost} Bids',
                  style: AppTextStyles.labelLarge,
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AppDimensions.xs + 2),
          Text(
            switch (remaining) {
              null => 'The wallet has not reported your Bids yet.',
              final left when left < 0 =>
                'You need ${-left} more Bids to send this proposal.',
              final left =>
                "When you submit this proposal, you'll have $left Bids remaining",
            },
            style: AppTextStyles.bodySmall.copyWith(
              color: (remaining ?? 0) < 0
                  ? AppColors.error
                  : AppColors.textSecondary,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AppDimensions.lg),
          Row(
            children: [
              const Expanded(
                child: Text(
                  "What is the rate you'd like to bid for",
                  style: AppTextStyles.labelLarge,
                ),
              ),
              AppTag(
                'Service fee ${(_serviceFee * 100).round()}%',
                highlight: true,
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AppDimensions.xs),
          Text(
            'Your Profile rate is ${_money(profileRate)}/hr',
            style: AppTextStyles.caption,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AppDimensions.md),
          LayoutBuilder(
            builder: (context, constraints) {
              final fields = <Widget>[
                _RateField(
                  label: 'Hourly Rate',
                  child: AppTextField(
                    controller: rate,
                    hint: '0.00',
                    suffixText: '/hr',
                    keyboardType: const TextInputType.numberWithOptions(
                      decimal: true,
                    ),
                    inputFormatters: [
                      FilteringTextInputFormatter.allow(
                        RegExp(r'^\d*\.?\d{0,2}'),
                      ),
                    ],
                    textInputAction: TextInputAction.done,
                    validator: _requiredRate,
                    onChanged: (_) => onRateChanged(),
                  ),
                ),
                _RateField(
                  label: "You'll Receive",
                  child: Container(
                    height: AppDimensions.textFieldHeight,
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                      horizontal: AppDimensions.md,
                    ),
                    alignment: Alignment.centerRight,
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: AppColors.surfaceVariant,
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimensions.radius),
                      border: Border.all(color: AppColors.border),
                    ),
                    child: Text(receive, style: AppTextStyles.labelLarge),
                  ),
                ),
              ];
              if (constraints.maxWidth < 380) {
                return Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                  children: [
                    for (final field in fields)
                      Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.only(
                          bottom: AppDimensions.md,
                        ),
                        child: field,
                      ),
                  ],
                );
              }
              return Row(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Expanded(child: fields[0]),
                  const SizedBox(width: AppDimensions.md),
                  Expanded(child: fields[1]),
                ],
              );
            },
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

String? _requiredRate(String? value) {
  final rate = double.tryParse((value ?? '').trim());
  if (rate == null || rate <= 0) return 'Enter the rate you want to bid';
  if (rate < FindWorkService.amountMin || rate > FindWorkService.amountMax) {
    return 'The bid rate must be between \$${FindWorkService.amountMin.toInt()} '
        'and \$${FindWorkService.amountMax.toInt()}';
  }
  return null;
}

String? _requiredProposal(String? value) {
  final text = (value ?? '').trim();
  if (text.length < FindWorkService.proposalMin) {
    return 'Write a clear proposal (min '
        '${FindWorkService.proposalMin} characters).';
  }
  if (text.length > FindWorkService.proposalMax) {
    return 'A proposal can be up to ${FindWorkService.proposalMax} characters.';
  }
  return null;
}

class _RateField extends StatelessWidget {

    required this.onAdd,

  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    // Only rows this device published have a backend id, and the id is what a
    // bid stores, so the picker cannot offer the profile's sample content.
    final items = ProfileController.instance.portfolio;
    final chosen = [
      for (final item in items)
        if (selected.contains(item.id)) item,
    ];
    return AppCard(
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          const Text('Portfolio Highlight', style: AppTextStyles.heading4),
          const SizedBox(height: AppDimensions.md),
          if (items.isEmpty)
            const Text(
              'Publish a project in your profile to highlight one here.',
              style: AppTextStyles.caption,
            ),
          Wrap(
            spacing: AppDimensions.md,
            runSpacing: AppDimensions.md,
            children: [
              for (final item in chosen)
                _PortfolioThumb(item: item, onRemove: () => onRemove(item.id)),
              if (chosen.length < _maxPortfolio && items.isNotEmpty)
                _AddPortfolio(
                  onAdd: onAdd,
                  remaining: _maxPortfolio - chosen.length,
                ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _PortfolioSheet extends StatefulWidget {
  final List<String> initial;
  final int max;

  const _PortfolioSheet({required this.initial, required this.max});

  @override
  State<_PortfolioSheet> createState() => _PortfolioSheetState();
}

class _PortfolioSheetState extends State<_PortfolioSheet> {
  late final List<String> _picked = [...widget.initial];

  void _toggle(String id) {
    setState(() {
      if (_picked.contains(id)) {
        _picked.remove(id);
      } else if (_picked.length < widget.max) {
        _picked.add(id);
      }
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final items = ProfileController.instance.portfolio;
    return SafeArea(
      child: ConstrainedBox(
        constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxHeight: 520),
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimensions.md),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Text('Portfolio projects', style: AppTextStyles.heading4),
              Text(
                'Choose up to ${widget.max} projects to highlight.',
                style: AppTextStyles.caption,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: AppDimensions.md),
              Flexible(
                child: ListView.builder(
                  shrinkWrap: true,
                  itemCount: items.length,
                  itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                    final item = items[index];
                    final checked = _picked.contains(item.id);
                    final blocked = !checked && _picked.length >= widget.max;
                    return CheckboxListTile(
                      value: checked,
                      controlAffinity: ListTileControlAffinity.leading,
                      activeColor: AppColors.primary,
                      contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                      onChanged: blocked ? null : (_) => _toggle(item.id),
                      title: Text(item.title, style: AppTextStyles.bodyMedium),
                    );
                  },
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: AppDimensions.sm),
              AppButton(
                text: 'Done',
                borderRadius: AppDimensions.radiusXl,
                onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, _picked),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

SOURCE FILE: D:\WorkingTrust\lib\features\find_work\services\find_work_service.dart
SHA256: BE420EF321D095DFDAACD5F1C031D018AAEDE416EDA6BE726EB8347B13EEA540

import '../../../core/network/api_client.dart';
import '../../../core/network/api_endpoints.dart';
import '../../../core/network/network_exceptions.dart';
import '../models/project_model.dart';

/// What a submitted proposal costs, as the server's own `Bid` row reports it.
typedef BidReceipt = ({String id, int connectsSpent});

/// The three Find Work routes the backend actually serves.
///
/// Everything else the board used to show — a project fetched by id, saved
/// projects, this account's own bids — has no route, so it has no method here.
class FindWorkService {
  FindWorkService._();

  static final FindWorkService instance = FindWorkService._();

  final ApiClient _apiClient = ApiClient();

  /// `bidSchema`'s ceilings, applied before the request so a proposal that
  /// cannot be stored never leaves the device.
  static const int proposalMin = 20;
  static const int proposalMax = 3000;
  static const double amountMin = 5;
  static const double amountMax = 10000;
  static const int attachmentMax = 5;
  static const int portfolioMax = 3;

  /// `GET /api/tasks` — every open project, newest first, optionally narrowed
  /// to one category slug and one keyword. The server matches [query] against
  /// the title, description, requirements, deliverables, client name and
  /// category name.
  Future<List<ProjectModel>> tasks({
    String query = '',
    String categorySlug = '',
  }) async {
    final response = await _apiClient.get(
      ApiEndpoints.tasks,
      queryParameters: {
        if (query.trim().isNotEmpty) 'q': query.trim(),
        if (categorySlug.isNotEmpty) 'category': categorySlug,
      },
    );
    final rows = response is Map ? response['tasks'] : null;
    if (rows is! List) {
      throw NetworkException('The server did not return any projects.');
    }
    return [
      for (final row in rows)
        if (row is Map) ProjectModel.fromJson(row),
    ];
  }

  /// `GET /api/categories` — the board's filter list. It carries no task
  /// counts, because the route does not send any.
  Future<List<CategoryModel>> categories() async {
    final response = await _apiClient.get(ApiEndpoints.categories);
    final rows = response is Map ? response['categories'] : null;
    if (rows is! List) {
      throw NetworkException('The server did not return any categories.');
    }
    return [
      for (final row in rows)
        if (row is Map) CategoryModel.fromJson(row),
    ];
  }

  /// `POST /api/tasks/{id}/bid`. The bid is only listed as sent once this
  /// answers 201 with the row the server stored.
  Future<BidReceipt> bid({
    required String taskId,
    required String proposal,
    double? bidAmount,
    List<String> attachmentUrls = const [],
    List<String> portfolioItemIds = const [],
  }) async {
    final text = proposal.trim();
    if (text.length < proposalMin) {
      throw NetworkException(
        'Write a clear proposal (min $proposalMin characters).',
      );
    }
    if (text.length > proposalMax) {
      throw NetworkException(
        'A proposal can be up to $proposalMax characters.',
      );
    }
    if (bidAmount != null && (bidAmount < amountMin || bidAmount > amountMax)) {
      throw NetworkException(
        'The bid rate must be between \$${amountMin.toInt()} and '
        '\$${amountMax.toInt()}.',
      );
    }
    if (attachmentUrls.length > attachmentMax) {
      throw NetworkException(
        'A proposal can carry up to $attachmentMax files.',
      );
    }
    if (portfolioItemIds.length > portfolioMax) {
      throw NetworkException(
        'A proposal can highlight up to $portfolioMax projects.',
      );
    }

    final response = await _apiClient.post(
      ApiEndpoints.taskBid(taskId),
      body: <String, dynamic>{
        'proposal': text,
        // The route turns an absent or empty amount into a null bidAmount, so
        // nothing is sent when the freelancer left the rate out.
        'bidAmount': ?bidAmount,
        'attachmentUrls': attachmentUrls.isEmpty ? null : attachmentUrls,
        'portfolioItemIds': portfolioItemIds.isEmpty ? null : portfolioItemIds,
      },
    );
    final row = response is Map ? response['bid'] : null;
    if (row is! Map) {
      throw NetworkException('The server did not confirm your proposal.');
    }
    final spent = row['connectsSpent'];
    return (
      id: row['id']?.toString() ?? '',
      connectsSpent: spent is num ? spent.round() : 0,
    );
  }

  /// Uploads one proposal file through the backend's existing media route and
  /// answers with the URL the bid request stores. It is the only upload the
  /// deployed backend offers a device, so a proposal's attachments have to go
  /// through it.
  Future<String> uploadAttachment({
    required List<int> bytes,
    required String filename,
    required String mimeType,
  }) async {
    final response = await _apiClient.postFile(
      ApiEndpoints.profilePortfolioMedia,
      field: 'file',
      bytes: bytes,
      filename: filename,
      mimeType: mimeType,
    );
    if (!response.isSuccess) {
      throw NetworkException(response.message, statusCode: response.statusCode);
    }
    final url = response.json?['url'];
    if (url is! String || url.isEmpty) {
      throw NetworkException('The server did not store that file.');
    }
    return url;
  }
}

SOURCE FILE: D:\WorkingTrust\lib\features\finance\controller\proposals_controller.dart
SHA256: AEF1AF29DD1096BE9D779E9B814E72F7E67B7A6ED41D02731665532117055167

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';

import '../../../core/network/network_exceptions.dart';
import '../../../core/network/session_store.dart';
import '../models/my_proposals.dart';
import '../services/proposals_service.dart';
import 'read_state.dart';

/// The proposals this account sent, one server page at a time.
///
/// The bids list is paged because the route pages it; `activeWork` and `counts`
/// come back whole with every answer, so a tab header shows the real number of
/// bids in each status rather than the count of the page currently open.
class ProposalsController extends ChangeNotifier {
  ProposalsController._();

  static final ProposalsController instance = ProposalsController._();

  static const int pageSize = 20;

  final ProposalsService _service = ProposalsService.instance;

  ReadState _state = ReadState.idle;
  String _error = '';
  bool _expired = false;
  bool _inFlight = false;
  bool _loadingMore = false;

  BidStatus _status = BidStatus.all;
  List<ProposalRow> _proposals = const [];
  List<ActiveWorkRow> _activeWork = const [];
  BidCounts _counts = (pending: 0, shortlisted: 0, awarded: 0, rejected: 0);
  int _total = 0;
  int _page = 0;
  int _totalPages = 1;

  ReadState get state => _state;
  String get error => _error;
  bool get sessionExpired => _expired;
  bool get isLoading => _state == ReadState.loading;
  bool get isLoadingMore => _loadingMore;
  bool get isBusy => _inFlight || _loadingMore;

  List<ProposalRow> get proposals => _proposals;
  List<ActiveWorkRow> get activeWork => _activeWork;
  BidCounts get counts => _counts;
  int get total => _total;

  /// A next page exists only once a page is actually on screen: an account with
  /// no bids has nothing to append.
  bool get hasMore => _page > 0 && _page < _totalPages;
  BidStatus get status => _status;

  /// The bids in the status the screen has open; `all` is every bid sent.
  List<ProposalRow> get visible => _proposals;

  Future<void> load({bool force = false}) async {
    if (_inFlight) return;
    if (!force && (_state == ReadState.ready || _state == ReadState.empty)) {
      return;
    }
    if (!SessionStore.instance.hasSessionCookie) {
      _fail(SessionExpiry.message, true);
      return;
    }
    _inFlight = true;
    _error = '';
    _expired = false;
    if (_proposals.isEmpty) _state = ReadState.loading;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      final feed = await _service.fetch(
        page: 1,
        limit: pageSize,
        status: _status,
      );
      _proposals = feed.proposals;
      _page = feed.proposals.isEmpty ? 0 : 1;
      _apply(feed);
    } on NetworkException catch (e) {
      _fail(
        SessionExpiry.isExpired(e.statusCode)
            ? SessionExpiry.message
            : e.message,
        SessionExpiry.isExpired(e.statusCode),
      );
    } catch (e) {
      _fail('$e', false);
    } finally {
      _inFlight = false;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  Future<void> refresh() => load(force: true);

  Future<void> loadMore() async {
    if (_inFlight || _loadingMore || !hasMore) return;
    if (!SessionStore.instance.hasSessionCookie) {
      _error = SessionExpiry.message;
      _expired = true;
      notifyListeners();
      return;
    }
    _loadingMore = true;
    _error = '';
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      final feed = await _service.fetch(
        page: _page + 1,
        limit: pageSize,
        status: _status,
      );
      _apply(feed);
      if (feed.proposals.isNotEmpty && feed.page > _page) {
        _proposals = [..._proposals, ...feed.proposals];
        _page = feed.page;
      } else {
        _totalPages = _page;
      }
    } on NetworkException catch (e) {
      _error = SessionExpiry.isExpired(e.statusCode)
          ? SessionExpiry.message
          : e.message;
      _expired = SessionExpiry.isExpired(e.statusCode);
    } catch (e) {
      _error = '$e';
    } finally {
      _loadingMore = false;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  Future<void> setStatus(BidStatus status) async {
    if (status == _status) return;
    _status = status;
    _proposals = const [];
    await load(force: true);
  }

  void clear() {
    _proposals = const [];
    _activeWork = const [];
    _counts = (pending: 0, shortlisted: 0, awarded: 0, rejected: 0);
    _total = 0;
    _page = 0;
    _totalPages = 1;
    _status = BidStatus.all;
    _state = ReadState.idle;
    _error = '';
    _expired = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void _apply(ProposalFeed feed) {
    _activeWork = feed.activeWork;
    _counts = feed.counts;
    _total = feed.total;
    _totalPages = feed.totalPages;
    _state = feed.total == 0 && feed.activeWork.isEmpty
        ? ReadState.empty
        : ReadState.ready;
  }

  void _fail(String message, bool expired) {
    _error = message;
    _expired = expired;
    _proposals = const [];
    _activeWork = const [];
    _state = ReadState.failed;
  }
}

import '../../../core/network/api_client.dart';
import '../../../core/network/api_endpoints.dart';
import '../../../core/network/network_exceptions.dart';
import '../models/my_proposals.dart';

/// The proposals this account sent, read from `GET /api/bids/my`.
///
/// The route answers the same two tables the website's panel splits into its
/// tabs, and it does the paging, the status filter and the per-status counts.
/// Nothing here is decided on the device: a bid keeps the status the server
/// stored for it, and reading it cannot award, shortlist, reject or refund it.
class ProposalsService {
  ProposalsService._();

  static final ProposalsService instance = ProposalsService._();

  final ApiClient _apiClient = ApiClient();

  Future<ProposalFeed> fetch({
    int page = 1,
    int limit = 20,
    BidStatus status = BidStatus.all,
  }) async {
    final response = await _apiClient.get(
      ApiEndpoints.bidsMy,
      queryParameters: <String, String>{
        'page': '$page',
        'limit': '$limit',
        'status': status.query,
      },
    );
    if (response is! Map<String, dynamic>) {
      throw NetworkException('The server did not return any proposals.');
    }
    return proposalsFromJson(response);
  }
}

SOURCE FILE: D:\WorkingTrust\lib\features\finance\models\my_proposals.dart
SHA256: C12E80DEB2578251FE7BF88FE9C81EF5C60C76B48925A7E26E64084B703132E8

import 'json_read.dart';

/// The proposals this account sent, from `GET /api/bids/my`.
///
/// `proposals` is one page of bids, newest first; `activeWork` is the
/// assignments still ACTIVE or SUBMITTED, which is the same split the website's
/// panel makes. `counts` covers every bid the account has ever sent, in each of
/// the four statuses, whatever page or filter is open — so a tab header can
/// show a real number without fetching the whole list.
typedef ProposalFeed = ({
  List<ProposalRow> proposals,
  List<ActiveWorkRow> activeWork,
  BidCounts counts,
  int total,
  int page,
  int limit,
  int totalPages,
});

typedef ProposalRow = ({
  String id,
  String taskId,
  String title,
  String description,
  String status,
  double? bidAmount,
  String proposal,
  int connectsSpent,
  DateTime? at,
  String category,
  String clientName,
  double? earningAmount,
});

typedef ActiveWorkRow = ({
  String id,
  String bidId,
  String taskId,
  String title,
  String status,
  String statusLabel,
  double? payoutAmount,
  DateTime? dueAt,
  DateTime? at,
  String category,
  String clientName,
});

/// The four statuses a bid can hold. The route refuses anything else with a
/// 400, so these are the only filters the screen offers.
enum BidStatus {
  all('all', 'All'),
  pending('PENDING', 'Pending'),
  shortlisted('SHORTLISTED', 'Shortlisted'),
  awarded('AWARDED', 'Awarded'),
  rejected('REJECTED', 'Rejected');

  const BidStatus(this.query, this.label);

  /// The value the `status` parameter takes.
  final String query;
  final String label;

  static List<String> get labels =>
      BidStatus.values.map((s) => s.label).toList(growable: false);

  static BidStatus fromLabel(String label) => BidStatus.values.firstWhere(
    (status) => status.label == label,
    orElse: () => BidStatus.all,
  );

  /// The status as the server's own word, or the raw column when the account
  /// holds something outside the four.
  static String labelOf(String stored) => BidStatus.values
      .firstWhere(
        (status) => status.query == stored.toUpperCase(),
        orElse: () => BidStatus.all,
      )
      .label;
}

typedef BidCounts = ({int pending, int shortlisted, int awarded, int rejected});

ProposalFeed proposalsFromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
  final counts = json['counts'];
  final map = counts is Map<String, dynamic>
      ? counts
      : const <String, dynamic>{};
  return (
    proposals: [
      for (final row in jsonRows(json['proposals']))
        if (jsonText(row['id']).isNotEmpty)
          (
            id: jsonText(row['id']),
            taskId: jsonText(row['taskId']),
            title: jsonText(row['title']),
            description: jsonText(row['description']),
            status: jsonText(row['status']),
            bidAmount: jsonAmount(row['bidAmount']),
            proposal: jsonText(row['proposal']),
            connectsSpent: jsonCount(row['connectsSpent']) ?? 0,
            at: jsonDate(row['createdAt']),
            category: jsonText(row['category']),
            clientName: jsonText(row['clientName']),
            earningAmount: jsonAmount(row['earningAmount']),
          ),
    ],
    activeWork: [
      for (final row in jsonRows(json['activeWork']))
        if (jsonText(row['id']).isNotEmpty)
          (
            id: jsonText(row['id']),
            bidId: jsonText(row['bidId']),
            taskId: jsonText(row['taskId']),
            title: jsonText(row['title']),
            status: jsonText(row['status']),
            statusLabel: jsonText(row['statusLabel']),
            payoutAmount: jsonAmount(row['payoutAmount']),
            dueAt: jsonDate(row['dueAt']),
            at: jsonDate(row['createdAt']),
            category: jsonText(row['category']),
            clientName: jsonText(row['clientName']),
          ),
    ],
    counts: (
      pending: jsonCount(map['PENDING']) ?? 0,
      shortlisted: jsonCount(map['SHORTLISTED']) ?? 0,
      awarded: jsonCount(map['AWARDED']) ?? 0,
      rejected: jsonCount(map['REJECTED']) ?? 0,
    ),
    total: jsonCount(json['total']) ?? 0,
    page: jsonCount(json['page']) ?? 1,
    limit: jsonCount(json['limit']) ?? 20,
    totalPages: jsonCount(json['totalPages']) ?? 1,
  );
}

extension ProposalRowText on ProposalRow {
  String get statusLabel => BidStatus.labelOf(status);

  String get sentLabel => at == null ? '—' : shortDate(at!);

  /// The project pays what the task said; a bid amount the account typed is
  /// only ever an offer, so the earning is what the panel shows.
  String get earningLabel => earningAmount == null ? '—' : usd(earningAmount!);

  String get subtitle => [
    if (clientName.isNotEmpty) clientName,
    if (category.isNotEmpty) category,
    if (connectsSpent > 0) '$connectsSpent connects spent',
  ].join(' · ');
}

extension ActiveWorkRowText on ActiveWorkRow {
  String get payoutLabel => payoutAmount == null ? '—' : usd(payoutAmount!);

  String get dueLabel => dueAt == null ? '—' : shortDate(dueAt!);

  String get subtitle => [
    if (clientName.isNotEmpty) clientName,
    if (category.isNotEmpty) category,
    'Due $dueLabel',
  ].join(' · ');
}

SOURCE FILE: D:\WorkingTrust\lib\features\finance\controller\wallet_controller.dart

import '../models/wallet_data.dart';
import '../services/wallet_service.dart';
import 'read_controller.dart';

/// The account's money, from `GET /api/wallet`.
class WalletController extends ReadController<WalletSnapshot> {
  WalletController._();

  static final WalletController instance = WalletController._();

  final WalletService _service = WalletService.instance;

  @override
  WalletSnapshot emptyValue() => (
    balance: null,
    connects: null,
    planActive: false,
    pendingDeposit: null,
    earnings: const [],
    moneyActivity: const [],
    connectsActivity: const [],
  );

  /// The wallet is never shown as a blank screen.
  ///
  /// Its summary card carries the balance and connects the route reported — a
  /// brand-new account legitimately sees `$0.00` and `0` there — and the three
  /// ledgers each name their own nothing-yet state, so an account with no rows
  /// still gets the whole screen rather than an empty page.
  @override
  bool isEmptyValue(WalletSnapshot value) => false;

  @override
  Future<WalletSnapshot> read() => _service.fetch();
}
