    return other.isEmpty || other == 'null' ? null : fallback;
  }

  /// `titleCaseStatus` from the transactions page.
  String _titleCase(Object? status) {
    final value = '${status ?? ''}';
    return switch (value) {
      'PAID' => 'Paid',
      'PENDING' => 'Pending',
      'REJECTED' => 'Rejected',
      'APPROVED' => 'Active',
      'ACTIVE' => 'Active',
      _ => value.isEmpty ? value : value[0] + value.substring(1).toLowerCase(),
    };
  }

  /// `resolveClientDisplayName`: a blank or missing name becomes the placeholder.
  String _displayName(Object? value) {
    final text = '${value ?? ''}'.trim();
    return text.isEmpty ? 'WorkingTrust Client' : text;
  }

  /// `readPaging`: clamped, never NaN, never negative.
  ({int page, int limit}) _readPaging(
    Map<String, String> params, {
    int defaultPage = 1,
    int defaultLimit = 50,
    int maxLimit = 200,
  }) {
    int read(String key, int fallback) {
      final value = int.tryParse(params[key] ?? '');
      return value == null || value == 0 ? fallback : value;
    }

    return (
      page: read('page', defaultPage).clamp(1, 1 << 31),
      limit: read('limit', defaultLimit).clamp(1, maxLimit),
    );
  }

  static const List<String> _months = [
    'Jan',
    'Feb',
    'Mar',
    'Apr',
    'May',
    'Jun',
    'Jul',
    'Aug',
    'Sep',
    'Oct',
    'Nov',
    'Dec',
  ];

  String _shortDate(DateTime date) =>
      '${_months[date.month - 1]} ${date.day}, ${date.year}';

  String _usd(double amount) =>
      '\$${amount.toStringAsFixed(2).replaceAllMapped(RegExp(r'(\d)(?=(\d{3})\.)'), (m) => '${m[1]},')}';

  http.Response _json(int status, Object body, {Map<String, String>? headers}) {
    return http.Response(
      jsonEncode(body),
      status,
      headers: {'content-type': 'application/json', ...?headers},
    );
  }
}

  planName      String
  amount        Float
  method        String
  trxId         String
  screenshot    String
  status        String   @default("PENDING")
  createdAt     DateTime @default(now())
}

  kind      String
  source    String?
  viewerId  String?
  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  user      User     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@index([userId, kind, createdAt])
  @@index([userId, createdAt])
}

  updatedAt      DateTime @updatedAt
}

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getServerSession } from "next-auth";
import { authOptions } from "@/lib/auth";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { chatSchema } from "@/lib/validations";
import { notifyUser } from "@/lib/notifications";

async function canAccessChat(userId: string, role: string, taskId: string) {
  if (role === "ADMIN") return true;
  const assignment = await prisma.taskAssignment.findFirst({
    where: {
      taskId,
      userId,
      status: { in: ["ACTIVE", "SUBMITTED", "APPROVED", "REJECTED"] },
    },
  });
  return !!assignment;
}

function presentMessage(
  message: {
    id: string;
    body: string;
    attachmentUrl: string | null;
    createdAt: Date;
    senderId: string;
    sender: { id: string; name: string; role: string };
  },
  clientName: string,
  viewerRole: string,
) {
  const isAdminSender = message.sender.role === "ADMIN";
  const displayName =
    viewerRole === "ADMIN"
      ? isAdminSender
        ? `You (as ${clientName})`
        : message.sender.name
      : isAdminSender
        ? clientName
        : "You";

  return {
    id: message.id,
    body: message.body,
    attachmentUrl: message.attachmentUrl,
    createdAt: message.createdAt.toISOString(),
    senderId: message.senderId,
    displayName,
    isClientSide: isAdminSender,
  };
}

export async function GET(
  _: Request,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> },
) {
  const session = await getServerSession(authOptions);
  if (!session?.user?.id)
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });

  const { id: taskId } = await params;
  const ok = await canAccessChat(session.user.id, session.user.role, taskId);
  if (!ok) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Chat unlocks only after you are hired on this project." },
      { status: 403 },
    );
  }

  const task = await prisma.task.findUnique({
    where: { id: taskId },
    select: {
      clientName: true,
      clientAvatarUrl: true,
      title: true,
      chatFlagged: true,
      chatClosed: true,
    },
  });
  if (!task) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Not found" }, { status: 404 });

  const hired = await prisma.taskAssignment.findFirst({
    where: {
      taskId,
      status: { in: ["ACTIVE", "SUBMITTED", "APPROVED", "REJECTED"] },
    },
    orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
    select: {
      userId: true,
      user: { select: { name: true, avatarUrl: true } },
    },
  });

  const messages = await prisma.chatMessage.findMany({
    where: { taskId },
    include: { sender: { select: { id: true, name: true, role: true } } },
    orderBy: { createdAt: "asc" },
  });

  return NextResponse.json({
    clientName: task.clientName,
    clientAvatarUrl: task.clientAvatarUrl,
    freelancerName: hired?.user.name || null,
    freelancerAvatarUrl: hired?.user.avatarUrl || null,
    projectTitle: task.title,
    flagged: task.chatFlagged,
    closed: task.chatClosed,
    messages: messages.map((m) =>
      presentMessage(m, task.clientName, session.user.role),
    ),
  });
}

export async function POST(
  req: Request,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> },
) {
  const session = await getServerSession(authOptions);
  if (!session?.user?.id)
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });

  if (session.user.role !== "ADMIN") {
    const me = await prisma.user.findUnique({
      where: { id: session.user.id },
      select: { status: true },
    });
    if (!me || me.status === "SUSPENDED") {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Account suspended." },
        { status: 403 },
      );
    }
  }

  const { id: taskId } = await params;
  const ok = await canAccessChat(session.user.id, session.user.role, taskId);
  if (!ok) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Chat unlocks only after you are hired on this project." },
      { status: 403 },
    );
  }

  const raw = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
  const parsed = chatSchema.safeParse({
    body: typeof raw.body === "string" ? raw.body : "",
    attachmentUrl:
      typeof raw.attachmentUrl === "string" ? raw.attachmentUrl : "",
  });
  if (!parsed.success)
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Write a message or attach a file." },
      { status: 400 },
    );

  const task = await prisma.task.findUnique({
    where: { id: taskId },
    select: {
      clientName: true,
      clientAvatarUrl: true,
      title: true,
      chatClosed: true,
    },
  });
  if (!task) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Not found" }, { status: 404 });

  if (task.chatClosed) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "This conversation is closed." },
      { status: 403 },
    );
  }

  const hired = await prisma.taskAssignment.findFirst({
    where: {
      taskId,
      status: { in: ["ACTIVE", "SUBMITTED", "APPROVED", "REJECTED"] },
    },
    orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
    select: {
      userId: true,
      user: { select: { name: true, avatarUrl: true } },
    },
  });

  const message = await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
    const created = await tx.chatMessage.create({
      data: {
        taskId,
        senderId: session.user.id,
        body: parsed.data.body.trim(),
        attachmentUrl: parsed.data.attachmentUrl || null,
      },
      include: { sender: { select: { id: true, name: true, role: true } } },
    });

    // Bump assignment so Messages inbox sorts by latest activity
    await tx.taskAssignment.updateMany({
      where: { taskId },
      data: { updatedAt: new Date() },
    });

    return created;
  });

  const notificationBody = parsed.data.body.trim()
    ? parsed.data.body.trim().slice(0, 120)
    : "Sent an attachment";

  if (session.user.role === "ADMIN" && hired) {
    await notifyUser({
      userId: hired.userId,
      type: "CHAT_MESSAGE",
      title: `Message from ${task.clientName}`,
      body: notificationBody,
      href: `/messages?task=${taskId}`,
    });
  }

  // Freelancer messaged → notify admin account(s)
  if (session.user.role !== "ADMIN") {
    const admins = await prisma.user.findMany({
      where: { role: "ADMIN" },
      select: { id: true },
      take: 5,
    });
    await Promise.all(
      admins.map((a) =>
        notifyUser({
          userId: a.id,
          type: "CHAT_MESSAGE",
          title: `Reply on “${task.title}”`,
          body: notificationBody,
          href: `/admin/messages?task=${taskId}`,
        }),
      ),
    );
  }

  return NextResponse.json(
    {
      message: presentMessage(message, task.clientName, session.user.role),
      clientName: task.clientName,
      clientAvatarUrl: task.clientAvatarUrl,
      freelancerName: hired?.user.name || null,
      freelancerAvatarUrl: hired?.user.avatarUrl || null,
    },
    { status: 201 },
  );
}


====================================================================================================
SOURCE FILE: D:\Trust Work\src\app\api\tasks\[id]\bid\route.ts
SHA256: 42887F5AA5EA3EDB4AE7136A77B3C6B9173E9697B329AE9118100B67571BF975
====================================================================================================
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getServerSession } from "next-auth";
import { authOptions } from "@/lib/auth";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { bidSchema } from "@/lib/validations";
import { writeAudit } from "@/lib/audit";
import { connectsForBudget } from "@/lib/connects";
import { notifyMany } from "@/lib/notifications";

export async function POST(
  req: Request,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> },
) {
  const session = await getServerSession(authOptions);
  if (!session?.user?.id)
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });

  const { id: taskId } = await params;
  const raw = await req.json();
  const parsed = bidSchema.safeParse({
    proposal: raw.proposal,
    bidAmount:
      raw.bidAmount === "" || raw.bidAmount == null
        ? undefined
        : Number(raw.bidAmount),
    extraConnects: raw.extraConnects == null ? 0 : Number(raw.extraConnects),
    attachmentUrls: Array.isArray(raw.attachmentUrls)
      ? raw.attachmentUrls.filter((u: unknown) => typeof u === "string")
      : undefined,
    portfolioItemIds: Array.isArray(raw.portfolioItemIds)
      ? raw.portfolioItemIds.filter((i: unknown) => typeof i === "string")
      : undefined,
  });
  if (!parsed.success) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Write a clear proposal (min 20 characters)." },
      { status: 400 },
    );
  }

  try {
    const result = await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
      const task = await tx.task.findUnique({ where: { id: taskId } });
      if (!task || task.status !== "OPEN") throw new Error("CLOSED");

      const existing = await tx.bid.findUnique({
        where: { userId_taskId: { userId: session.user.id, taskId } },
      });
      if (existing) throw new Error("EXISTS");

      const baseCost =
        task.connectsCost || connectsForBudget(task.earningAmount);
      const extraConnects = parsed.data.extraConnects ?? 0;
      const cost = baseCost + extraConnects;
      const user = await tx.user.findUnique({ where: { id: session.user.id } });
      if (!user) throw new Error("NO_USER");
      if (user.status === "SUSPENDED") throw new Error("SUSPENDED");
      if (user.connects < cost) throw new Error("LOW_CONNECTS");

      await tx.user.update({
        where: { id: user.id },
        data: { connects: { decrement: cost } },
      });
      await tx.connectLedger.create({
        data: {
          userId: user.id,
          delta: -cost,
          reason: `Applied on project: ${task.title}`,
          refId: task.id,
        },
      });

      const bid = await tx.bid.create({
        data: {
          userId: user.id,
          taskId: task.id,
          proposal: parsed.data.proposal.trim(),
          bidAmount: Number.isFinite(parsed.data.bidAmount as number)
            ? (parsed.data.bidAmount as number)
            : null,
          extraConnects,
          connectsSpent: cost,
          attachmentUrls: parsed.data.attachmentUrls?.length
            ? JSON.stringify(parsed.data.attachmentUrls)
            : null,
          portfolioItemIds: parsed.data.portfolioItemIds?.length
            ? JSON.stringify(parsed.data.portfolioItemIds)
            : null,
          status: "PENDING",
        },
      });
      await tx.task.update({
        where: { id: task.id },
        data: { submittedProposals: { increment: 1 } },
      });
      return { bid, taskTitle: task.title };
    }, { timeout: 15000 });

    try {
      await writeAudit("BID_CREATED", session.user.id, {
        bidId: result.bid.id,
        taskId,
      });
      const admins = await prisma.user.findMany({
        where: { role: "ADMIN", status: "ACTIVE" },
        select: { id: true },
      });
      await notifyMany(
        admins.map((admin) => admin.id),
        {
          type: "BID_CREATED",
          title: "New proposal received",
          body: `A new proposal was submitted for “${result.taskTitle}”.`,
          href: "/admin/bids",
        },
      );
    } catch {
      // The proposal itself already saved; audit/notification failures
      // shouldn't surface as a failed application to the applicant.
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ bid: result.bid }, { status: 201 });
  } catch (e) {
    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : "FAIL";
    const map: Record<string, [number, string]> = {
      CLOSED: [400, "This project is not open for applications."],
      EXISTS: [400, "You already applied on this project."],
      LOW_CONNECTS: [400, "Not enough connects. Buy a package first."],
      NO_USER: [404, "User not found."],
      SUSPENDED: [403, "Account suspended."],
    };
    const [status, error] = map[msg] || [500, "Could not apply."];
    return NextResponse.json({ error }, { status });
  }
}

====================================================================================================
SOURCE FILE: D:\Trust Work\src\app\api\admin\bids\[id]\award\route.ts
SHA256: 1414839C5E78C532EE28FFF1E2DF7AE7B900CB326E34F9E41971C63CE06E685B
====================================================================================================
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getServerSession } from "next-auth";
import { authOptions } from "@/lib/auth";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { writeAudit } from "@/lib/audit";
import { notifyMany, notifyUser } from "@/lib/notifications";
import { refundBidConnects } from "@/lib/bid-refunds";
import { roundMoney } from "@/lib/money";

export async function POST(
  _: Request,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> },
) {
  const session = await getServerSession(authOptions);
  if (!session?.user?.id || session.user.role !== "ADMIN") {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }

  const { id: bidId } = await params;

  try {
    const result = await prisma.$transaction(
      async (tx) => {
        const bid = await tx.bid.findUnique({
          where: { id: bidId },
          include: { task: true, user: true },
        });
        if (
          !bid ||
          (bid.status !== "PENDING" && bid.status !== "SHORTLISTED")
        ) {
          throw new Error("BAD_BID");
        }
        if (bid.task.status !== "OPEN") throw new Error("TASK_CLOSED");

        const others = await tx.bid.findMany({
          where: {
            taskId: bid.taskId,
            id: { not: bid.id },
            status: { in: ["PENDING", "SHORTLISTED"] },
          },
          include: { task: true },
        });

        await tx.bid.update({
          where: { id: bid.id },
          data: { status: "AWARDED" },
        });
        await tx.bid.updateMany({
          where: {
            taskId: bid.taskId,
            id: { not: bid.id },
            status: { in: ["PENDING", "SHORTLISTED"] },
          },
          data: { status: "REJECTED" },
        });

        for (const other of others) {
          await refundBidConnects(tx, other);
        }

        const payoutAmount = roundMoney(
          Number.isFinite(bid.bidAmount as number) &&
            (bid.bidAmount as number) > 0
            ? (bid.bidAmount as number)
            : bid.task.earningAmount,
        );

        const dueAt = new Date(
          Date.now() + bid.task.deadlineHours * 60 * 60 * 1000,
        );
        const assignment = await tx.taskAssignment.create({
          data: {
            userId: bid.userId,
            taskId: bid.taskId,
            bidId: bid.id,
            connectsSpent: bid.connectsSpent,
            payoutAmount,
            dueAt,
            status: "ACTIVE",
          },
        });

        await tx.task.update({
          where: { id: bid.taskId },
          data: { status: "ASSIGNED" },
        });

        return {
          assignment,
          bid,
          otherUserIds: others.map((b) => b.userId),
          refundedConnects: others.reduce((s, b) => s + b.connectsSpent, 0),
          payoutAmount,
        };
      },
      { maxWait: 10000, timeout: 15000 },
    );

    await prisma.chatMessage.create({
      data: {
        taskId: result.bid.taskId,
        senderId: session.user.id,
        body: `Your proposal was accepted for “${result.bid.task.title}”. Congratulations! You can now communicate with ${result.bid.task.clientName} here and get started on the project. The agreed payout is $${result.payoutAmount.toFixed(2)}.`,
      },
    });

    await writeAudit("BID_AWARDED", session.user.id, {
      bidId,
      assignmentId: result.assignment.id,
      payoutAmount: result.payoutAmount,
    });
    await notifyUser({
      userId: result.bid.userId,
      type: "BID_AWARDED",
      title: "Your proposal was accepted",
      body: `${result.bid.task.clientName} accepted your proposal for “${result.bid.task.title}”. You can now communicate with the client and begin the project. Agreed payout: $${result.payoutAmount.toFixed(2)}.`,
      href: `/messages?task=${result.bid.taskId}`,
    });
    await notifyMany(result.otherUserIds, {
      type: "BID_REJECTED",
      title: "Not selected — connects refunded",
      body: `Another freelancer was hired for “${result.bid.task.title}”. Your connects for that apply were refunded.`,
      href: "/wallet",
    });
    return NextResponse.json({ assignment: result.assignment });
  } catch (e) {
    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : "FAIL";
    if (msg === "BAD_BID")
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Bid not available." },
        { status: 400 },
      );
    if (msg === "TASK_CLOSED")
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Project already awarded." },
        { status: 400 },
      );
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: `Award failed: ${msg}` },
      { status: 500 },
    );
  }
}

SOURCE FILE: D:\Trust Work\src\app\api\admin\bids\[id]\reject\route.ts
SHA256: B273A089A1A8831946491D2B44A13B793CF610C213F1AEE60F312A8ACB912D48

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getServerSession } from "next-auth";
import { authOptions } from "@/lib/auth";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { writeAudit } from "@/lib/audit";
import { notifyUser } from "@/lib/notifications";
import { refundBidConnects } from "@/lib/bid-refunds";

export async function POST(req: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const session = await getServerSession(authOptions);
  if (!session?.user?.id || session.user.role !== "ADMIN") {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }

  const { id: bidId } = await params;
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
  const note = typeof body.note === "string" ? body.note : "";

  try {
    const bid = await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
      const current = await tx.bid.findUnique({
        where: { id: bidId },
        include: { task: true },
      });
      if (
        !current ||
        (current.status !== "PENDING" && current.status !== "SHORTLISTED")
      ) {
        throw new Error("BAD_BID");
      }

      await tx.bid.update({ where: { id: bidId }, data: { status: "REJECTED" } });
      await refundBidConnects(tx, current);
      return current;
    });

    await writeAudit("BID_REJECTED", session.user.id, { bidId, note, refunded: bid.connectsSpent });
    await notifyUser({
      userId: bid.userId,
      type: "BID_REJECTED",
      title: "Proposal declined — connects refunded",
      body: note
        ? `“${bid.task.title}” declined. ${bid.connectsSpent} connects returned. Note: ${note}`
        : `Your proposal on “${bid.task.title}” was declined. ${bid.connectsSpent} connects were refunded.`,
      href: "/my-work?tab=proposals",
    });

    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, refunded: bid.connectsSpent });
  } catch (e) {
    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : "FAIL";
    if (msg === "BAD_BID") return NextResponse.json({ error: "Bid not pending" }, { status: 400 });
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Reject failed" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

SOURCE FILE: D:\Trust Work\src\app\api\admin\chat\[taskId]\route.ts
SHA256: 8F56B6264B72535A01E6089BE4668A8BCEDF168E0C155D7EC24F2B106C7B7EA9

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getServerSession } from "next-auth";
import { authOptions } from "@/lib/auth";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { writeAudit } from "@/lib/audit";

export async function PATCH(
  req: Request,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ taskId: string }> },
) {
  const session = await getServerSession(authOptions);
  if (!session?.user?.id || session.user.role !== "ADMIN") {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }

  const { taskId } = await params;
  const body = (await req.json().catch(() => ({}))) as {
    flagged?: boolean;
    closed?: boolean;
  };

  const task = await prisma.task.findUnique({
    where: { id: taskId },
    select: { id: true, title: true, chatFlagged: true, chatClosed: true },
  });
  if (!task) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Thread not found" }, { status: 404 });
  }

  const data: { chatFlagged?: boolean; chatClosed?: boolean } = {};
  if (typeof body.flagged === "boolean") data.chatFlagged = body.flagged;
  if (typeof body.closed === "boolean") data.chatClosed = body.closed;

  if (Object.keys(data).length === 0) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Provide flagged and/or closed" },
      { status: 400 },
    );
  }

  const updated = await prisma.task.update({
    where: { id: taskId },
    data,
    select: {
      id: true,
      chatFlagged: true,
      chatClosed: true,
    },
  });

  await writeAudit("CHAT_THREAD_UPDATED", session.user.id, {
    taskId,
    ...data,
  });

  return NextResponse.json({
    taskId: updated.id,
    flagged: updated.chatFlagged,
    closed: updated.chatClosed,
  });
}

SOURCE FILE: D:\Trust Work\src\app\api\notifications\route.ts
SHA256: 18B46A9A4A3E6FCBA37F2BEA104965B29D0C22E992B50EB91F83C38FDA9A8AB0

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getServerSession } from "next-auth";
import { authOptions } from "@/lib/auth";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import {
  matchesActivityLevel,
  parseNotificationPrefs,
  unreadTypeFilter,
} from "@/lib/notification-prefs";

export async function GET() {
  const session = await getServerSession(authOptions);
  if (!session?.user?.id)
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });

  const user = await prisma.user.findUnique({
    where: { id: session.user.id },
    select: { notificationPrefs: true },
  });
  const prefs = parseNotificationPrefs(user?.notificationPrefs);
  const typeFilter = unreadTypeFilter(prefs);

  const items = await prisma.notification.findMany({
    where: { userId: session.user.id, dismissedAt: null },
    orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
    take: 80,
  });

  const visible = items
    .filter((n) => matchesActivityLevel(n.type, prefs.desktopShow))
    .slice(0, 40);

  const unread =
    typeFilter !== null && typeFilter.length === 0
      ? 0
      : await prisma.notification.count({
          where: {
            userId: session.user.id,
            readAt: null,
            dismissedAt: null,
            ...(typeFilter === null ? {} : { type: { in: typeFilter } }),
          },
        });

  return NextResponse.json({ notifications: visible, unread });
}

export async function PATCH(req: Request) {
  const session = await getServerSession(authOptions);
  if (!session?.user?.id)
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });

  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
  const id = typeof body.id === "string" ? body.id : null;
  const dismiss = body.dismiss === true;
  const all = body.all === true;
  const related = body.related === true;
  const taskId = typeof body.taskId === "string" ? body.taskId : null;

  if (all) {
    await prisma.notification.updateMany({
      where: { userId: session.user.id, readAt: null },
      data: { readAt: new Date() },
    });
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  }

  if (taskId) {
    await prisma.notification.updateMany({
      where: {
        userId: session.user.id,
        type: "CHAT_MESSAGE",
        readAt: null,
        OR: [
          { href: { equals: `/messages?task=${taskId}` } },
          { href: { equals: `/admin/chat/${taskId}` } },
        ],
      },
      data: { readAt: new Date() },
    });
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  }

  if (!id) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Missing id" }, { status: 400 });

  if (dismiss) {
    await prisma.notification.updateMany({
      where: { id, userId: session.user.id },
      data: { dismissedAt: new Date(), readAt: new Date() },
    });
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  }

  if (related) {
    const notification = await prisma.notification.findFirst({
      where: { id, userId: session.user.id },
      select: { type: true, href: true },
    });
    if (notification) {
      await prisma.notification.updateMany({
        where: {
          userId: session.user.id,
          type: notification.type,
          href: notification.href,
          readAt: null,
        },
        data: { readAt: new Date() },
      });
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  }

  await prisma.notification.updateMany({
    where: { id, userId: session.user.id },
    data: { readAt: new Date() },
  });
  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
}

SOURCE FILE: D:\Trust Work\src\app\api\devices\fcm-token\route.ts
SHA256: A13403B33D9F650F48AE39B9556DA08F36465C212346012C6296290C16435EF8
====================================================================================================
import { Prisma } from "@prisma/client";
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getServerSession } from "next-auth";
import { authOptions } from "@/lib/auth";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

const TOKEN_PATTERN = /^[^\u0000-\u001f\u007f]{20,4096}$/;
const PLATFORMS = ["android", "ios"] as const;
type Platform = (typeof PLATFORMS)[number];

function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
}

function isToken(value: unknown): value is string {
  return (
    typeof value === "string" &&
    value.trim() === value &&
    TOKEN_PATTERN.test(value)
  );
}

function isPlatform(value: unknown): value is Platform {
  return (
    typeof value === "string" &&
    (PLATFORMS as readonly string[]).includes(value)
  );
}

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const session = await getServerSession(authOptions);
  if (!session?.user?.id) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }

  const body: unknown = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  if (
    !isRecord(body) ||
    !isToken(body.token) ||
    !isPlatform(body.platform) ||
    (body.previousToken != null && !isToken(body.previousToken))
  ) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "A valid token and platform are required." },
      { status: 400 },
    );
  }

  const token = body.token;
  const platform = body.platform;
  const previousToken =
    typeof body.previousToken === "string" ? body.previousToken : null;

  try {
    const registered = await prisma.$transaction(
      async (tx) => {
        const existing = await tx.fcmDeviceToken.findUnique({
          where: { token },
          select: { userId: true, active: true },
        });

        if (existing?.active && existing.userId !== session.user.id) {
          return false;
        }

        if (previousToken && previousToken !== token) {
          await tx.fcmDeviceToken.updateMany({
            where: {
              token: previousToken,
              userId: session.user.id,
              active: true,
            },
            data: { active: false },
          });
        }

        if (existing) {
          await tx.fcmDeviceToken.update({
            where: { token },
            data: { userId: session.user.id, platform, active: true },
          });
        } else {
          await tx.fcmDeviceToken.create({
            data: { token, platform, userId: session.user.id },
          });
        }
        return true;
      },
      { maxWait: 10000, timeout: 15000 },
    );

    if (!registered) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "This device token is already registered to another account." },
        { status: 409 },
      );
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (error) {
    if (
      error instanceof Prisma.PrismaClientKnownRequestError &&
      error.code === "P2002"
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "This device token is already registered." },
        { status: 409 },
      );
    }
    console.error("[devices] FCM token registration failed", {
      userId: session.user.id,
      error:
        error instanceof Prisma.PrismaClientKnownRequestError
          ? error.code
          : error instanceof Error
            ? error.name
            : "Unknown error",
    });
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Unable to register this device." },
      { status: 500 },
    );
  }
}

export async function DELETE(req: Request) {
  const session = await getServerSession(authOptions);
  if (!session?.user?.id) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }

  const body: unknown = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!isRecord(body) || !isToken(body.token)) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "A valid token is required." },
      { status: 400 },
    );
  }

  try {
    await prisma.fcmDeviceToken.updateMany({
      where: { token: body.token, userId: session.user.id, active: true },
      data: { active: false },
    });
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("[devices] FCM token deactivation failed", {
      userId: session.user.id,
      error:
        error instanceof Prisma.PrismaClientKnownRequestError
          ? error.code
          : error instanceof Error
            ? error.name
            : "Unknown error",
    });
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Unable to deactivate this device." },
      { status: 500 },
    );
  }
}

SOURCE FILE: D:\Trust Work\src\app\api\admin\submissions\[id]\route.ts
SHA256: 7EF7DF21CDA2E58646B7175C8BD1290B9E29EA6B0323A35EF3BD53D910ADF818

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getServerSession } from "next-auth";
import { authOptions } from "@/lib/auth";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { writeAudit } from "@/lib/audit";
import { notifyUser } from "@/lib/notifications";
import { formatUsd } from "@/lib/format";
import { roundMoney } from "@/lib/money";

export async function PATCH(req: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const session = await getServerSession(authOptions);
  if (!session?.user?.id || session.user.role !== "ADMIN") {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }

  const { id } = await params;
  const body = await req.json();
  const action = body.action as "approve" | "reject" | "revision" | "decline";
  const note = typeof body.note === "string" ? body.note : "";

  const submission = await prisma.submission.findUnique({
    where: { id },
    include: { assignment: { include: { task: true } } },
  });

  if (!submission || submission.assignment.status !== "SUBMITTED") {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Submission not in review" }, { status: 400 });
  }

  // Only the latest submission for this assignment can be approved/rejected
  const latest = await prisma.submission.findFirst({
    where: { assignmentId: submission.assignmentId },
    orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
    select: { id: true },
  });
  if (!latest || latest.id !== id) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Only the latest submission for this job can be reviewed." },
      { status: 400 }
    );
  }

  const assignment = submission.assignment;
  const task = assignment.task;
  const payout = roundMoney(assignment.payoutAmount ?? task.earningAmount);

  // "reject" and "revision" both request changes (freelancer can resubmit)
  if (action === "reject" || action === "revision") {
    await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
      await tx.submission.update({ where: { id }, data: { adminNote: note } });
      await tx.taskAssignment.update({
        where: { id: assignment.id },
        data: { status: "REJECTED" },
      });
      await tx.task.update({ where: { id: task.id }, data: { status: "ASSIGNED" } });
    });
    await writeAudit("SUBMISSION_REJECTED", session.user.id, { submissionId: id });
    await notifyUser({
      userId: assignment.userId,
      type: "SUBMISSION_REJECTED",
      title: "Work needs changes",
      body: note
        ? `“${task.title}” was sent back. Note: ${note}`
        : `“${task.title}” needs revision. Check My work and resubmit.`,
      href: "/my-work",
    });
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  }

  if (action === "decline") {
    await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
      await tx.submission.update({ where: { id }, data: { adminNote: note } });
      await tx.taskAssignment.update({
        where: { id: assignment.id },
        data: { status: "DECLINED" },
      });
      await tx.task.update({
        where: { id: task.id },
        data: { status: "CANCELLED" },
      });
    });
    await writeAudit("SUBMISSION_DECLINED", session.user.id, { submissionId: id });
    await notifyUser({
      userId: assignment.userId,
      type: "SUBMISSION_REJECTED",
      title: "Submission rejected",
      body: note
        ? `“${task.title}” was rejected. Note: ${note}`
        : `Your submission for “${task.title}” was rejected.`,
      href: "/my-work",
    });
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  }

  if (action !== "approve") {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid action" }, { status: 400 });
  }

  await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
    await tx.submission.update({ where: { id }, data: { adminNote: note } });
    await tx.taskAssignment.update({
      where: { id: assignment.id },
      data: { status: "APPROVED", payoutAmount: payout },
    });
    await tx.task.update({ where: { id: task.id }, data: { status: "COMPLETED" } });
    await tx.user.update({
      where: { id: assignment.userId },
      data: { walletBalance: { increment: payout } },
    });
    await tx.walletTransaction.create({
      data: {
        userId: assignment.userId,
        type: "CREDIT",
        amount: payout,
        reason: `Task approved: ${task.title}`,
        refId: assignment.id,
      },
    });
  });

  await writeAudit("SUBMISSION_APPROVED", session.user.id, {
    submissionId: id,
    amount: payout,
  });
  await notifyUser({
    userId: assignment.userId,
    type: "SUBMISSION_APPROVED",
    title: "Work approved — wallet credited",
    body: `${formatUsd(payout)} added for “${task.title}”. You can withdraw from Settings → WithDrawals.`,
    href: "/wallet",
  });

  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
}

SHA256: 189F8DC8FA0168802B99F89074C3605E519BC6719CB24CDC30C4A99340FC8997

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getServerSession } from "next-auth";
import { authOptions } from "@/lib/auth";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { submissionSchema } from "@/lib/validations";
import { writeAudit } from "@/lib/audit";
import { notifyMany } from "@/lib/notifications";
import {
  isCloudinaryFileUrl,
  ownedFileExists,
  parseOwnedFileUrl,
} from "@/lib/owned-file";

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const session = await getServerSession(authOptions);
  if (!session?.user?.id)
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });

  const me = await prisma.user.findUnique({
    where: { id: session.user.id },
    select: { status: true },
  });
  if (!me || me.status === "SUSPENDED") {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Account suspended." }, { status: 403 });
  }

  const parsed = submissionSchema.safeParse(await req.json());
  if (!parsed.success)
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid submission" }, { status: 400 });

  const workUrl = (parsed.data.workUrl || "").trim();
  const fileUrl = (parsed.data.fileUrl || "").trim();
  const notes = (parsed.data.notes || "").trim();

  if (!workUrl && !fileUrl) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Add a work URL or upload a deliverable file." },
      { status: 400 },
    );
  }

  let safeFileUrl: string | null = null;
  if (fileUrl) {
    if (isCloudinaryFileUrl(fileUrl, session.user.id)) {
      safeFileUrl = fileUrl;
    } else {
      const filename = parseOwnedFileUrl(fileUrl, session.user.id);
      if (!filename) {
        return NextResponse.json(
          { error: "Invalid file. Upload again from this form." },
          { status: 400 },
        );
      }
      const exists = await ownedFileExists(session.user.id, filename);
      if (!exists) {
        return NextResponse.json(
          { error: "Uploaded file not found. Upload again." },
          { status: 400 },
        );
      }
      safeFileUrl = `/api/files/${session.user.id}/${filename}`;
    }
  }

  const assignment = await prisma.taskAssignment.findFirst({
    where: {
      id: parsed.data.assignmentId,
      userId: session.user.id,
      status: { in: ["ACTIVE", "REJECTED"] },
    },
    include: { task: true },
  });

  if (!assignment) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Assignment not found or not editable." },
      { status: 404 },
    );
  }

  const submission = await prisma
    .$transaction(
      async (tx) => {
        // Prevent double-submit race
        const locked = await tx.taskAssignment.findFirst({
          where: {
            id: assignment.id,
            status: { in: ["ACTIVE", "REJECTED"] },
          },
        });
        if (!locked) throw new Error("BUSY");

        const sub = await tx.submission.create({
          data: {
            assignmentId: assignment.id,
            notes: notes || null,
            workUrl: workUrl || null,
            fileUrl: safeFileUrl,
          },
        });

        await tx.taskAssignment.update({
          where: { id: assignment.id },
          data: { status: "SUBMITTED" },
        });

        await tx.task.update({
          where: { id: assignment.taskId },
          data: { status: "IN_REVIEW" },
        });

        const existingDeliveryNote = await tx.chatMessage.findFirst({
          where: {
            taskId: assignment.taskId,
            senderId: session.user.id,
            body: "Project delivered\nThe freelancer submitted the project for review.",
          },
          orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
        });

        if (!existingDeliveryNote) {
          await tx.chatMessage.create({
            data: {
              taskId: assignment.taskId,
              senderId: session.user.id,
              body: "Project delivered\nThe freelancer submitted the project for review.",
            },
          });
        }

        return sub;
      },
      { maxWait: 10000, timeout: 15000 },
    )
    .catch((e) => {
      if (e instanceof Error && e.message === "BUSY") return null;
      throw e;
    });

  if (!submission) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Already submitted. Wait for review." },
      { status: 409 },
    );
  }

  await writeAudit("SUBMISSION_CREATED", session.user.id, {
    submissionId: submission.id,
    assignmentId: assignment.id,
  });

  const admins = await prisma.user.findMany({
    where: { role: "ADMIN", status: "ACTIVE" },
    select: { id: true },
  });
  await notifyMany(
    admins.map((a) => a.id),
    {
      type: "SUBMISSION_CREATED",
      title: "New work submission",
      body: `“${assignment.task.title}” is ready for review.`,
      href: "/admin/submissions",
    },
  );

  return NextResponse.json({ submission }, { status: 201 });
}

import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/session";
import { formatShortDate } from "@/lib/format";
import { resolveClientDisplayName } from "@/lib/client-profile";
import { DashboardFooter } from "@/components/dashboard/dashboard-footer";
import {
  MessagesInbox,
  type ConversationRow,
} from "@/components/messages-inbox";

function clientLocation(
  client: { city: string | null; country: string | null } | null,
  task: { clientRegion: string | null; clientCountry: string | null },
) {
  const profileCity = client?.city?.trim() || "";
  const profileCountry = client?.country?.trim() || "";
  const parts =
    profileCity || profileCountry
      ? [profileCity, profileCountry]
      : [task.clientRegion?.trim() || "", task.clientCountry?.trim() || ""];
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  const unique = parts.filter((part) => {
    if (!part || /^not specified$/i.test(part)) return false;
    const key = part.toLowerCase();
    if (seen.has(key)) return false;
    seen.add(key);
    return true;
  });
  return {
    city: unique[0] || "Worldwide",
    profileCountry: unique[1] || null,
  };
}

export default async function MessagesPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<{ task?: string }>;
}) {
  const session = await requireUser();
  const { task: selectedId } = await searchParams;

  const [assignments, unreadChatNotifications, bids] = await Promise.all([
    prisma.taskAssignment.findMany({
      where: {
        userId: session.user.id,
        status: { in: ["ACTIVE", "SUBMITTED", "APPROVED", "REJECTED"] },
      },
      include: {
        task: {
          include: {
            messages: {
              orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
              take: 1,
            },
            client: {
              select: { createdAt: true, city: true, country: true },
            },
          },
        },
      },
      orderBy: { updatedAt: "desc" },
    }),
    prisma.notification.findMany({
      where: {
        userId: session.user.id,
        type: "CHAT_MESSAGE",
        readAt: null,
        href: { startsWith: "/messages?task=" },
      },
      select: { href: true },
    }),
    prisma.bid.findMany({
      where: { userId: session.user.id },
      select: { id: true, taskId: true },
    }),
  ]);

  assignments.sort((a, b) => {
    const at =
      a.task.messages[0]?.createdAt?.getTime() || a.updatedAt.getTime();
    const bt =
      b.task.messages[0]?.createdAt?.getTime() || b.updatedAt.getTime();
    return bt - at;
  });

  const unreadTaskIds = new Set(
    unreadChatNotifications
      .map((n) => n.href)
      .filter((href): href is string => !!href)
      .map((href) => href.replace("/messages?task=", ""))
      .filter(Boolean),
  );

  const bidByTaskId = new Map(bids.map((b) => [b.taskId, b.id]));

  const selectedAssignment =
    assignments.find((a) => a.taskId === selectedId) || assignments[0] || null;

  const conversations: ConversationRow[] = assignments.map((a) => ({
    taskId: a.taskId,
    name: resolveClientDisplayName(a.task.clientName),
    avatarUrl: a.task.clientAvatarUrl?.trim() || null,
    taskTitle: a.task.title,
    lastAt: (a.task.messages[0]?.createdAt || a.createdAt).toISOString(),
    unread: unreadTaskIds.has(a.taskId),
    country: a.task.clientCountry,
    ...clientLocation(a.task.client, a.task),
    memberSince: a.task.client?.createdAt
      ? formatShortDate(a.task.client.createdAt)
      : null,
    bidHref: bidByTaskId.has(a.taskId)
      ? `/my-work/proposals/${bidByTaskId.get(a.taskId)}`
      : null,
  }));

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-full flex-col bg-white">
      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-[1440px] flex-1 px-4 pb-16 pt-8 sm:px-6 sm:pt-[50px] lg:px-[50px] lg:pb-[100px]">
        <MessagesInbox
          conversations={conversations}
          selectedTaskId={selectedAssignment?.taskId || null}
          hasExplicitSelection={Boolean(selectedId)}
          currentUserId={session.user.id}
        />
      </div>
      <DashboardFooter isLoggedIn />
    </div>
  );
}

import {
  AdminClientChat,
  type AdminChatThread,
} from "@/components/admin-client-chat";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { requireAdmin } from "@/lib/session";

function shortChatId(id: string) {
  let hash = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < id.length; i++) {
    hash = (hash * 31 + id.charCodeAt(i)) >>> 0;
  }
  return `CHT-${String(1000 + (hash % 9000)).padStart(4, "0")}`;
}

function previewText(body: string, attachmentUrl: string | null) {
  const text = body.trim();
  if (text) return text;
  if (attachmentUrl) return attachmentUrl.split("/").pop() || "Attachment";
  return "";
}

export default async function AdminMessagesPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<{ task?: string }>;
}) {
  const session = await requireAdmin();
  const { task: initialTaskId } = await searchParams;

  const tasks = await prisma.task.findMany({
    where: { messages: { some: {} } },
    select: {
      id: true,
      title: true,
      clientName: true,
      chatFlagged: true,
      chatClosed: true,
      messages: {
        orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
        take: 40,
        select: {
          body: true,
          attachmentUrl: true,
          createdAt: true,
          sender: { select: { role: true } },
        },
      },
      assignments: {
        take: 1,
        orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
        select: { user: { select: { name: true } } },
      },
    },
  });

  const threads: AdminChatThread[] = tasks
    .map((t) => {
      const msgs = t.messages;
      const last = msgs[0];
      let unreadCount = 0;
      for (const m of msgs) {
        if (m.sender.role === "USER") unreadCount += 1;
        else break;
      }

      return {
        taskId: t.id,
        displayId: shortChatId(t.id),
        projectTitle: t.title,
        clientName: t.clientName || "Client",
        freelancerName: t.assignments[0]?.user.name || "Freelancer",
        lastPreview: last
          ? previewText(last.body, last.attachmentUrl)
          : "",
        lastAt: (last?.createdAt || new Date()).toISOString(),
        unreadCount: t.chatClosed ? 0 : unreadCount,
        flagged: t.chatFlagged,
        closed: t.chatClosed,
      } satisfies AdminChatThread;
    })
    .sort(
      (a, b) => new Date(b.lastAt).getTime() - new Date(a.lastAt).getTime(),
    );

  return (
    <AdminClientChat
      threads={threads}
      initialTaskId={initialTaskId || null}
      adminUserId={session.user.id}
    />
  );
}

import { redirect } from "next/navigation";

export default async function AdminChatPage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ taskId: string }>;
}) {
  const { taskId } = await params;
  redirect(`/admin/messages?task=${encodeURIComponent(taskId)}`);
}

"use client";

import { FormEvent, useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import Image from "next/image";
import { Paperclip, X } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Textarea } from "@/components/ui";
import { formatDate } from "@/lib/format";

type Msg = {
  id: string;
  body: string;
  attachmentUrl?: string | null;
  createdAt: string;
  senderId: string;
  displayName: string;
  isClientSide: boolean;
};

const IMAGE_EXT = /\.(png|jpe?g|webp|gif)$/i;

function timeOnly(iso: string) {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {
    hour: "numeric",
    minute: "2-digit",
    hour12: true,
  })
    .format(new Date(iso))
    .toLowerCase();
}

function dayLabel(iso: string) {
  const date = new Date(iso);
  const today = new Date();
  const yesterday = new Date();
  yesterday.setDate(today.getDate() - 1);
  const sameDay = (a: Date, b: Date) =>
    a.getFullYear() === b.getFullYear() &&
    a.getMonth() === b.getMonth() &&
    a.getDate() === b.getDate();
  if (sameDay(date, today)) return "Today";
  if (sameDay(date, yesterday)) return "Yesterday";
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {
    month: "short",
    day: "numeric",
    year: "numeric",
  }).format(date);
}

function AttachmentPreview({ url, mine }: { url: string; mine: boolean }) {
  const isImage = IMAGE_EXT.test(url);
  if (isImage) {
    return (
      <a
        href={url}
        target="_blank"
        rel="noreferrer"
        className="relative mt-2 block h-32 w-full max-w-[220px] overflow-hidden rounded-lg"
      >
        <Image src={url} alt="" fill unoptimized className="object-cover" />
      </a>
    );
  }
  return (
    <a
      href={url}
      target="_blank"
      rel="noreferrer"
      className={`mt-2 flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium underline ${
        mine ? "bg-white/10" : "bg-white"
      }`}
    >
      <Paperclip className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
      {url.split("/").pop() || "Attachment"}
    </a>
  );
}

export function ProjectChat({
  taskId,
  currentUserId,
  title,
  hint,
  sendLabel,
  isAdminView = false,
  compact = false,
  variant = "default",
  onActivity,
}: {
  taskId: string;
  currentUserId: string;
  title?: string;
  hint?: string;
  sendLabel: string;
  isAdminView?: boolean;
  compact?: boolean;
  variant?: "default" | "messages";
  onActivity?: (createdAt: string) => void;
}) {
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<Msg[]>([]);
  const [clientName, setClientName] = useState("Client");
  const [clientAvatarUrl, setClientAvatarUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [freelancerName, setFreelancerName] = useState("Freelancer");
  const [freelancerAvatarUrl, setFreelancerAvatarUrl] = useState<string | null>(
    null,
  );
  const [projectTitle, setProjectTitle] = useState("");
  const [body, setBody] = useState("");
  const [pendingAttachment, setPendingAttachment] = useState<string>("");
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [boot, setBoot] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const bottom = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const messageScroller = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const stickToLatest = useRef(true);
  const onActivityRef = useRef(onActivity);
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const cameraInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  onActivityRef.current = onActivity;

  const refresh = useCallback(
    async (silent = false) => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(`/api/tasks/${taskId}/chat`, {
          cache: "no-store",
        });
        if (!res.ok) {
          if (!silent) {
            const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
            setError(data.error || "Could not load chat");
          }
          return;
        }
        const data = await res.json();
        const next = (data.messages || []) as Msg[];
        setMessages(next);
        const latest = next[next.length - 1];
        if (latest?.createdAt) onActivityRef.current?.(latest.createdAt);
        if (data.clientName) setClientName(data.clientName);
        setClientAvatarUrl(data.clientAvatarUrl || null);
        if (data.freelancerName) setFreelancerName(data.freelancerName);
        setFreelancerAvatarUrl(data.freelancerAvatarUrl || null);
        if (data.projectTitle) setProjectTitle(data.projectTitle);
        setError("");
      } finally {
        setBoot(false);
      }
    },
    [taskId],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    setMessages([]);
    setBoot(true);
    setBody("");
    setPendingAttachment("");
    setError("");
    void refresh();
    const t = setInterval(() => void refresh(true), 2500);
    return () => clearInterval(t);
  }, [refresh]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (variant === "messages") {
      const scroller = messageScroller.current;
      if (!scroller || !stickToLatest.current) return;
      scroller.scrollTop = scroller.scrollHeight;
      return;
    }
    bottom.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth" });
  }, [messages.length, variant]);

  async function onAttach(file: File | undefined) {
    if (!file) return;
    setUploading(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      const form = new FormData();
      form.append("file", file);
      const res = await fetch("/api/upload", { method: "POST", body: form });
      const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.error || "Upload failed.");
      setPendingAttachment(data.url as string);
    } catch (uploadError) {
      setError(
        uploadError instanceof Error ? uploadError.message : "Upload failed.",
      );
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }
  }

  async function onSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    const text = body.trim();
    if ((!text && !pendingAttachment) || loading) return;
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");

    const tempId = `temp-${Date.now()}`;
    const optimistic: Msg = {
      id: tempId,
      body: text,
      attachmentUrl: pendingAttachment || null,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      senderId: currentUserId,
      displayName: isAdminView ? `You (as ${clientName})` : "You",
      isClientSide: isAdminView,
    };
    setMessages((m) => [...m, optimistic]);
    setBody("");
    const attachmentToSend = pendingAttachment;
    setPendingAttachment("");

    const res = await fetch(`/api/tasks/${taskId}/chat`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ body: text, attachmentUrl: attachmentToSend }),
    });
    const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
    setLoading(false);

    if (!res.ok) {
      setMessages((m) => m.filter((x) => x.id !== tempId));
      setBody(text);
      setPendingAttachment(attachmentToSend);
      setError(data.error || "Message failed to send");
      return;
    }

    setMessages((m) => {
      const withoutTemp = m.filter((x) => x.id !== tempId);
      if (data.message && !withoutTemp.some((x) => x.id === data.message.id)) {
        return [...withoutTemp, data.message as Msg];
      }
      return withoutTemp;
    });
    await refresh(true);
  }

  const heading =
    title ||
    (isAdminView ? `Chat as ${clientName}` : `Chat with ${clientName}`);
  const headerName = isAdminView ? freelancerName : clientName;
  const headerAvatarUrl = isAdminView ? freelancerAvatarUrl : clientAvatarUrl;
  const height = compact
    ? "max-h-64 overflow-y-auto sm:max-h-72"
    : variant === "messages"
      ? ""
      : "h-[min(420px,50dvh)] overflow-y-auto sm:h-[min(420px,55vh)]";

  const isMessages = variant === "messages";

  return (
    <div
      className={
        isMessages
          ? "flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col"
          : "flex flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-[var(--line)] bg-white"
      }
    >
      {!isMessages ? (
        <div className="border-b border-[var(--line)] bg-[var(--surface-2)] px-4 py-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <span className="relative h-9 w-9 shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-[var(--hero-deep)]">
              {headerAvatarUrl ? (
                <Image
                  src={headerAvatarUrl}
                  alt=""
                  fill
                  className="object-cover"
                  unoptimized
                />
              ) : (
                <span className="flex h-full w-full items-center justify-center text-xs font-bold text-white">
                  {headerName
                    .split(" ")
                    .map((part) => part[0])
                    .join("")
                    .slice(0, 2)
                    .toUpperCase()}
                </span>
              )}
            </span>
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <h3 className="truncate font-semibold text-[var(--ink)]">
                {heading}
              </h3>
              <p className="truncate text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                {headerName}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          {projectTitle ? (
            <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{projectTitle}</p>
          ) : null}
          {hint ? (
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">{hint}</p>
          ) : null}
          {isAdminView ? (
            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--accent)]">
              Freelancer sees you as <strong>{clientName}</strong>
            </p>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      <div
        ref={isMessages ? messageScroller : undefined}
        onScroll={
          isMessages
            ? (event) => {
                const el = event.currentTarget;
                stickToLatest.current =
                  el.scrollHeight - el.scrollTop - el.clientHeight < 80;
              }
            : undefined
        }
        className={
          isMessages
            ? "min-h-0 flex-1 space-y-4 overflow-y-auto overscroll-contain px-5 py-4 scrollbar-none"
            : `${height} space-y-3 px-4 py-4`
        }
      >
        {boot && !isMessages ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">Loading conversation…</p>
        ) : boot ? null : messages.length === 0 ? (
          <div
            className={
              isMessages
                ? "px-4 py-10 text-center"
                : "rounded-xl border border-dashed border-[var(--line)] px-4 py-8 text-center"
            }
          >
            <p
              className={
                isMessages
                  ? "font-['Poppins',_sans-serif] text-sm font-medium text-[#131313]"
                  : "text-sm font-medium text-[var(--ink)]"
              }
            >
              No messages yet
            </p>
            <p
              className={
                isMessages
                  ? "mt-1 font-['Poppins',_sans-serif] text-xs text-[#8a8a8a]"
                  : "mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]"
              }
            >
              Say assalamualaikum and confirm the scope.
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          messages.map((m, i) => {
            const mine = isAdminView ? m.isClientSide : !m.isClientSide;
            const showDivider =
              isMessages &&
              (i === 0 || dayLabel(m.createdAt) !== dayLabel(messages[i - 1].createdAt));
            return (
              <div key={m.id}>
                {showDivider ? (
                  <div className="mb-4 flex justify-center">
                    <span className="rounded-[35px] bg-[#f9f9f9] px-4 py-2 font-['Inter',_sans-serif] text-sm font-medium leading-[17px] text-[#979797]">
                      {dayLabel(m.createdAt)}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                ) : null}
                {isMessages ? (
                  <div
                    className={`flex flex-col gap-1.5 ${mine ? "items-end" : "items-start"}`}
                  >
                    <div
                      className={`max-w-[421px] px-3 py-3 font-['Poppins',_sans-serif] text-[16px] leading-6 text-[#131313] ${
                        mine &&
                        m.body.length > 0 &&

                            ? "rounded-[8px] bg-[#c6e9a0]"

                    >
                      {m.body ? (
                        <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap">{m.body}</p>
                      ) : null}
                      {m.attachmentUrl ? (
                        <AttachmentPreview url={m.attachmentUrl} mine={mine} />
                      ) : null}
                    </div>
                    <p

                        mine ? "text-right" : "text-left"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {timeOnly(m.createdAt)}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <div
                    className={`flex ${mine ? "justify-end" : "justify-start"}`}

                      <p

                          mine ? "text-white/80" : "text-[var(--muted)]"

                      >

                        <span className="font-normal opacity-80">
                          {" "}

                        <p className="mt-1 whitespace-pre-wrap leading-relaxed">
                          {m.body}
                        </p>
                      ) : null}

                        <AttachmentPreview url={m.attachmentUrl} mine={mine} />

      </div>

            ? "mt-auto shrink-0 px-5 pb-8 pt-3"

        }
      >
        {isMessages ? (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">

                  alt=""
                  width={24}
                  height={24}
                />

                value={body}
                onChange={(e) => setBody(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Send msg here..."
                onKeyDown={(e) => {
                  if (e.key === "Enter") {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    e.currentTarget.form?.requestSubmit();
                  }
                }}
                className="min-w-0 flex-1 bg-transparent font-['Inter',_sans-serif] text-[16px] text-[#131313] outline-none placeholder:text-[#979797]"
              />
              <input
                ref={cameraInputRef}
                type="file"
                accept="image/*"
                capture="environment"
                className="sr-only"
                disabled={uploading}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  void onAttach(e.target.files?.[0]);
                  e.target.value = "";
                }}
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => cameraInputRef.current?.click()}
                disabled={uploading}
                aria-label="Attach photo"
                className="flex h-6 w-6 shrink-0 items-center justify-center disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                <Image
                  src="/messages/camera.svg"
                  alt=""
                  width={24}
                  height={24}
                />
              </button>
            </div>
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading || uploading || (!body.trim() && !pendingAttachment)}
              aria-label={sendLabel}
              className="flex h-[60px] w-[60px] shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-[30px] bg-[#004743] transition-colors hover:bg-[#00332f] disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              <Image src="/messages/send.svg" alt="" width={32} height={32} />
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <Textarea
            value={body}
            onChange={(e) => setBody(e.target.value)}
            rows={2}
            placeholder={

            }
            onKeyDown={(e) => {
              if (e.key === "Enter" && !e.shiftKey) {
                e.preventDefault();

            <Paperclip className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            {pendingAttachment.split("/").pop()}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setPendingAttachment("")}
              aria-label="Remove attachment"
            >
              <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : null}
        {uploading ? (
          <p
            className={
              isMessages
                ? "mt-2 font-['Poppins',_sans-serif] text-xs text-[#8a8a8a]"
                : "text-xs text-[var(--muted)]"
            }
          >
            Uploading…
          </p>
        ) : null}
        {error ? <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-red-700">{error}</p> : null}

        {!isMessages ? (
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <label className="flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-1 text-[11px] text-[var(--muted)]">
              <input
                ref={fileInputRef}
                type="file"
                className="sr-only"

                  void onAttach(e.target.files?.[0]);
                  e.target.value = "";
                }}
              />
              <Paperclip className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> Attach
            </label>
            <Button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading || uploading || (!body.trim() && !pendingAttachment)}
            >
              {loading ? "Sending…" : sendLabel}
            </Button>
          </div>
        ) : null}
      </form>

  );
}
