"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import Image from "next/image";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Search } from "lucide-react";
import { ProjectChat } from "@/components/project-chat";
import { LocalTime } from "@/components/dashboard/local-time";
import { initialsFromClientName } from "@/lib/client-profile";

const FAVOURITES_KEY = "wt-message-favourites";

export type ConversationRow = {
  taskId: string;
  name: string;
  avatarUrl: string | null;
  taskTitle: string;
  lastAt: string;
  unread: boolean;
  country: string | null;
  city: string | null;
  profileCountry: string | null;
  memberSince: string | null;
  bidHref: string | null;
};

function timeLabel(iso: string) {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {
    hour: "numeric",
    minute: "2-digit",
    hour12: true,
  })
    .format(new Date(iso))
    .toLowerCase();
}

function Avatar({
  url,
  name,
  size,
}: {
  url: string | null;
  name: string;
  size: number;
}) {
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);
  const photo = url?.trim() && !failed ? url.trim() : "";

  return (
    <span
      className="relative inline-block shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-[var(--hero-deep)]"
      style={{ width: size, height: size }}
    >
      {photo ? (
        <Image
          src={photo}
          alt=""
          fill
          unoptimized
          className="object-cover"
          onError={() => setFailed(true)}
        />
      ) : (
        <span
          className="flex h-full w-full items-center justify-center font-['Inter',_sans-serif] font-semibold text-white"
          style={{ fontSize: Math.max(12, Math.round(size * 0.3)) }}
        >
          {initialsFromClientName(name)}
        </span>
      )}
    </span>
  );
}

export function RefreshThreadList() {
  const router = useRouter();

  useEffect(() => {
    const id = window.setInterval(() => {
      if (document.visibilityState === "visible") router.refresh();
    }, 4000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(id);
  }, [router]);

  return null;
}

export function MessagesInbox({
  conversations,
  selectedTaskId,
  hasExplicitSelection,
  currentUserId,
}: {
  conversations: ConversationRow[];
  selectedTaskId: string | null;
  hasExplicitSelection: boolean;
  currentUserId: string;
}) {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<"all" | "unread" | "favourite">("all");
  const [favourites, setFavourites] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [rows, setRows] = useState(conversations);
  const [activeId, setActiveId] = useState(selectedTaskId);
  const [mobileList, setMobileList] = useState(!hasExplicitSelection);

  useEffect(() => {
    setRows(conversations);
  }, [conversations]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setActiveId(selectedTaskId);
  }, [selectedTaskId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(FAVOURITES_KEY);
      if (raw) setFavourites(new Set(JSON.parse(raw)));
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  }, []);

  function toggleFavourite(taskId: string) {
    setFavourites((prev) => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      if (next.has(taskId)) next.delete(taskId);
      else next.add(taskId);
      try {
        window.localStorage.setItem(
          FAVOURITES_KEY,
          JSON.stringify(Array.from(next)),
        );
      } catch {
        // ignore
      }
      return next;
    });
  }

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const term = search.trim().toLowerCase();
    return rows.filter((c) => {
      if (filter === "unread" && !c.unread) return false;
      if (filter === "favourite" && !favourites.has(c.taskId)) return false;
      if (term && !c.name.toLowerCase().includes(term)) return false;
      return true;
    });
  }, [rows, filter, favourites, search]);

  function noteActivity(createdAt: string) {
    if (!activeId) return;
    setRows((current) =>
      current
        .map((row) =>
          row.taskId === activeId ? { ...row, lastAt: createdAt } : row,
        )
        .sort(
          (a, b) => new Date(b.lastAt).getTime() - new Date(a.lastAt).getTime(),
        ),
    );
  }

  function selectThread(taskId: string) {
    setActiveId(taskId);
    setMobileList(false);
    window.history.replaceState(null, "", `/messages?task=${taskId}`);
  }

  const active = rows.find((row) => row.taskId === activeId) || rows[0] || null;
  const mobileChatOpen = !mobileList && Boolean(active);
  const location = active
    ? [active.city, active.profileCountry]
        .filter((part) => part && !/^not specified$/i.test(part.trim()))
        .join(", ")
    : "";

  return (
    <div className="relative flex flex-col gap-2 overflow-anchor-none lg:block lg:h-[749px] lg:overflow-hidden">
      <aside
        className={`h-[min(749px,calc(100dvh-9rem))] flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-[16px] border border-[#c6e9a0] bg-white lg:absolute lg:inset-y-0 lg:left-0 lg:h-[749px] lg:w-[350px] ${
          mobileChatOpen ? "hidden lg:flex" : "flex"
        }`}
      >
        <div className="shrink-0 px-4 pt-4">
          <p className="font-['Inter',_sans-serif] text-[24px] font-medium leading-8 text-[#131313] sm:text-[32px] sm:leading-[39px]">
            Messages
          </p>
          <div className="mt-4 flex h-12 items-center gap-2 rounded-[50px] bg-[#f9f9f9] px-3">
            <Search className="h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-[#979797]" strokeWidth={1.75} />
            <input
              value={search}
              onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Search here..."
              aria-label="Search here..."
              className="min-w-0 flex-1 bg-transparent font-['Inter',_sans-serif] text-[16px] leading-[19px] text-[#131313] outline-none placeholder:text-[#979797]"
            />
          </div>
          <div className="mt-[23px] flex gap-2">
            {(
              [
                { id: "all", label: "All" },
                { id: "unread", label: "Unread" },
                { id: "favourite", label: "Favourite" },
              ] as const
            ).map((item) => {
              const active = filter === item.id;
              return (
                <button
                  key={item.id}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setFilter(item.id)}
                  className={`inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-[40px] px-6 font-['Poppins',_sans-serif] text-[16px] leading-6 text-[#004743] ${
                    active
                      ? "h-10 bg-[#c6e9a0] font-medium"
                      : "h-[42px] border border-[#f3f3f3] bg-white font-normal"
                  }`}
                >
                  {item.label}
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-6 min-h-0 flex-1 space-y-2 overflow-y-auto px-2 pb-4 scrollbar-none">
          {filtered.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="px-3 py-6 text-center font-['Poppins',_sans-serif] text-sm text-[#8a8a8a]">
              No conversations found.
            </p>
          ) : (
            filtered.map((c) => {
              const selectedRow = c.taskId === active?.taskId;
              return (
                <button
                  key={c.taskId}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => selectThread(c.taskId)}
                  className={`grid h-[74px] w-full grid-cols-[54px_minmax(0,1fr)_auto] items-center gap-3 rounded-[12px] px-3 text-left ${
                    selectedRow ? "bg-[#f9f9f9]" : "hover:bg-[#f9f9f9]"
                  }`}
                >
                  <span className="relative h-[54px] w-[54px]">
                    <Avatar url={c.avatarUrl} name={c.name} size={54} />
                    {c.unread ? (
                      <span className="absolute bottom-0.5 right-0.5 h-3.5 w-3.5 rounded-full border-2 border-[#f9f9f9] bg-[#0ed204]" />
                    ) : null}
                  </span>
                  <span className="truncate font-['Inter',_sans-serif] text-[18px] font-medium leading-[22px] text-[#131313]">
                    {c.name}
                  </span>
                  <span
                    className={`font-['Poppins',_sans-serif] text-sm leading-[21px] ${
                      selectedRow ? "text-[#131313]" : "text-[#7f7f7f]"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {timeLabel(c.lastAt)}
                  </span>
                </button>
              );
            })
          )}
        </div>
      </aside>

      <section
        className={`h-[min(749px,calc(100dvh-9rem))] min-w-0 flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-[16px] border border-[#c6e9a0] bg-white lg:absolute lg:inset-y-0 lg:left-[358px] lg:right-[349px] lg:h-[749px] ${
          mobileChatOpen ? "flex" : "hidden lg:flex"
        }`}
      >
        {active ? (
          <>
            <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center justify-between gap-3 px-5 pb-4 pt-4">
              <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setMobileList(true)}
                  className="font-['Poppins',_sans-serif] text-xs font-semibold text-[#004743] lg:hidden"
                >
                  ← Inbox
                </button>
                <Avatar
                  url={active.avatarUrl}
                  name={active.name}
                  size={54}
                />
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <p className="truncate font-['Poppins',_sans-serif] text-[18px] font-medium leading-[27px] text-[#131313]">
                    {active.name}
                  </p>
                  <p className="truncate font-['Inter',_sans-serif] text-sm font-medium leading-[17px] text-[#979797]">
                    {active.taskTitle}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => toggleFavourite(active.taskId)}
                aria-label={
                  favourites.has(active.taskId)
                    ? "Remove from favourites"
                    : "Add to favourites"
                }
                className="flex h-[50px] w-[50px] shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#f9f9f9]"
              >
                <Image
                  src={
                    favourites.has(active.taskId)
                      ? "/messages/heart-filled.svg"
                      : "/messages/heart.svg"
                  }
                  alt=""
                  width={24}
                  height={24}
                />
              </button>
            </div>
            <div className="h-px shrink-0 bg-[#f7f7f7]" />
            <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col overflow-hidden">
              <ProjectChat
                key={active.taskId}
                taskId={active.taskId}
                currentUserId={currentUserId}
                sendLabel="Send"
                variant="messages"
                onActivity={noteActivity}
              />
            </div>
          </>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 p-8 text-center">
            <p className="font-['Poppins',_sans-serif] text-lg font-medium text-[#131313]">
              {conversations.length ? "Select a conversation" : "No conversations yet"}
            </p>
            <p className="max-w-sm font-['Poppins',_sans-serif] text-sm text-[#7f7f7f]">
              {conversations.length
                ? "Choose a client on the left to read and send messages."
                : "When a client hires you, the thread appears here."}
            </p>
            {conversations.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-3">
                <Link
                  href="/find-work"
                  className="rounded-[56px] bg-[#004743] px-6 py-2.5 font-['Poppins',_sans-serif] text-sm font-medium text-white"
                >
                  Find work
                </Link>
                <Link
                  href="/my-work/proposals"
                  className="rounded-[56px] border border-[#c6e9a0] px-6 py-2.5 font-['Poppins',_sans-serif] text-sm font-medium text-[#004743]"
                >
                  My proposals
                </Link>
              </div>
            ) : null}
          </div>
        )}
      </section>

      {active ? (
        <aside className="hidden h-[749px] w-[350px] shrink-0 flex-col items-center overflow-hidden rounded-[16px] border border-[#c6e9a0] bg-white px-6 pt-8 text-center lg:absolute lg:inset-y-0 lg:right-0 lg:flex">
          <Avatar url={active.avatarUrl} name={active.name} size={100} />
          <p className="mt-4 font-['Poppins',_sans-serif] text-[20px] font-medium leading-[30px] text-[#131313]">
            {active.name}
          </p>
          <p className="mt-0.5 h-[21px] font-['Poppins',_sans-serif] text-sm leading-[21px] text-[#626262]">
            {active.country ? <LocalTime country={active.country} /> : null}
          </p>
          {location ? (
            <p className="mt-3 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 font-['Poppins',_sans-serif] text-[16px] leading-6 text-[#7f7f7f]">
              <span>{location}</span>
              <Image src="/messages/location.svg" alt="" width={22} height={22} />
            </p>
          ) : (
            <p className="mt-3 h-6" />
          )}
          {active.bidHref ? (
            <Link
              href={active.bidHref}
              className="mt-8 inline-flex h-11 items-center gap-2 rounded-[56px] border border-[#c6e9a0] bg-white px-8 font-['Poppins',_sans-serif] text-[16px] font-medium leading-6 text-[#004743]"
            >
              View Proposal
              <Image src="/messages/view.svg" alt="" width={20} height={20} />
            </Link>
          ) : null}
          {active.memberSince ? (
            <p className="mt-3 font-['Poppins',_sans-serif] text-xs leading-[18px] text-[#7f7f7f]">
              Member since {active.memberSince}
            </p>
          ) : null}
        </aside>
      ) : (
        <aside className="hidden h-[749px] w-[350px] rounded-[16px] border border-[#c6e9a0] bg-white lg:absolute lg:inset-y-0 lg:right-0 lg:block" />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import {
  FormEvent,
  useCallback,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useRef,
  useState,
  useTransition,
} from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { cn } from "@/components/ui";

export type AdminChatThread = {
  taskId: string;
  displayId: string;
  projectTitle: string;
  clientName: string;
  freelancerName: string;
  lastPreview: string;
  lastAt: string;
  unreadCount: number;
  flagged: boolean;
  closed: boolean;
};

type ChatMsg = {
  id: string;
  body: string;
  attachmentUrl?: string | null;
  createdAt: string;
  senderId: string;
  displayName: string;
  isClientSide: boolean;
};

type TabFilter = "ALL" | "UNREAD" | "FLAGGED" | "CLOSED";

const AVATAR_COLORS = [
  "bg-[#C6E9A0] text-[#004743]",
  "bg-[#D4F0E0] text-[#004743]",
  "bg-[#FFE0B8] text-[#8A4B00]",
  "bg-[#D6E4FF] text-[#1E3A8A]",
  "bg-[#F5D0FE] text-[#86198F]",
];

function initials(name: string) {
  const parts = name.trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  if (parts.length === 0) return "?";
  if (parts.length === 1) return parts[0]!.slice(0, 1).toUpperCase();
  return `${parts[0]!.slice(0, 1)}${parts[1]!.slice(0, 1)}`.toUpperCase();
}

function avatarColor(seed: string) {
  let hash = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < seed.length; i++) {
    hash = (hash * 31 + seed.charCodeAt(i)) >>> 0;
  }
  return AVATAR_COLORS[hash % AVATAR_COLORS.length]!;
}

function timeLabel(iso: string) {
  const date = new Date(iso);
  const now = new Date();
  const sameDay =
    date.getFullYear() === now.getFullYear() &&
    date.getMonth() === now.getMonth() &&
    date.getDate() === now.getDate();
  if (sameDay) {
    return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", {
      hour: "2-digit",
      minute: "2-digit",
      hour12: false,
    }).format(date);
  }
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", {
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "short",
  }).format(date);
}

function messageTime(iso: string) {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", {
    hour: "2-digit",
    minute: "2-digit",
    hour12: false,
  }).format(new Date(iso));
}

function pairLabel(client: string, freelancer: string) {
  const left = client.trim() || "Client";
  const right = freelancer.trim() || "Freelancer";
  return `${left} ↔ ${right}`;
}

function truncate(text: string, max: number) {
  const t = text.trim();
  if (t.length <= max) return t;
  return `${t.slice(0, max - 1)}…`;
}

export function AdminClientChat({
  threads: initialThreads,
  initialTaskId,
  adminUserId,
}: {
  threads: AdminChatThread[];
  initialTaskId?: string | null;
  adminUserId: string;
}) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [, startTransition] = useTransition();
  const [threads, setThreads] = useState(initialThreads);
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<TabFilter>("ALL");
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string | null>(
    initialTaskId && initialThreads.some((t) => t.taskId === initialTaskId)
      ? initialTaskId
      : initialThreads[0]?.taskId ?? null,
  );
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<ChatMsg[]>([]);
  const [boot, setBoot] = useState(true);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [sending, setSending] = useState(false);
  const [body, setBody] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [actionBusy, setActionBusy] = useState(false);
  const bottomRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const scrollerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const stickToLatest = useRef(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    setThreads(initialThreads);
  }, [initialThreads]);

  const counts = useMemo(
    () => ({
      ALL: threads.filter((t) => !t.closed).length,
      UNREAD: threads.filter((t) => !t.closed && t.unreadCount > 0).length,
      FLAGGED: threads.filter((t) => !t.closed && t.flagged).length,
      CLOSED: threads.filter((t) => t.closed).length,
    }),
    [threads],
  );

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    return threads.filter((t) => {
      if (tab === "ALL" && t.closed) return false;
      if (tab === "UNREAD" && (t.closed || t.unreadCount === 0)) return false;
      if (tab === "FLAGGED" && (t.closed || !t.flagged)) return false;
      if (tab === "CLOSED" && !t.closed) return false;
      if (!q) return true;
      return (
        t.projectTitle.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        t.clientName.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        t.freelancerName.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        t.displayId.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        t.lastPreview.toLowerCase().includes(q)
      );
    });
  }, [threads, tab, query]);

  const selected =
    filtered.find((t) => t.taskId === selectedId) ||
    threads.find((t) => t.taskId === selectedId) ||
    null;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selectedId) return;
    if (filtered.some((t) => t.taskId === selectedId)) return;
    setSelectedId(filtered[0]?.taskId ?? null);
  }, [filtered, selectedId]);

  const refreshMessages = useCallback(
    async (taskId: string, silent = false) => {
      if (!silent) setLoading(true);
      try {
        const res = await fetch(`/api/tasks/${taskId}/chat`, {
          cache: "no-store",
        });
        const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        if (!res.ok) {
          if (!silent) {
            setError(
              typeof data.error === "string"
                ? data.error
                : "Could not load chat",
            );
          }
          return;
        }
        setMessages((data.messages || []) as ChatMsg[]);
        setThreads((prev) =>
          prev.map((t) =>
            t.taskId === taskId
              ? {
                  ...t,
                  flagged: Boolean(data.flagged),
                  closed: Boolean(data.closed),
                  unreadCount: 0,
                }
              : t,
          ),
        );
        setError(null);
      } finally {
        setBoot(false);
        setLoading(false);
      }
    },
    [],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selectedId) {
      setMessages([]);
      setBoot(false);
      return;
    }
    setBoot(true);
    setMessages([]);
    setBody("");
    setError(null);
    stickToLatest.current = true;
    void refreshMessages(selectedId);
    const id = window.setInterval(() => {
      void refreshMessages(selectedId, true);
    }, 2500);
    return () => window.clearInterval(id);
  }, [selectedId, refreshMessages]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!stickToLatest.current) return;
    const scroller = scrollerRef.current;
    if (scroller) {
      scroller.scrollTop = scroller.scrollHeight;
    } else {
      bottomRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth" });
    }
  }, [messages.length, selectedId]);

  async function patchThread(patch: { flagged?: boolean; closed?: boolean }) {
    if (!selectedId) return;
    setActionBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/chat/${selectedId}`, {
        method: "PATCH",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify(patch),
      });
      const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(
          typeof data.error === "string" ? data.error : "Update failed",
        );
      }
      setThreads((prev) =>
        prev.map((t) =>
          t.taskId === selectedId
            ? {
                ...t,
                flagged:
                  typeof data.flagged === "boolean" ? data.flagged : t.flagged,
                closed:
                  typeof data.closed === "boolean" ? data.closed : t.closed,
              }
            : t,
        ),
      );
      startTransition(() => router.refresh());
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Update failed");
    } finally {
      setActionBusy(false);
    }
  }

  async function onSend(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!selectedId || !selected || selected.closed) return;
    const text = body.trim();
    if (!text || sending) return;

    setSending(true);
    setError(null);
    const tempId = `temp-${Date.now()}`;
    const optimistic: ChatMsg = {
      id: tempId,
      body: text,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      senderId: adminUserId,
      displayName: selected.clientName,
      isClientSide: true,
    };
    setMessages((m) => [...m, optimistic]);
    setBody("");
    stickToLatest.current = true;

    const res = await fetch(`/api/tasks/${selectedId}/chat`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ body: text }),
    });
    const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
    setSending(false);

    if (!res.ok) {
      setMessages((m) => m.filter((x) => x.id !== tempId));
      setBody(text);
      setError(
        typeof data.error === "string" ? data.error : "Message failed to send",
      );
      return;
    }

    setMessages((m) => {
      const without = m.filter((x) => x.id !== tempId);
      if (data.message && !without.some((x) => x.id === data.message.id)) {
        return [...without, data.message as ChatMsg];
      }
      return without;
    });

    setThreads((prev) => {
      const next = prev.map((t) =>
        t.taskId === selectedId
          ? {
              ...t,
              lastPreview: text,
              lastAt: new Date().toISOString(),
              unreadCount: 0,
            }
          : t,
      );
      next.sort(
        (a, b) => new Date(b.lastAt).getTime() - new Date(a.lastAt).getTime(),
      );
      return next;
    });
  }

  const tabs: { key: TabFilter; label: string; count?: number }[] = [
    { key: "ALL", label: "All" },
    { key: "UNREAD", label: "Unread", count: counts.UNREAD },
    { key: "FLAGGED", label: "Flagged", count: counts.FLAGGED },
    { key: "CLOSED", label: "Closed" },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="min-w-0 max-w-full space-y-5 font-['Inter',_sans-serif]">
      <div className="min-w-0">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold leading-8 text-[#1E1E1E]">
          Client Chat
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm leading-5 text-[#626262]">
          Monitor client-freelancer conversations, step in as admin, and act on
          flagged threads.
        </p>
      </div>

      {error ? (
        <p className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
          {error}
        </p>
      ) : null}

      <section className="flex min-h-[min(680px,calc(100dvh-220px))] min-w-0 max-w-full overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-[#EFEFEF] bg-white">
        {/* Thread list */}
        <aside
          className={cn(
            "flex w-full max-w-[340px] shrink-0 flex-col border-r border-[#EFEFEF] md:w-[320px] lg:w-[340px]",
            selectedId ? "max-md:hidden" : "max-md:max-w-full",
          )}
        >
          <div className="shrink-0 space-y-3 border-b border-[#EFEFEF] p-3 sm:p-4">
            <div className="relative">
              <svg
                className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[#9A9A9A]"
                width="16"
                height="16"
                viewBox="0 0 16 16"
                fill="none"
                aria-hidden
              >
                <circle
                  cx="7"
                  cy="7"
                  r="5"
                  stroke="currentColor"
                  strokeWidth="1.4"
                />
                <path
                  d="M11 11l3 3"
                  stroke="currentColor"
                  strokeWidth="1.4"
                  strokeLinecap="round"
                />
              </svg>
              <input
                value={query}
                onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Search people or projects..."
                className="h-10 w-full rounded-lg border border-[#EFEFEF] bg-[#F9F9F9] pl-9 pr-3 text-sm text-[#1E1E1E] outline-none placeholder:text-[#9A9A9A] focus:border-[#C6E9A0] focus:bg-white"
              />
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1 overflow-x-auto">
              {tabs.map((item) => {
                const active = tab === item.key;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={item.key}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setTab(item.key)}
                    className={cn(
                      "shrink-0 border-b-2 px-2.5 pb-2 pt-1 text-sm font-medium transition-colors",
                      active
                        ? "border-[#004743] text-[#004743]"
                        : "border-transparent text-[#626262] hover:text-[#1E1E1E]",
                    )}
                  >
                    {item.label}
                    {item.count != null && item.count > 0 ? (
                      <span className="ms-1 text-[#9A9A9A]">{item.count}</span>
                    ) : null}
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto">
            {filtered.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="px-4 py-10 text-center text-sm text-[#626262]">
                No conversations match this filter.
              </p>
            ) : (
              filtered.map((thread) => {
                const active = thread.taskId === selectedId;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={thread.taskId}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setSelectedId(thread.taskId)}
                    className={cn(
                      "flex w-full gap-3 border-b border-[#F3F3F3] px-3 py-3 text-left transition-colors sm:px-4",
                      active
                        ? "bg-[#E8F5D9]"
                        : "bg-white hover:bg-[#F9F9F9]",
                    )}
                  >
                    <span
                      className={cn(
                        "mt-0.5 flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-xs font-semibold",
                        avatarColor(thread.clientName),
                      )}
                    >
                      {initials(thread.clientName)}
                    </span>
                    <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                        <p className="truncate text-sm font-semibold leading-5 text-[#1E1E1E]">
                          {truncate(
                            pairLabel(
                              thread.clientName,
                              thread.freelancerName,
                            ),
                            28,
                          )}
                        </p>
                        <span className="shrink-0 text-xs leading-5 text-[#9A9A9A]">
                          {timeLabel(thread.lastAt)}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <p className="mt-0.5 truncate text-xs leading-4 text-[#626262]">
                        {thread.projectTitle}
                      </p>
                      <div className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-2">
                        <p className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-xs leading-4 text-[#9A9A9A]">
                          {thread.lastPreview || "No messages yet"}
                        </p>
                        <span className="inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5">
                          {thread.flagged ? (
                            <span
                              className="inline-flex text-[#DC2626]"
                              title="Flagged"
                              aria-label="Flagged"
                            >
                              <FlagIcon />
                            </span>
                          ) : null}
                          {thread.closed ? (
                            <span className="rounded-full bg-[#F3F4F6] px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium text-[#626262]">
                              Closed
                            </span>
                          ) : null}
                          {!thread.closed && thread.unreadCount > 0 ? (
                            <span className="inline-flex h-5 min-w-5 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#004743] px-1.5 text-[10px] font-semibold text-white">
                              {thread.unreadCount > 99
                                ? "99+"
                                : thread.unreadCount}
                            </span>
                          ) : null}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </button>
                );
              })
            )}
          </div>
        </aside>

        <div
          className={cn(
            "flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col",
            !selectedId ? "max-md:hidden" : "max-md:w-full",
          )}
        >
          {!selected ? (
            <div className="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center px-6 text-center">
              <div>
                <p className="text-sm font-medium text-[#1E1E1E]">
                  Select a conversation
                </p>
                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-[#626262]">
                  Choose a thread from the list to monitor or reply.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <>
              <header className="flex shrink-0 flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3 border-b border-[#EFEFEF] px-4 py-3 sm:px-5">
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setSelectedId(null)}
                    className="mb-2 text-sm font-medium text-[#004743] md:hidden"
                  >
                    ← Back to list
                  </button>
                  <h2 className="truncate text-base font-semibold leading-6 text-[#1E1E1E]">
                    {pairLabel(selected.clientName, selected.freelancerName)}
                  </h2>
                  <p className="mt-0.5 truncate text-xs leading-4 text-[#626262]">
                    {selected.projectTitle} · {selected.displayId}
                  </p>
                </div>
                <div className="flex shrink-0 flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                  {selected.closed ? (
                    <span className="inline-flex h-9 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg bg-[#F3F4F6] px-2.5 text-xs font-medium text-[#626262]">
                      <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#9A9A9A]" />
                      Closed
                    </span>
                  ) : (
                    <span className="inline-flex h-9 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg bg-[#DBEAFE] px-2.5 text-xs font-medium text-[#1D4ED8]">
                      <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#3B82F6]" />
                      Open
                    </span>
                  )}
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    disabled={actionBusy}
                    onClick={() =>
                      void patchThread({ flagged: !selected.flagged })
                    }
                    className={cn(
                      "inline-flex h-9 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border px-3 text-sm font-medium transition-colors disabled:opacity-50",
                      selected.flagged
                        ? "border-[#FECACA] bg-white text-[#DC2626] hover:bg-[#FEF2F2]"
                        : "border-[#EFEFEF] bg-white text-[#1E1E1E] hover:bg-[#F9F9F9]",
                    )}
                  >
                    <FlagIcon />
                    {selected.flagged ? "Unflag" : "Flag"}
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    disabled={actionBusy}
                    onClick={() =>
                      void patchThread({ closed: !selected.closed })
                    }
                    className="inline-flex h-9 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-[#EFEFEF] bg-white px-3 text-sm font-medium text-[#1E1E1E] transition-colors hover:bg-[#F9F9F9] disabled:opacity-50"
                  >
                    <LockIcon />
                    {selected.closed ? "Reopen" : "Close"}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </header>

              <div
                ref={scrollerRef}
                onScroll={(e) => {
                  const el = e.currentTarget;
                  stickToLatest.current =
                    el.scrollHeight - el.scrollTop - el.clientHeight < 80;
                }}
                className="min-h-0 flex-1 space-y-5 overflow-y-auto px-4 py-4 sm:px-5"
              >
                {boot || loading ? (
                  <p className="py-10 text-center text-sm text-[#626262]">
                    Loading conversation…
                  </p>
                ) : messages.length === 0 ? (
                  <p className="py-10 text-center text-sm text-[#626262]">
                    No messages yet in this thread.
                  </p>
                ) : (
                  messages.map((m) => {
                    const isClient = m.isClientSide;
                    const name = isClient
                      ? selected.clientName
                      : selected.freelancerName || m.displayName;
                    return (
                      <div
                        key={m.id}
                        className={cn(
                          "flex gap-2.5",
                          isClient ? "justify-start" : "justify-end",
                        )}
                      >
                        {isClient ? (
                          <span
                            className={cn(
                              "mt-5 flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-[10px] font-semibold",
                              avatarColor(name),
                            )}
                          >
                            {initials(name)}
                          </span>
                        ) : null}
                        <div
                          className={cn(
                            "flex max-w-[min(100%,420px)] flex-col",
                            isClient ? "items-start" : "items-end",
                          )}
                        >
                          <p className="mb-1 text-xs leading-4 text-[#9A9A9A]">
                            {name} · {messageTime(m.createdAt)}
                          </p>
                          <div
                            className={cn(
                              "rounded-2xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm leading-5",
                              isClient
                                ? "rounded-tl-md border border-[#EFEFEF] bg-white text-[#1E1E1E]"
                                : "rounded-tr-md bg-[#004743] text-white",
                            )}
                          >
                            {m.body ? (
                              <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap">{m.body}</p>
                            ) : null}
                            {m.attachmentUrl ? (
                              <a
                                href={m.attachmentUrl}
                                target="_blank"
                                rel="noreferrer"
                                className={cn(
                                  "mt-2 block text-xs underline",
                                  isClient ? "text-[#004743]" : "text-white/90",
                                )}
                              >
                                {m.attachmentUrl.split("/").pop() ||
                                  "Attachment"}
                              </a>
                            ) : null}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                        {!isClient ? (
                          <span
                            className={cn(
                              "mt-5 flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-[10px] font-semibold",
                              avatarColor(name),
                            )}
                          >
                            {initials(name)}
                          </span>
                        ) : null}
                      </div>
                    );
                  })
                )}
                <div ref={bottomRef} />
              </div>

              <form
                onSubmit={onSend}
                className="shrink-0 border-t border-[#EFEFEF] px-4 py-3 sm:px-5"
              >
                {selected.closed ? (
                  <p className="rounded-lg bg-[#F9F9F9] px-3 py-3 text-center text-sm text-[#626262]">
                    This conversation is closed. Reopen it to send a message.
                  </p>
                ) : (
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <input
                      value={body}
                      onChange={(e) => setBody(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="Message both parties as admin..."
                      disabled={sending}
                      className="h-11 min-w-0 flex-1 rounded-lg border border-[#EFEFEF] bg-[#F9F9F9] px-3.5 text-sm text-[#1E1E1E] outline-none placeholder:text-[#9A9A9A] focus:border-[#C6E9A0] focus:bg-white disabled:opacity-60"
                    />
                    <button
                      type="submit"
                      disabled={sending || !body.trim()}
                      className="inline-flex h-11 shrink-0 items-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-[#004743] px-4 text-sm font-medium text-white transition-colors hover:bg-[#003832] disabled:opacity-50"
                    >
                      <SendIcon />
                      Send
                    </button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </form>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

import type { NextAuthOptions } from "next-auth";
import CredentialsProvider from "next-auth/providers/credentials";
import GoogleProvider from "next-auth/providers/google";
import AppleProvider from "next-auth/providers/apple";
import bcrypt from "bcryptjs";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { rateLimit } from "@/lib/rate-limit";
import { resolveProviderAccount } from "@/lib/google-account";

const providers: NextAuthOptions["providers"] = [
  CredentialsProvider({
    name: "credentials",
    credentials: {
      email: { label: "Email", type: "email" },
      password: { label: "Password", type: "password" },
    },
    async authorize(credentials) {
      if (!credentials?.email || !credentials.password) return null;

      const login = credentials.email.toLowerCase().trim();
      const email =
        login === "admin" || login === "admin@trustwork.pk" ? "admin" : login;

      const limited = rateLimit(`login:${email}`, 8, 15 * 60 * 1000);
      if (!limited.ok) {
        throw new Error(
          `Too many login attempts. Try again in ${limited.retryAfterSec}s.`,
        );
      }

      const user = await prisma.user.findUnique({
        where: { email },
        select: {
          id: true,
          email: true,
          name: true,
          role: true,
          status: true,
          suspendedReason: true,
          passwordHash: true,
          emailVerified: true,
        },
      });
      if (!user) return null;

      if (user.status === "SUSPENDED") {
        throw new Error(
          user.suspendedReason ||
            "Your account is suspended. Contact support.",
        );
      }

      const valid = await bcrypt.compare(
        credentials.password,
        user.passwordHash,
      );
      if (!valid) return null;

      if (!user.emailVerified) {
        throw new Error("Please verify your email before logging in.");
      }

      return {
        id: user.id,
        email: user.email,
        name: user.name,
        role: user.role,
      };
    },
  }),
  GoogleProvider({
    clientId: process.env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID!,
    clientSecret: process.env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET!,
  }),
];

if (process.env.APPLE_ID?.trim() && process.env.APPLE_SECRET?.trim()) {
  providers.push(
    AppleProvider({
      clientId: process.env.APPLE_ID,
      clientSecret: process.env.APPLE_SECRET,
    }),
  );
}

export const authOptions: NextAuthOptions = {
  session: { strategy: "jwt" },
  pages: {
    signIn: "/login",
  },
  providers,
  callbacks: {
    async signIn({ user, account }) {
      if (account?.provider !== "google" && account?.provider !== "apple") {
        return true;
      }

      const resolved = await resolveProviderAccount({
        provider: account.provider as "google" | "apple",
        providerAccountId: account.providerAccountId,
        profile: { email: user.email, name: user.name, image: user.image },
      });
      if (!resolved) return false;

      user.id = resolved.id;
      user.role = resolved.role;

      return true;
    },
    async jwt({ token, user }) {
      if (user) {
        token.id = user.id;
        token.role = (user as { role?: string }).role;
      }
      return token;
    },
    async session({ session, token }) {
      if (session.user) {
        session.user.id = token.id as string;
        session.user.role = token.role as string;
      }
      return session;
    },
    async redirect({ url, baseUrl }) {
      const profileUrl = `${baseUrl}/dashboard`;
      if (url.startsWith("/")) {
        return url === "/" ? profileUrl : url;
      }
      try {
        const parsed = new URL(url);
        if (parsed.origin === baseUrl) {
          return parsed.pathname === "/" ? profileUrl : url;
        }
      } catch {
        // ignore malformed url
      }
      return profileUrl;
    },
  },
};

import { getServerSession } from "next-auth";
import { authOptions } from "@/lib/auth";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

export async function getSession() {
  return getServerSession(authOptions);
}

export async function requireUser() {
  const session = await getSession();
  if (!session?.user?.id) redirect("/login");
  // Admins use /admin only — keep public app for freelancers
  if (session.user.role === "ADMIN") redirect("/admin");
  if (session.user.role === "SUSPENDED") redirect("/login?error=suspended");

  const user = await prisma.user.findUnique({
    where: { id: session.user.id },
    select: { status: true, suspendedReason: true },
  });
  if (!user || user.status === "SUSPENDED") redirect("/login?error=suspended");

  return session;
}

export async function requireAdmin() {
  const session = await getSession();
  if (!session?.user?.id) redirect("/admin/login");
  if (session.user.role !== "ADMIN") redirect("/admin/login");
  return session;
}

import { withAuth } from "next-auth/middleware";
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";

const userAppPaths = [
  "/dashboard",
  "/my-work",
  "/messages",
  "/wallet",
  "/deposit",
  "/buy-bids",
  "/report",
  "/reports",
  "/notifications",
  "/settings",
];

export default withAuth(
  function middleware(req) {
    const path = req.nextUrl.pathname;
    const role = req.nextauth.token?.role as string | undefined;

    if (path.startsWith("/admin") && !path.startsWith("/admin/login")) {
      if (role !== "ADMIN") {
        return NextResponse.redirect(new URL("/admin/login", req.url));
      }
    }

    // Admin must stay in admin area — not the freelancer app
    if (role === "ADMIN" && userAppPaths.some((p) => path === p || path.startsWith(`${p}/`))) {
      return NextResponse.redirect(new URL("/admin", req.url));
    }

    return NextResponse.next();
  },
  {
    callbacks: {
      authorized: ({ token, req }) => {
        const path = req.nextUrl.pathname;
        if (path.startsWith("/admin/login")) return true;
        if (path.startsWith("/admin")) return true;
        return !!token;
      },
    },
  }
);

export const config = {
  matcher: [
    "/dashboard/:path*",
    "/my-work/:path*",
    "/messages/:path*",
    "/wallet/:path*",
    "/deposit/:path*",
    "/buy-bids/:path*",
    "/report/:path*",
    "/reports/:path*",
    "/notifications/:path*",
    "/settings/:path*",
    "/admin/:path*",
  ],
};

import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { sendExternalAlert } from "@/lib/alerts";
import {
  matchesActivityLevel,
  parseNotificationPrefs,
  shouldCreateInAppNotification,
  shouldSendEmailAlert,
} from "@/lib/notification-prefs";
import { sendPushNotification } from "@/lib/firebase-push";

const EXTERNAL_TYPES = new Set([
  "DEPOSIT_APPROVED",
  "DEPOSIT_REJECTED",
  "BID_AWARDED",
  "BID_REJECTED",
  "SUBMISSION_APPROVED",
  "SUBMISSION_REJECTED",
  "WITHDRAWAL_PAID",
  "WITHDRAWAL_REJECTED",
  "WITHDRAWAL_REQUESTED",
  "MEMBERSHIP_RENEWED",
  "ACCOUNT_VERIFIED",
  "CHAT_MESSAGE",
  "REPORT_UPDATE",
  "ACCOUNT_SUSPENDED",
]);

export async function notifyUser(input: {
  userId: string;
  type: string;
  title: string;
  body: string;
  href?: string;
}) {
  try {
    const user = await prisma.user.findUnique({
      where: { id: input.userId },
      select: { notificationPrefs: true },
    });
    const prefs = parseNotificationPrefs(user?.notificationPrefs);

    const createInApp = shouldCreateInAppNotification(input.type, prefs);
    if (createInApp) {
      const notification = await prisma.notification.create({
        data: {
          userId: input.userId,
          type: input.type,
          title: input.title,
          body: input.body,
          href: input.href || null,
        },
      });

      if (matchesActivityLevel(input.type, prefs.mobileShow)) {
        try {
          await sendPushNotification(input.userId, {
            id: notification.id,
            type: notification.type,
            title: notification.title,
            body: notification.body,
            href: notification.href,
          });
        } catch (error: unknown) {
          const code =
            typeof error === "object" && error !== null && "code" in error
              ? String(error.code)
              : error instanceof Error
                ? error.name
                : "UnknownError";
          console.warn("[notifications] FCM push failed", {
            userId: input.userId,
            type: input.type,
            code,
          });
        }
      }
    }

    if (
      EXTERNAL_TYPES.has(input.type) &&
      shouldSendEmailAlert(input.type, prefs)
    ) {
      void sendExternalAlert({
        userId: input.userId,
        title: input.title,
        body: input.body,
        href: input.href,
      });
    }
  } catch (error) {
    const code =
      typeof error === "object" && error !== null && "code" in error
        ? String(error.code)
        : error instanceof Error
          ? error.name
          : "UnknownError";
    console.warn("[notifications] In-app notification failed", {
      userId: input.userId,
      type: input.type,
      code,
    });
  }
}

export async function notifyMany(
  userIds: string[],
  input: Omit<Parameters<typeof notifyUser>[0], "userId">,
) {
  const unique = [...new Set(userIds.filter(Boolean))];
  await Promise.all(unique.map((userId) => notifyUser({ ...input, userId })));
}


====================================================================================================
SOURCE FILE: D:\Trust Work\src\lib\notification-prefs.ts
SHA256: DB6DF9936C76732C75CA01B3EA9D394918F2C79B81FAB516BB08F444E89D672B

export const ACTIVITY_LEVELS = [
  "ALL",
  "IMPORTANT",
  "NONE",
] as const;

export const COUNTER_LEVELS = [
  "ALL",
  "MESSAGES",
  "NONE",
] as const;

export const EMAIL_FREQUENCIES = [
  "IMMEDIATE",
  "DAILY",
  "WEEKLY",
  "NEVER",
] as const;

export type ActivityLevel = (typeof ACTIVITY_LEVELS)[number];
export type CounterLevel = (typeof COUNTER_LEVELS)[number];
export type EmailFrequency = (typeof EMAIL_FREQUENCIES)[number];

export type NotificationPrefs = {
  desktopShow: ActivityLevel;
  desktopCounter: CounterLevel;
  mobileShow: ActivityLevel;
  mobileCounter: CounterLevel;
  emailShow: ActivityLevel;
  emailFrequency: EmailFrequency;
};

export const DEFAULT_NOTIFICATION_PREFS: NotificationPrefs = {
  desktopShow: "ALL",
  desktopCounter: "ALL",
  mobileShow: "ALL",
  mobileCounter: "ALL",
  emailShow: "ALL",
  emailFrequency: "IMMEDIATE",
};

export const ACTIVITY_LEVEL_LABELS: Record<ActivityLevel, string> = {
  ALL: "All activity",
  IMPORTANT: "Important activity only",
  NONE: "Nothing",
};

export const COUNTER_LEVEL_LABELS: Record<CounterLevel, string> = {
  ALL: "All activity",
  MESSAGES: "Direct messages only",
  NONE: "Nothing",
};

export const EMAIL_FREQUENCY_LABELS: Record<EmailFrequency, string> = {
  IMMEDIATE: "Immediately",
  DAILY: "Daily",
  WEEKLY: "Weekly",
  NEVER: "Never",
};

/** Types treated as “important activity” for filtering. */
export const IMPORTANT_NOTIFICATION_TYPES = new Set([
  "DEPOSIT_APPROVED",
  "DEPOSIT_REJECTED",
  "BID_AWARDED",
  "BID_REJECTED",
  "SUBMISSION_APPROVED",
  "SUBMISSION_REJECTED",
  "WITHDRAWAL_PAID",
  "WITHDRAWAL_REJECTED",
  "WITHDRAWAL_REQUESTED",
  "MEMBERSHIP_RENEWED",
  "ACCOUNT_VERIFIED",
  "REPORT_UPDATE",
  "ACCOUNT_SUSPENDED",
  "SECURITY",
]);

export function isImportantNotificationType(type: string) {
  return IMPORTANT_NOTIFICATION_TYPES.has(type);
}

export function matchesActivityLevel(type: string, level: ActivityLevel) {
  if (level === "NONE") return false;
  if (level === "ALL") return true;
  return isImportantNotificationType(type);
}

export function matchesCounterLevel(type: string, level: CounterLevel) {
  if (level === "NONE") return false;
  if (level === "MESSAGES") return type === "CHAT_MESSAGE";
  return true;
}

function isActivityLevel(value: unknown): value is ActivityLevel {
  return (
    typeof value === "string" &&
    (ACTIVITY_LEVELS as readonly string[]).includes(value)
  );
}

function isCounterLevel(value: unknown): value is CounterLevel {
  return (
    typeof value === "string" &&
    (COUNTER_LEVELS as readonly string[]).includes(value)
  );
}

function isEmailFrequency(value: unknown): value is EmailFrequency {
  return (
    typeof value === "string" &&
    (EMAIL_FREQUENCIES as readonly string[]).includes(value)
  );
}

export function parseNotificationPrefs(
  raw: string | null | undefined,
): NotificationPrefs {
  if (!raw) return { ...DEFAULT_NOTIFICATION_PREFS };
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as Partial<NotificationPrefs>;
    return {
      desktopShow: isActivityLevel(parsed.desktopShow)
        ? parsed.desktopShow
        : DEFAULT_NOTIFICATION_PREFS.desktopShow,
      desktopCounter: isCounterLevel(parsed.desktopCounter)
        ? parsed.desktopCounter
        : DEFAULT_NOTIFICATION_PREFS.desktopCounter,
      mobileShow: isActivityLevel(parsed.mobileShow)
        ? parsed.mobileShow
        : DEFAULT_NOTIFICATION_PREFS.mobileShow,
      mobileCounter: isCounterLevel(parsed.mobileCounter)
        ? parsed.mobileCounter
        : DEFAULT_NOTIFICATION_PREFS.mobileCounter,
      emailShow: isActivityLevel(parsed.emailShow)
        ? parsed.emailShow
        : DEFAULT_NOTIFICATION_PREFS.emailShow,
      emailFrequency: isEmailFrequency(parsed.emailFrequency)
        ? parsed.emailFrequency
        : DEFAULT_NOTIFICATION_PREFS.emailFrequency,
    };
  } catch {
    return { ...DEFAULT_NOTIFICATION_PREFS };
  }
}

export function shouldCreateInAppNotification(
  type: string,
  prefs: NotificationPrefs,
) {
  // Desktop or mobile “show” allows creating the in-app notification record.
  return (
    matchesActivityLevel(type, prefs.desktopShow) ||
    matchesActivityLevel(type, prefs.mobileShow)
  );
}

export function shouldCountAsUnread(
  type: string,
  prefs: NotificationPrefs,
) {
  // Web badge uses desktop counter; mobile counter is stored for mobile clients.
  return matchesCounterLevel(type, prefs.desktopCounter);
}

export function shouldSendEmailAlert(
  type: string,
  prefs: NotificationPrefs,
) {
  if (prefs.emailFrequency === "NEVER") return false;
  if (prefs.emailFrequency !== "IMMEDIATE") return false;
  return matchesActivityLevel(type, prefs.emailShow);
}

export function unreadTypeFilter(prefs: NotificationPrefs): string[] | null {
  if (prefs.desktopCounter === "NONE") return [];
  if (prefs.desktopCounter === "MESSAGES") return ["CHAT_MESSAGE"];
  return null; // all types
}

SOURCE FILE: D:\Trust Work\src\lib\firebase-push.ts
SHA256: 53F55EF58340EAFC15BD326273F8A7FD18B6F012C01B95A584682A065031DCF3

import { cert, getApps, initializeApp } from "firebase-admin/app";
import { getMessaging } from "firebase-admin/messaging";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

type PushNotification = {
  id: string;
  type: string;
  title: string;
  body: string;
  href: string | null;
};

let missingConfigurationReported = false;

function messaging() {
  const projectId = process.env.FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID?.trim();
  const clientEmail = process.env.FIREBASE_CLIENT_EMAIL?.trim();
  const privateKey = process.env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY?.replace(/\\n/g, "\n");
  if (!projectId || !clientEmail || !privateKey) {
    if (!missingConfigurationReported) {
      console.warn(
        "[notifications] FCM is not configured; push delivery is disabled.",
      );
      missingConfigurationReported = true;
    }
    return null;
  }

  const appName = "workingtrust-push";
  const app =
    getApps().find((candidate) => candidate.name === appName) ??
    initializeApp(
      {
        credential: cert({ projectId, clientEmail, privateKey }),
        projectId,
      },
      appName,
    );
  return getMessaging(app);
}

export async function sendPushNotification(
  userId: string,
  notification: PushNotification,
) {
  const devices = await prisma.fcmDeviceToken.findMany({
    where: { userId, active: true },
    select: { token: true },
  });
  if (devices.length === 0) {
    console.warn("[notifications] FCM push skipped: no active device tokens", {
      userId,
      type: notification.type,
    });
    return;
  }

  const fcm = messaging();
  if (!fcm) return;

  const tokens = devices.map((device) => device.token);
  const message = {
    notification: {
      title: notification.title,
      body: notification.body,
    },
    data: {
      id: notification.id,
      type: notification.type,
      title: notification.title,
      body: notification.body,
      ...(notification.href ? { href: notification.href } : {}),
    },
    android: {
      priority: "high" as const,
      notification: {
        channelId: "workingtrust_notifications_v2",
        tag: notification.id,
        sound: "default",
      },
    },
    apns: {
      headers: { "apns-collapse-id": notification.id },
      payload: { aps: { sound: "default" } },
    },
  };

  const invalidTokens: string[] = [];
  let successCount = 0;
  let failureCount = 0;
  for (let offset = 0; offset < tokens.length; offset += 500) {
    const chunk = tokens.slice(offset, offset + 500);
    const result = await fcm.sendEachForMulticast({ ...message, tokens: chunk });
    successCount += result.successCount;
    failureCount += result.failureCount;

    result.responses.forEach((response, index) => {
      if (response.success) return;
      const code = response.error?.code ?? "unknown";
      if (
        code === "messaging/invalid-registration-token" ||
        code === "messaging/registration-token-not-registered"
      ) {
        invalidTokens.push(chunk[index]);
        return;
      }
      console.warn("[notifications] FCM delivery failed", {
        userId,
        type: notification.type,
        code,
      });
    });
  }

  console.info("[notifications] FCM push send completed", {
    userId,
    type: notification.type,
    deviceCount: tokens.length,
    successCount,
    failureCount,
  });

  if (invalidTokens.length) {
    await prisma.fcmDeviceToken.updateMany({
      where: { userId, token: { in: invalidTokens }, active: true },
      data: { active: false },
    });
  }
}

SOURCE FILE: D:\Trust Work\src\lib\bid-refunds.ts

import type { Prisma } from "@prisma/client";

type Tx = Prisma.TransactionClient;

/** Refund connects for a declined / not-selected bid. */
export async function refundBidConnects(
  tx: Tx,
  bid: { id: string; userId: string; connectsSpent: number; task: { title: string } }
) {
  if (bid.connectsSpent <= 0) return;
  await tx.user.update({
    where: { id: bid.userId },
    data: { connects: { increment: bid.connectsSpent } },
  });
  await tx.connectLedger.create({
    data: {
      userId: bid.userId,
      delta: bid.connectsSpent,
      reason: `Connects refunded — proposal not selected: ${bid.task.title}`,
      refId: bid.id,
    },
  });
}

SOURCE FILE: D:\Trust Work\src\lib\connects.ts

/** Higher dollar projects cost more connects. Floor $5 → 2 connects. */
export function connectsForBudget(earningAmount: number): number {
  const amount = Math.max(5, earningAmount);
  if (amount <= 8) return 2;
  if (amount <= 15) return 3;
  if (amount <= 25) return 5;
  if (amount <= 40) return 8;
  return Math.max(10, Math.round(amount / 4));
}

/** Clear marketplace rule shown to users. */
export const CONNECTS_REFUND_POLICY =
  "If your proposal is not selected (declined or someone else is hired), your spent connects are refunded automatically to your balance.";

SOURCE FILE: D:\Trust Work\src\app\api\account\notifications\route.ts
SHA256: DB7DFC0E38567FD6DD90DFDA33C82A95AE3458B7D10963CCE67F959A68EA1146
====================================================================================================
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getServerSession } from "next-auth";
import { authOptions } from "@/lib/auth";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { writeAudit } from "@/lib/audit";
import { rateLimit } from "@/lib/rate-limit";
import {
  ACTIVITY_LEVELS,
  COUNTER_LEVELS,
  EMAIL_FREQUENCIES,
  parseNotificationPrefs,
  type NotificationPrefs,
} from "@/lib/notification-prefs";

function isValidPrefs(body: unknown): body is NotificationPrefs {
  if (!body || typeof body !== "object") return false;
  const b = body as Record<string, unknown>;
  return (
    (ACTIVITY_LEVELS as readonly string[]).includes(String(b.desktopShow)) &&
    (COUNTER_LEVELS as readonly string[]).includes(String(b.desktopCounter)) &&
    (ACTIVITY_LEVELS as readonly string[]).includes(String(b.mobileShow)) &&
    (COUNTER_LEVELS as readonly string[]).includes(String(b.mobileCounter)) &&
    (ACTIVITY_LEVELS as readonly string[]).includes(String(b.emailShow)) &&
    (EMAIL_FREQUENCIES as readonly string[]).includes(String(b.emailFrequency))
  );
}

export async function GET() {
  const session = await getServerSession(authOptions);
  if (!session?.user?.id) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }

  const user = await prisma.user.findUnique({
    where: { id: session.user.id },
    select: { notificationPrefs: true },
  });

  return NextResponse.json({
    prefs: parseNotificationPrefs(user?.notificationPrefs),
  });
}

export async function PATCH(req: Request) {
  const session = await getServerSession(authOptions);
  if (!session?.user?.id) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }

  const limited = rateLimit(
    `notification-prefs:${session.user.id}`,
    40,
    15 * 60 * 1000,
  );
  if (!limited.ok) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        error: `Too many updates. Try again in ${limited.retryAfterSec}s.`,
      },
      { status: 429 },
    );
  }

  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!isValidPrefs(body)) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Invalid notification preferences." },
      { status: 400 },
    );
  }

  const prefs: NotificationPrefs = {
    desktopShow: body.desktopShow,
    desktopCounter: body.desktopCounter,
    mobileShow: body.mobileShow,
    mobileCounter: body.mobileCounter,
    emailShow: body.emailShow,
    emailFrequency: body.emailFrequency,
  };

  const user = await prisma.user.update({
    where: { id: session.user.id },
    data: { notificationPrefs: JSON.stringify(prefs) },
    select: { notificationPrefs: true },
  });

  await writeAudit("NOTIFICATION_PREFS_UPDATED", session.user.id, prefs);

  return NextResponse.json({
    ok: true,
    prefs: parseNotificationPrefs(user.notificationPrefs),
  });
}

SOURCE FILE: D:\Trust Work\src\app\api\auth\[...nextauth]\route.ts
SHA256: 31A359C46DE53145F3444F9ADA81553B9A511E77947D420E034CBD117C21EA77

import NextAuth from "next-auth";
import { authOptions } from "@/lib/auth";

const handler = NextAuth(authOptions);

export { handler as GET, handler as POST };
